# KaninPHP v1 Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** Turn the broken KaninPHP v0.0.1 skeleton into a lightweight, beginner-readable, API-first PHP 8.5 framework (v1.0.0 public release) with routing, middleware, a safe query builder, models, validation, JSON error handling, a CLI, a demo `/api/users` resource, and full README documentation.

**Architecture:** A small `Kanin\` core under `system/` (PSR-4) — `Application` (DI container) boots `.env` + `config/*`, loads `routes/api.php` under `/api`, and pushes each `Request` through global middleware → `Router` → controller, converting return values to JSON responses; every error becomes JSON via `Exceptions\Handler`. App code lives in `app/` (`App\`).

**Tech Stack:** PHP 8.5 (no runtime dependencies; ext-pdo, ext-mbstring, ext-json), Composer autoloading, PHPUnit 12, SQLite (default) / MySQL / PostgreSQL via PDO.

**Spec:** `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-13-kaninphp-framework-design.md`

## Global Constraints

- PHP `^8.5`; every PHP file starts with `<?php` + `declare(strict_types=1);`.
- No runtime Composer dependencies; dev only `phpunit/phpunit` ^12.
- Namespaces: `Kanin\` → `system/`, `App\` → `app/`, `Tests\` → `tests/`.
- Lightweight API-first: no views, sessions, CSRF, ORM relationships, migrations, queues, events, cache.
- Security: all SQL values bound; identifiers/operators/directions allowlisted; no unscoped update/delete; `$fillable`/`$hidden` enforced; bearer tokens compared with `hash_equals`, empty token list denies; production 500 body is `{"message":"Server Error"}`; malformed JSON → 400; docroot is `public/`.
- License MIT. Branch `feature/kanin-v1`; never push without asking.
- Commits: conventional messages ending with `Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>`.
- Run commands from the repo root in Git Bash after `export PATH="$LOCALAPPDATA/Microsoft/WinGet/Packages/PHP.PHP.8.5_Microsoft.Winget.Source_8wekyb3d8bbwe:$PATH"`.
- Execute tasks strictly in number order (Task 9 before Task 10).

## Known deviation from spec

- Spec §1.3 targets ~300 lines per core class and ~3,000 lines in `system/`. Measured during plan verification: ~4,300 lines (mostly beginner docblocks); `Request`, `QueryBuilder`, `Kanin`, `Validator`, `Router`, `Container` are 305–370 lines. Accepted for v1 unless the reviewer asks for a split.

---

## Interface Contract (binding for every task)

Every class below lives under `system/` with namespace `Kanin\...` mapped by PSR-4 (`Kanin\Http\Request` → `system/Http/Request.php`). Every PHP file starts with `<?php` then `declare(strict_types=1);`. Signatures here are authoritative; a task MUST NOT rename, reorder or retype them. Classes not listed are task-private.

### Kanin\Container (`system/Container.php`) + `Kanin\ContainerException extends \RuntimeException`
- `bind(string $abstract, \Closure|string|null $concrete = null): void` — new instance each `make`.
- `singleton(string $abstract, \Closure|string|null $concrete = null): void` — resolved once, cached.
- `instance(string $abstract, mixed $instance): mixed` — stores and returns it.
- `has(string $abstract): bool` — bound/instance exists.
- `make(string $abstract, array $parameters = []): mixed` — closures are called as `$concrete($this, $parameters)`; class strings are autowired by reflection: each constructor param resolved by (1) name key in `$parameters`, (2) non-builtin class/interface type → `make(type)`, (3) default value, (4) nullable → null, else throw `ContainerException("Unresolvable dependency [\$name] in class [X]")`. Unknown non-class abstract → `ContainerException`.
- `call(callable|array $callback, array $parameters = []): mixed` — invokes a closure or `[object|class-string, 'method']` (class-string is `make`d first), resolving each method parameter with the same 4 rules. Named `$parameters` values are cast to `int`/`float` when the param type is `int`/`float` and the value is a numeric string.
- `static getInstance(): static` (creates one if none) and `static setInstance(?Container $container): void`.

### Kanin\Application extends Container (`system/Application.php`)
- `public const VERSION = '1.0.0';`
- `__construct(string $basePath)` — normalizes path (no trailing slash, forward slashes), calls `static::setInstance($this)`, `instance(Application::class, $this)`, `instance(Container::class, $this)`.
- `basePath(string $path = ''): string` — `basePath('config')` → `<root>/config`.
- `boot(): void` — idempotent. Order: `Env::load(basePath('.env'))`; `instance(Config::class, Config::fromDirectory(basePath('config')))`; `date_default_timezone_set(config app.timezone, default 'UTC')`; `Handler::registerErrorHandler()`; register singletons `Router::class` (`new Router($this)`), `Connection::class` (`new Connection(config "database.connections.{database.default}")`), `Handler::class` (`new Handler($this)`); register each `config('app.middleware_aliases', [])` via `Router::aliasMiddleware`; if `basePath('routes/api.php')` exists, load it inside `$router->group(['prefix' => 'api'], fn (Router $router) => require ...)` (the file sees `$router`).
- `handle(Request $request): Response` — `boot()`; `instance(Request::class, $request)`; runs `(new Pipeline($this))->send($request)->through(config('app.middleware', []))->then(fn (Request $r) => $this->make(Router::class)->dispatch($r))`. The destination catches `\Throwable` → `Handler::report` + `Handler::render` (so global middleware such as CORS still decorates error responses); an outer try/catch does the same for exceptions thrown by middleware itself.
- `run(): void` — `handle(Request::capture())->send()`.
- `isDebug(): bool` — `(bool) config('app.debug', false)`.

### Kanin\Support\Env (`system/Support/Env.php`) — static only
- `parse(string $contents): array` — `KEY=value` lines; ignores blanks and `#` comments; strips matching single/double quotes; unquoted values lose trailing ` # comment`; values kept as strings.
- `load(string $path): void` — no-op if file missing; for each pair, only if `getenv($key) === false` and `!array_key_exists($key, $_ENV)`: sets `$_ENV[$key]`, `$_SERVER[$key]`, `putenv("$key=$value")`.
- `get(string $key, mixed $default = null): mixed` — reads `$_ENV`, then `$_SERVER`, then `getenv`; casts `true/(true)`→true, `false/(false)`→false, `null/(null)`→null, `empty/(empty)`→''; missing → `$default`.

### Kanin\Support\Config (`system/Support/Config.php`)
- `__construct(array $items = [])`; `static fromDirectory(string $path): static` (each `*.php` returning an array, keyed by filename without extension).
- `get(string $key, mixed $default = null): mixed` (dot notation), `set(string $key, mixed $value): void` (dot notation, creates nested arrays), `has(string $key): bool`, `all(): array`.

### Kanin\Http
- `enum Method: string { case GET='GET'; HEAD; POST; PUT; PATCH; DELETE; OPTIONS }` (`system/Http/Method.php`) + `public function isReadOnly(): bool` (GET, HEAD, OPTIONS).
- `interface Middleware { public function handle(Request $request, \Closure $next): Response; }` (`system/Http/Middleware.php`).
- `Request` (`system/Http/Request.php`):
  - `__construct(string $method, string $uri, array $query = [], array $request = [], array $server = [], array $headers = [], ?string $content = null)` — header keys normalized to lowercase.
  - `static capture(): static` (superglobals; headers built from `HTTP_*`, `CONTENT_TYPE`, `CONTENT_LENGTH`; content from `php://input`).
  - `static create(string $uri, string $method = 'GET', array $parameters = [], array $headers = [], ?string $content = null, array $server = []): static` — query string in `$uri` parsed into query; `$parameters` go to query for GET/HEAD else to body; server defaults `HTTP_HOST=localhost`, `REQUEST_METHOD`, `REQUEST_URI`.
  - `method(): Method` — real method, except POST with override: input `_method` or header `X-HTTP-Method-Override` ∈ {PUT, PATCH, DELETE} (case-insensitive). Unknown real method → `HttpException(405, 'Method Not Allowed')`.
  - `path(): string` — url-decoded path, always leading `/`, no trailing `/` except root.
  - `input(?string $key = null, mixed $default = null): mixed` — body merged over query; `null` key → all. When `isJson()` and content non-empty, body = `json_decode($content, true, 512, JSON_THROW_ON_ERROR)`; decode failure or non-array result → `HttpException(400, 'Malformed JSON body.')` (decoded lazily, once).
  - `all(): array`, `only(string ...$keys): array` (only present keys), `except(string ...$keys): array`, `has(string $key): bool`, `query(?string $key = null, mixed $default = null): mixed`.
  - `header(string $name, ?string $default = null): ?string` (case-insensitive), `bearerToken(): ?string` (from `Authorization: Bearer <token>`, case-insensitive scheme, trimmed, empty → null), `isJson(): bool` (Content-Type contains `/json` or `+json`), `isSecure(): bool`, `host(): string` (Host header, else `SERVER_NAME`, else `localhost`).
  - `setRouteParameters(array $parameters): void`, `route(?string $key = null, mixed $default = null): mixed`.
  - `validate(array $rules, array $messages = []): array` — `Validator::make($this->all(), $rules, $messages)->validate()`.
- `Response` (`system/Http/Response.php`): `__construct(string $content = '', int $status = 200, array $headers = [])` (default `Content-Type: text/plain; charset=UTF-8` unless given); `getContent(): string`, `setContent(string): static`, `getStatusCode(): int`, `setStatusCode(int): static`, `header(string $name, string $value): static` (replaces, case-insensitive), `getHeader(string $name): ?string` (case-insensitive), `headers(): array`, `send(): void` (`http_response_code`, headers unless `headers_sent()`, echo content).
- `JsonResponse extends Response` (`system/Http/JsonResponse.php`): `__construct(mixed $data = null, int $status = 200, array $headers = [])` — `Content-Type: application/json`, `json_encode($data, JSON_THROW_ON_ERROR | JSON_UNESCAPED_SLASHES | JSON_UNESCAPED_UNICODE)`; status 204 → empty content; `getData(): mixed` (decoded as assoc array; empty content → null).
- `HttpException extends \RuntimeException` (`system/Http/HttpException.php`): `__construct(int $statusCode, string $message = '', array $headers = [], ?\Throwable $previous = null)`; `getStatusCode(): int`; `getHeaders(): array`.
- `NotFoundHttpException extends HttpException` (`system/Http/NotFoundHttpException.php`): `__construct(string $message = 'Not Found')` → 404.
- `MethodNotAllowedHttpException extends HttpException` (`system/Http/MethodNotAllowedHttpException.php`): `__construct(array $allowed)` → 405, message `Method Not Allowed`, header `Allow: GET, POST` (joined with `, `).

### Kanin\Middleware\HandleCors implements Kanin\Http\Middleware (`system/Middleware/HandleCors.php`)
- `__construct(Config $config)` reading `cors.*`: `allowed_origins` (list, `'*'` allowed), `allowed_methods` (list), `allowed_headers` (list), `exposed_headers` (list), `max_age` (int), `supports_credentials` (bool).
- No `Origin` header → `$next($request)` untouched.
- Origin allowed when list contains `'*'` or the exact origin. Allow-Origin value: `'*'` when list has `'*'` and credentials off; otherwise the request origin plus `Vary: Origin`.
- Preflight (`OPTIONS` + `Access-Control-Request-Method` header): returns `new Response('', 204)` directly (no routing) with `Access-Control-Allow-Origin`, `Access-Control-Allow-Methods` (joined `, `), `Access-Control-Allow-Headers` (joined, or the requested `Access-Control-Request-Headers` when list is `['*']`), `Access-Control-Max-Age`, and `Access-Control-Allow-Credentials: true` when enabled. Disallowed-origin preflight → `Response('', 204)` with no CORS headers.
- Actual request: response from `$next` gets `Access-Control-Allow-Origin` (+ `Vary`, credentials, `Access-Control-Expose-Headers` when non-empty) when origin allowed; untouched otherwise.

### Kanin\Middleware\AuthenticateToken implements Kanin\Http\Middleware (`system/Middleware/AuthenticateToken.php`)
- `__construct(Config $config)`; tokens = `config('auth.tokens', [])` (list of non-empty strings). Passes when `bearerToken()` is non-null and `hash_equals` matches any token (checks every token, no early exit). Otherwise `throw new HttpException(401, 'Unauthenticated.', ['WWW-Authenticate' => 'Bearer'])`. Empty token list → always 401.
- Registered under alias `auth.token` in `config/app.php`.

### Kanin\Routing
- `Route` (`system/Routing/Route.php`): `__construct(array $methods, string $uri, \Closure|array|string $action)` (methods as uppercase strings; uri normalized with leading `/`, no trailing `/` except root); `methods(): array`, `uri(): string`, `getAction(): \Closure|array|string`, `name(string $name): static`, `getName(): ?string`, `middleware(string|array $middleware): static` (appends), `getMiddleware(): array`, `where(string|array $name, ?string $pattern = null): static`, `matches(string $path): ?array` (param map, or null). Segment syntax `{id}` (default pattern `[^/]+`) and optional trailing `{id?}` (preceding `/` optional; missing → param absent). Static facade proxying to `Container::getInstance()->make(Router::class)`: `static get/post/put/patch/delete(string $uri, \Closure|array|string $action): Route`, `static match(array $methods, string $uri, $action): Route`, `static any(string $uri, $action): Route`, `static group(array $attributes, \Closure $routes): void`, `static apiResource(string $name, string $controller, array $options = []): void`.
- `Router` (`system/Routing/Router.php`): `__construct(Container $container)`; `get/post/put/patch/delete(string $uri, \Closure|array|string $action): Route`; `match(array $methods, string $uri, $action): Route`; `any(...)` (GET, POST, PUT, PATCH, DELETE); `group(array $attributes, \Closure $routes): void` (keys `prefix`, `middleware`; nests; closure receives `$this`; group middleware prepended to route middleware); `apiResource(string $name, string $controller, array $options = []): void` — registers `GET /{name}` index, `POST /{name}` store, `GET /{name}/{id}` show, `PUT|PATCH /{name}/{id}` update, `DELETE /{name}/{id}` destroy, named `{name}.index` etc., `{id}` constrained to `[0-9]+`; options `only` (list of actions) and `middleware` (applied to every registered route); `aliasMiddleware(string $alias, string $class): void`; `routes(): array`; `findRoute(Request $request): Route` (HEAD matches GET routes; path matched but no method → `MethodNotAllowedHttpException` listing allowed methods; none → `NotFoundHttpException`); `dispatch(Request $request): Response`; `url(string $name, array $parameters = []): string` (relative path, unknown name → `\InvalidArgumentException`, missing required param → `\InvalidArgumentException`, leftover params → query string); `static toResponse(mixed $result): Response`.
  - Dispatch: findRoute → `$request->setRouteParameters($params)` → `$container->instance(Request::class, $request)` → `Pipeline` through route middleware (aliases resolved; unknown alias that is not a class → `\InvalidArgumentException`) → action. Action forms: `\Closure`, `[Controller::class, 'method']`, `'Controller@method'`. Invoked via `$container->call($callable, $routeParams)`.
  - `toResponse`: `Response` → same; `array` or `\JsonSerializable` → `JsonResponse`; `string` or `\Stringable` → `new Response((string) $x)`; `null` → `new Response('', 204)`; anything else → `\UnexpectedValueException`.
- `Pipeline` (`system/Routing/Pipeline.php`): `__construct(Container $container)`; `send(Request $request): static`; `through(array $middleware): static` (class strings resolved via `make`, or objects); `then(\Closure $destination): Response`. First listed middleware runs outermost.

### Kanin\Database
- `Connection` (`system/Database/Connection.php`): `__construct(array $config)` (keys `driver` sqlite|mysql|pgsql, `database`, `host`, `port`, `username`, `password`, `charset` default utf8mb4); `getPdo(): \PDO` (lazy; `ERRMODE_EXCEPTION`, `FETCH_ASSOC`, `EMULATE_PREPARES false`; sqlite enables `PRAGMA foreign_keys = ON`); `getDriverName(): string`; `select(string $sql, array $bindings = []): array`; `selectOne(string $sql, array $bindings = []): ?array`; `insert(string $sql, array $bindings = []): bool`; `update(string $sql, array $bindings = []): int`; `delete(string $sql, array $bindings = []): int`; `statement(string $sql, array $bindings = []): bool`; `unprepared(string $sql): bool` (`PDO::exec`, used for seed files); `lastInsertId(): string`; `transaction(\Closure $callback): mixed` (commit / rollBack + rethrow; callback receives `$this`); `table(string $table): QueryBuilder`.
- `QueryBuilder` (`system/Database/QueryBuilder.php`): `__construct(Connection $connection, string $table)`; `setModel(string $modelClass): static`; `select(string ...$columns): static`; `where(string $column, mixed $operator = null, mixed $value = null): static` (`func_num_args() === 2` → operator `=`); `orWhere(...)` same; `whereIn(string $column, array $values): static` (empty → `0 = 1`); `whereNull(string $column): static`; `whereNotNull(string $column): static`; `orderBy(string $column, string $direction = 'asc'): static`; `limit(int $limit): static`; `offset(int $offset): static`; `get(): array` (rows, or model instances via `$model::newFromRow` when a model is set); `first(): array|Model|null`; `find(int|string $id, string $column = 'id'): array|Model|null`; `count(): int`; `exists(): bool`; `insert(array $values): int|string` (returns `lastInsertId`, numeric strings cast to int); `update(array $values): int`; `delete(): int`; `paginate(int $perPage = 15, int $page = 1): array` (returns `['data' => <rows/models of that page>, 'meta' => ['current_page' => int, 'per_page' => int, 'total' => int, 'last_page' => int (min 1)]]`; `$perPage` clamped to 1..100, `$page` min 1; count and page query share the same where clauses); `toSql(): string`; `getBindings(): array`.
  - Identifier rule: `/^[A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_]*(\.[A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_]*)?$/`, plus `*`, plus `<identifier> as <alias>` (case-insensitive) in `select`. Violations → `\InvalidArgumentException`. Quoting: backtick for mysql, double quote otherwise; each dotted part quoted separately; `*` unquoted.
  - Operators allowlist: `=`, `!=`, `<>`, `<`, `>`, `<=`, `>=`, `like`, `not like` (case-insensitive) else `\InvalidArgumentException`. Direction `asc|desc` (case-insensitive) else `\InvalidArgumentException`.
  - `update()` / `delete()` with no where clauses → `\LogicException`. Empty `insert`/`update` values → `\InvalidArgumentException`.
  - SQL shape (sqlite): `select * from "users" where "id" = ? and "name" like ? or "x" is null order by "id" desc limit 10 offset 5`. Limit/offset inlined as ints.
- `Model implements \JsonSerializable` (`system/Database/Model.php`, abstract): `protected string $table = ''` (default: snake_case plural of short class name: `User`→`users`, `BlogPost`→`blog_posts`, `Category`→`categories`, `Box`→`boxes`); `protected string $primaryKey = 'id'`; `protected array $fillable = []`; `protected array $hidden = []`; `protected array $attributes = []`; `public bool $exists = false`. Static: `setConnection(?Connection $c): void`, `getConnection(): Connection` (static property, else `Container::getInstance()->make(Connection::class)`), `query(): QueryBuilder`, `all(): array`, `find(int|string $id): ?static`, `findOrFail(int|string $id): static` (→ `NotFoundHttpException`), `where(string $column, mixed $operator = null, mixed $value = null): QueryBuilder` (forwards `...func_get_args()`), `create(array $attributes): static`, `newFromRow(array $row): static` (bypasses fillable, `exists = true`). Instance: `fill(array $attributes): static` (fillable keys only), `save(): bool`, `delete(): bool`, `getKey(): int|string|null`, `getTable(): string`, `toArray(): array` (minus hidden), `jsonSerialize(): array`, `__get`, `__set`, `__isset`, `__unset`.

### Kanin\Validation
- `Validator` (`system/Validation/Validator.php`): `__construct(array $data, array $rules, array $messages = [], ?Connection $connection = null)`; `static make(array $data, array $rules, array $messages = []): static`; `passes(): bool`; `fails(): bool`; `errors(): array` (`field => list<string>`); `validated(): array` (rule keys present in data); `validate(): array` (validated or throws). Rules as `'a|b:1'` or `['a', 'b:1']`. Rules: `required, nullable, string, email, numeric, integer, min, max, between, confirmed, in, same, alpha_num, url, unique:table,column[,ignoreId[,idColumn]], exists:table,column`. Absent/empty value with no `required` → other rules skipped. Size for min/max/between: numeric value when field has `numeric` or `integer` rule, array count for arrays, else `mb_strlen`. Unknown rule → `\InvalidArgumentException`. Default messages (`:attribute` = field with `_`→space): required "The :attribute field is required.", email "The :attribute must be a valid email address.", min (string) "The :attribute must be at least :min characters.", max (string) "The :attribute may not be greater than :max characters.", unique "The :attribute has already been taken.", confirmed "The :attribute confirmation does not match." (others similar). Custom messages keyed `field.rule` or `rule`. `unique`/`exists` use `$connection ?? Container::getInstance()->make(Connection::class)` via `QueryBuilder`.
- `ValidationException extends \RuntimeException` (`system/Validation/ValidationException.php`): `__construct(array $errors, string $message = 'The given data was invalid.')`; `errors(): array`; `getStatusCode(): int` (422).

### Kanin\Exceptions\Handler (`system/Exceptions/Handler.php`)
- `__construct(Application $app)`; `report(\Throwable $e): void` (skips `HttpException` with status < 500 and `ValidationException`; appends `[Y-m-d H:i:s] ClassName: message in file:line` + trace to `basePath('storage/logs/kanin.log')`, creating the dir; never throws); `render(Request $request, \Throwable $e): JsonResponse`; `static registerErrorHandler(): void` (converts PHP warnings/notices within `error_reporting()` into `\ErrorException`).
- Render (always JSON): `ValidationException` → `{"message": ..., "errors": {...}}` 422. `HttpException` → `{"message": msg}` (empty msg → standard reason phrase) with its status and headers. Other → 500 `{"message": "Server Error"}`; when `$app->isDebug()` the body is instead `{"message": <real message>, "exception": <class>, "file", "line", "trace": <list of strings from getTraceAsString() lines>}`.

### Kanin\Console\Kanin (`system/Console/Kanin.php`)
- `__construct(Application $app, $output = null)` (stream resource, default `STDOUT`); `run(array $argv): int`. Commands: `help` (default, also `--help`/`-h`), `serve [--host=127.0.0.1] [--port=8000]` (`passthru` of `PHP_BINARY -S host:port -t public public/index.php`, every arg `escapeshellarg`ed; host must match `/^[A-Za-z0-9.\-]+$/`, port 1–65535, else exit 1), `routes` (table METHOD | URI | NAME | ACTION), `make:controller Name [--api]`, `make:model Name`, `make:middleware Name`, `db:seed` (sqlite: creates the database file if missing; runs `database/seed.sql` via `unprepared`). Names must match `/^[A-Z][A-Za-z0-9]*$/` (else exit 1 with message). Generators write `app/Controllers/Name.php` / `app/Models/Name.php` / `app/Middleware/Name.php` from `system/Console/stubs/{controller,controller.api,model,middleware}.stub` replacing `{{ class }}`, refuse to overwrite (exit 1), create the directory if missing. Unknown command → exit 1.
- `kanin` script (repo root): requires `vendor/autoload.php`, `$app = new Application(__DIR__); $app->boot(); exit((new Kanin($app))->run($argv));`.

### Global helpers (`system/Support/helpers.php`, composer `autoload.files`; each wrapped in `if (! function_exists(...))`)
`app(?string $abstract = null, array $parameters = []): mixed` (null → container), `config(?string $key = null, mixed $default = null): mixed` (null → `Config`), `env(string $key, mixed $default = null): mixed` (→ `Env::get`), `response(mixed $data = null, int $status = 200, array $headers = []): JsonResponse`, `route(string $name, array $parameters = []): string`, `url(string $path = ''): string` (`rtrim(config('app.url'), '/') . '/' . ltrim($path, '/')`; `''` → base url without trailing slash), `request(?string $key = null, mixed $default = null): mixed` (null → `Request`), `db(?string $table = null): Connection|QueryBuilder`, `abort(int $code, string $message = '', array $headers = []): never` (404 → `NotFoundHttpException`), `dd(mixed ...$vars): never` (`var_dump` each, `exit(1)`).

### Config files (keys used by the framework)
- `config/app.php`: `name` (APP_NAME 'KaninPHP'), `env` (APP_ENV 'production'), `debug` (APP_DEBUG false), `url` (APP_URL 'http://localhost:8000'), `timezone` (APP_TIMEZONE 'UTC'), `middleware` (`[HandleCors::class]`), `middleware_aliases` (`['auth.token' => AuthenticateToken::class]`).
- `config/database.php`: `default` (DB_CONNECTION 'sqlite'), `connections.sqlite` (`driver`, `database` = DB_DATABASE or `dirname(__DIR__).'/database/database.sqlite'`), `connections.mysql` (DB_HOST 127.0.0.1, DB_PORT 3306, DB_DATABASE kanin, DB_USERNAME root, DB_PASSWORD '', charset utf8mb4), `connections.pgsql` (port 5432, user postgres).
- `config/cors.php`: `allowed_origins` (CORS_ALLOWED_ORIGINS comma-split, default `['*']`), `allowed_methods` (`['GET','POST','PUT','PATCH','DELETE','OPTIONS']`), `allowed_headers` (`['Content-Type','Authorization','Accept','X-Requested-With']`), `exposed_headers` (`[]`), `max_age` (600), `supports_credentials` (false).
- `config/auth.php`: `tokens` = `array_values(array_filter(array_map('trim', explode(',', (string) env('API_TOKENS', '')))))`.
- `.env.example`: APP_NAME=KaninPHP, APP_ENV=local, APP_DEBUG=true, APP_URL=http://localhost:8000, APP_TIMEZONE=UTC, DB_CONNECTION=sqlite, CORS_ALLOWED_ORIGINS=*, API_TOKENS=change-me (comment: generate one with `php -r "echo bin2hex(random_bytes(32));"`).
- `phpunit.xml` `<php>` sets (`force="true"`) `APP_ENV=testing`, `APP_DEBUG=true`, `DB_CONNECTION=sqlite`, `DB_DATABASE=:memory:`, `API_TOKENS=test-token`, `CORS_ALLOWED_ORIGINS=*`.

### Application code (demo)
- `routes/api.php`: `$router->get('/', fn () => ['name' => config('app.name'), 'version' => Application::VERSION])->name('home');` then `$router->apiResource('users', UserController::class, ['only' => ['index', 'show']]);` and `$router->group(['middleware' => 'auth.token'], fn (Router $router) => $router->apiResource('users', UserController::class, ['only' => ['store', 'update', 'destroy']]));`. Everything ends up under `/api`.
- `App\Models\User extends Model`: `$fillable = ['name', 'email']`.
- `App\Controllers\UserController`: `index(Request $request)` → `User::query()->orderBy('id')->paginate((int) $request->query('per_page', 15), (int) $request->query('page', 1))`; `show(int $id)` → `User::findOrFail($id)`; `store(Request $request)` → validate (`name` required|string|max:100, `email` required|email|max:255|unique:users,email) → `response(User::create($data), 201)`; `update(Request $request, int $id)` → findOrFail, validate (same, unique ignores `$id`), `fill($data)->save()`, return model; `destroy(int $id)` → findOrFail, delete, `response(null, 204)`.
- `database/seed.sql`: `DROP TABLE IF EXISTS users; CREATE TABLE users (id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT, name VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL, email VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL UNIQUE, created_at TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP);` plus 3 inserts (Ada Lovelace / ada@example.com, Alan Turing / alan@example.com, Grace Hopper / grace@example.com).

### Test conventions
- Unit tests `tests/Unit/<Area>/<Class>Test.php`, namespace `Tests\Unit\<Area>`, extend `PHPUnit\Framework\TestCase`, methods named `test_*` (snake case, `public function test_x(): void`). Feature tests `tests/Feature/*Test.php` extend `Tests\TestCase`.
- `Tests\TestCase` (`tests/TestCase.php`, abstract, extends PHPUnit TestCase): `protected Application $app`; `setUp()` builds `new Application(dirname(__DIR__))`, boots, replaces `Connection::class` with `new Connection(['driver' => 'sqlite', 'database' => ':memory:'])` via `instance()`, runs `file_get_contents(basePath('database/seed.sql'))` through `unprepared`, calls `Model::setConnection(null)`. Helpers: `json(string $method, string $uri, array $data = [], array $headers = []): Response` (non-GET: JSON-encodes `$data` as content; GET: data → query; always `Content-Type`/`Accept: application/json`), `getJson(string $uri, array $headers = [])`, `postJson/putJson/patchJson/deleteJson(string $uri, array $data = [], array $headers = [])`, `withToken(string $token = 'test-token'): static` (adds `Authorization: Bearer` to subsequent calls), `decode(Response $response): mixed`. `tearDown()` calls `Container::setInstance(null)` and `Model::setConnection(null)`.
- Run tests with `vendor/bin/phpunit` from the repo root (Git Bash) after `export PATH="$LOCALAPPDATA/Microsoft/WinGet/Packages/PHP.PHP.8.5_Microsoft.Winget.Source_8wekyb3d8bbwe:$PATH"`. Composer: `php "$LOCALAPPDATA/Microsoft/WinGet/Packages/PHP.PHP.8.5_Microsoft.Winget.Source_8wekyb3d8bbwe/composer.phar"`.

- [ ] **Step 6: Write the failing autoload sanity test**

Create `tests/Unit/Core/AutoloadTest.php`:

```php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace Tests\Unit\Core;

use Composer\Autoload\ClassLoader;
use PHPUnit\Framework\TestCase;

final class AutoloadTest extends TestCase
{
    public function test_framework_namespace_maps_to_the_system_directory(): void
    {
        $this->assertSame($this->rootPath('system'), $this->psr4Path('Kanin\\'));
    }

    public function test_application_namespace_maps_to_the_app_directory(): void
    {
        $this->assertSame($this->rootPath('app'), $this->psr4Path('App\\'));
    }

    public function test_tests_namespace_maps_to_the_tests_directory(): void
    {
        $this->assertSame($this->rootPath('tests'), $this->psr4Path('Tests\\'));
    }

    public function test_project_runs_on_php_8_5_or_newer(): void
    {
        $this->assertTrue(version_compare(PHP_VERSION, '8.5.0', '>='));
    }

    /**
     * Ask Composer's class loader which directory a PSR-4 prefix points to.
     */
    private function psr4Path(string $prefix): string
    {
        $paths = $this->loader()->getPrefixesPsr4()[$prefix] ?? [];
        $this->assertCount(1, $paths, "Prefix [{$prefix}] should map to exactly one directory.");

        return $this->normalize((string) realpath($paths[0]) ?: $paths[0]);
    }

    private function loader(): ClassLoader
    {
        $loaders = ClassLoader::getRegisteredLoaders();
        $this->assertNotEmpty($loaders, 'The Composer autoloader is not registered.');

        return reset($loaders);
    }

    private function rootPath(string $directory): string
    {
        $path = dirname(__DIR__, 3) . '/' . $directory;

        return $this->normalize((string) realpath($path) ?: $path);
    }

    private function normalize(string $path): string
    {
        return rtrim(str_replace('\\', '/', $path), '/');
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 11: Lock down the project root and add the front-controller rewrite**

Create the root `.htaccess` (security: the docroot must be `public/`; anything served from the root is refused):

```apache
# The web root of a KaninPHP project is the public/ folder.
# If the server points at the project root by mistake, refuse everything
# so .env, config/ and the database are never downloadable.
<IfModule mod_authz_core.c>
    Require all denied
</IfModule>
<IfModule !mod_authz_core.c>
    Order deny,allow
    Deny from all
</IfModule>
```

Create `public/.htaccess`:

```apache
<IfModule mod_rewrite.c>
    <IfModule mod_negotiation.c>
        Options -MultiViews -Indexes
    </IfModule>

    RewriteEngine On

    # Pass the Authorization header to PHP (needed for bearer tokens).
    RewriteCond %{HTTP:Authorization} .
    RewriteRule .* - [E=HTTP_AUTHORIZATION:%{HTTP:Authorization}]

    # Serve real files (images, css, ...) directly.
    RewriteCond %{REQUEST_FILENAME} !-d
    RewriteCond %{REQUEST_FILENAME} !-f
    # Everything else goes to the front controller.
    RewriteRule ^ index.php [L]
</IfModule>
```

```bash
vendor/bin/phpunit
git check-ignore -q .env && echo ".env ignored"
git check-ignore -q storage/logs/.gitkeep || echo ".gitkeep tracked"
```

Expected: `OK (4 tests, 10 assertions)`, then `.env ignored` and `.gitkeep tracked`.

### Task 2: Container

**Files:**
- Create: `system/ContainerException.php`
- Create: `system/Container.php`
- Test: `tests/Unit/Core/ContainerTest.php`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Composer autoloading from Task 1 (`Kanin\` → `system/`, `Tests\` → `tests/`).
- Produces (exactly as the contract states; later tasks rely on these):
  - `Kanin\ContainerException extends \RuntimeException`
  - `Kanin\Container`:
    - `public function bind(string $abstract, \Closure|string|null $concrete = null): void`
    - `public function singleton(string $abstract, \Closure|string|null $concrete = null): void`
    - `public function instance(string $abstract, mixed $instance): mixed`
    - `public function has(string $abstract): bool`
    - `public function make(string $abstract, array $parameters = []): mixed`
    - `public function call(callable|array $callback, array $parameters = []): mixed`
    - `public static function getInstance(): static`
    - `public static function setInstance(?Container $container): void`
  - Behaviour later tasks may rely on:
    - Closures are called as `$concrete($this, $parameters)`. A string concrete different from the abstract is `make()`d (so `bind(Interface::class, Impl::class)` works).
    - `instance()` always wins over a binding with the same name; `bind()`/`singleton()` forget any cached object for that name.
    - Parameter resolution order (constructors and `call()`): (1) `$parameters[<name>]`, (2) a class/interface type the container can build (it is bound, has an instance, or is an instantiable class), (3) default value, (4) `null` when the declared type is nullable. Untyped parameters without a default are NOT treated as nullable. Otherwise `ContainerException('Unresolvable dependency [$<name>] in class [<Class>]')` where `<Class>` is the class being built, the method's class for `call([obj|class, 'method'])`, or `Closure` for closures.
    - Named values are cast for `int` (only when the string is a valid integer, e.g. `"42"`) and `float` (any numeric string) parameters; other types receive the value untouched. Variadic parameters are never filled.
    - Unknown abstract that is not an instantiable class (unbound interface, abstract class, plain string like `'mailer'`): `ContainerException('Target [<abstract>] cannot be built: it is not bound and is not an instantiable class.')`.
    - Circular constructor dependencies: `ContainerException('Circular dependency detected: A -> B -> A.')`.
    - `call()` accepts a `\Closure`, an invokable object, a function name, `'Class::method'`, or `[object|class-string, 'method']` (class strings are `make()`d first). Missing method → `ContainerException('Method [Class::method] does not exist.')`; non-public method → `ContainerException('Method [Class::method] is not public.')`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing test**

Create `tests/Unit/Core/ContainerTest.php` (the small fixture classes at the top live in the same file so the test is self-contained):

```php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace Tests\Unit\Core;

use Kanin\Container;
use Kanin\ContainerException;
use PHPUnit\Framework\TestCase;

interface ContainerTestLogger
{
}

final class ContainerTestFileLogger implements ContainerTestLogger
{
}

final class ContainerTestEngine
{
}

final class ContainerTestMailer
{
    public function __construct(public ContainerTestLogger $logger)
    {
    }
}

final class ContainerTestCar
{
    public function __construct(
        public ContainerTestEngine $engine,
        public string $color = 'red',
        public ?ContainerTestLogger $logger = null,
    ) {
    }
}

final class ContainerTestNeedsName
{
    public function __construct(public string $name)
    {
    }
}

final class ContainerTestNeedsUntyped
{
    public function __construct(public $value)
    {
    }
}

final class ContainerTestNeedsLogger
{
    public function __construct(public ContainerTestLogger $logger)
    {
    }
}

abstract class ContainerTestAbstract
{
}

final class ContainerTestCircularA
{
    public function __construct(public ContainerTestCircularB $b)
    {
    }
}

final class ContainerTestCircularB
{
    public function __construct(public ContainerTestCircularA $a)
    {
    }
}

final class ContainerTestController
{
    public function __construct(public ContainerTestEngine $engine)
    {
    }

    public function show(int $id, ContainerTestEngine $engine): array
    {
        return ['id' => $id, 'engine' => $engine];
    }

    public function price(float $amount): float
    {
        return $amount;
    }

    public function slug(string $slug): string
    {
        return $slug;
    }

    public function page(int $page = 1): int
    {
        return $page;
    }

    public static function version(): string
    {
        return 'v1';
    }

    private function secret(): string
    {
        return 'hidden';
    }
}

final class ContainerTestInvokable
{
    public function __invoke(ContainerTestEngine $engine): string
    {
        return $engine::class;
    }
}

final class ContainerTest extends TestCase
{
    protected function tearDown(): void
    {
        Container::setInstance(null);
    }

    public function test_bind_returns_a_new_instance_on_every_make(): void
    {
        $container = new Container();
        $container->bind(ContainerTestEngine::class);

        $first = $container->make(ContainerTestEngine::class);
        $second = $container->make(ContainerTestEngine::class);

        $this->assertInstanceOf(ContainerTestEngine::class, $first);
        $this->assertNotSame($first, $second);
    }

    public function test_bind_closure_receives_the_container_and_parameters(): void
    {
        $container = new Container();
        $container->bind('greeting', fn (Container $c, array $parameters) => [$c, $parameters]);

        [$received, $parameters] = $container->make('greeting', ['name' => 'Ada']);

        $this->assertSame($container, $received);
        $this->assertSame(['name' => 'Ada'], $parameters);
    }

    public function test_bind_interface_to_a_class_name(): void
    {
        $container = new Container();
        $container->bind(ContainerTestLogger::class, ContainerTestFileLogger::class);

        $mailer = $container->make(ContainerTestMailer::class);

        $this->assertInstanceOf(ContainerTestFileLogger::class, $mailer->logger);
    }

    public function test_singleton_is_built_once_and_cached(): void
    {
        $container = new Container();
        $container->singleton(ContainerTestEngine::class);

        $this->assertSame(
            $container->make(ContainerTestEngine::class),
            $container->make(ContainerTestEngine::class),
        );
    }

    public function test_singleton_closure_runs_only_once(): void
    {
        $container = new Container();
        $calls = 0;
        $container->singleton('counter', function () use (&$calls): object {
            $calls++;

            return new \stdClass();
        });

        $container->make('counter');
        $container->make('counter');

        $this->assertSame(1, $calls);
    }

    public function test_rebinding_forgets_the_cached_singleton(): void
    {
        $container = new Container();
        $container->singleton('value', fn () => 'old');
        $container->make('value');

        $container->singleton('value', fn () => 'new');

        $this->assertSame('new', $container->make('value'));
    }

    public function test_instance_stores_and_returns_the_value(): void
    {
        $container = new Container();
        $engine = new ContainerTestEngine();

        $this->assertSame($engine, $container->instance(ContainerTestEngine::class, $engine));
        $this->assertSame($engine, $container->make(ContainerTestEngine::class));
    }

    public function test_instance_wins_over_an_existing_binding(): void
    {
        $container = new Container();
        $container->bind('name', fn () => 'from binding');
        $container->instance('name', 'from instance');

        $this->assertSame('from instance', $container->make('name'));
    }

    public function test_has_reports_bindings_and_instances(): void
    {
        $container = new Container();
        $container->bind('bound');
        $container->singleton('shared', fn () => 1);
        $container->instance('stored', null);

        $this->assertTrue($container->has('bound'));
        $this->assertTrue($container->has('shared'));
        $this->assertTrue($container->has('stored'));
        $this->assertFalse($container->has('missing'));
        $this->assertFalse($container->has(ContainerTestEngine::class));
    }

    public function test_make_autowires_a_class_that_was_never_bound(): void
    {
        $car = (new Container())->make(ContainerTestCar::class);

        $this->assertInstanceOf(ContainerTestCar::class, $car);
        $this->assertInstanceOf(ContainerTestEngine::class, $car->engine);
    }

    public function test_make_uses_default_values_and_null_for_nullable_types(): void
    {
        $car = (new Container())->make(ContainerTestCar::class);

        $this->assertSame('red', $car->color);
        $this->assertNull($car->logger);
    }

    public function test_make_uses_bindings_for_nested_dependencies(): void
    {
        $container = new Container();
        $container->singleton(ContainerTestLogger::class, ContainerTestFileLogger::class);

        $car = $container->make(ContainerTestCar::class);

        $this->assertSame($container->make(ContainerTestLogger::class), $car->logger);
    }

    public function test_make_prefers_parameters_passed_by_name(): void
    {
        $engine = new ContainerTestEngine();

        $car = (new Container())->make(ContainerTestCar::class, ['engine' => $engine, 'color' => 'blue']);

        $this->assertSame($engine, $car->engine);
        $this->assertSame('blue', $car->color);
    }

    public function test_make_passes_parameters_through_a_class_binding(): void
    {
        $container = new Container();
        $container->bind('named', ContainerTestNeedsName::class);

        $this->assertSame('Ada', $container->make('named', ['name' => 'Ada'])->name);
    }

    public function test_make_throws_for_an_unresolvable_builtin_parameter(): void
    {
        $this->expectException(ContainerException::class);
        $this->expectExceptionMessage('Unresolvable dependency [$name] in class [' . ContainerTestNeedsName::class . ']');

        (new Container())->make(ContainerTestNeedsName::class);
    }

    public function test_make_throws_for_an_untyped_parameter_without_default(): void
    {
        $this->expectException(ContainerException::class);
        $this->expectExceptionMessage('Unresolvable dependency [$value] in class [' . ContainerTestNeedsUntyped::class . ']');

        (new Container())->make(ContainerTestNeedsUntyped::class);
    }

    public function test_make_throws_for_an_unbound_interface_parameter(): void
    {
        $this->expectException(ContainerException::class);
        $this->expectExceptionMessage('Unresolvable dependency [$logger] in class [' . ContainerTestNeedsLogger::class . ']');

        (new Container())->make(ContainerTestNeedsLogger::class);
    }

    public function test_make_throws_for_an_unknown_non_class_abstract(): void
    {
        $this->expectException(ContainerException::class);
        $this->expectExceptionMessage('Target [mailer] cannot be built: it is not bound and is not an instantiable class.');

        (new Container())->make('mailer');
    }

    public function test_make_throws_for_an_unbound_interface(): void
    {
        $this->expectException(ContainerException::class);
        $this->expectExceptionMessage('Target [' . ContainerTestLogger::class . '] cannot be built');

        (new Container())->make(ContainerTestLogger::class);
    }

    public function test_make_throws_for_an_abstract_class(): void
    {
        $this->expectException(ContainerException::class);
        $this->expectExceptionMessage('Target [' . ContainerTestAbstract::class . '] cannot be built');

        (new Container())->make(ContainerTestAbstract::class);
    }

    public function test_make_detects_circular_dependencies(): void
    {
        $this->expectException(ContainerException::class);
        $this->expectExceptionMessage(sprintf(
            'Circular dependency detected: %s -> %s -> %s.',
            ContainerTestCircularA::class,
            ContainerTestCircularB::class,
            ContainerTestCircularA::class,
        ));

        (new Container())->make(ContainerTestCircularA::class);
    }

    public function test_call_invokes_a_closure_with_resolved_parameters(): void
    {
        $result = (new Container())->call(
            fn (ContainerTestEngine $engine, string $name, int $count = 3) => [$engine, $name, $count],
            ['name' => 'Ada'],
        );

        $this->assertInstanceOf(ContainerTestEngine::class, $result[0]);
        $this->assertSame('Ada', $result[1]);
        $this->assertSame(3, $result[2]);
    }

    public function test_call_makes_a_class_string_before_calling_the_method(): void
    {
        $result = (new Container())->call([ContainerTestController::class, 'show'], ['id' => '42']);

        $this->assertSame(42, $result['id']);
        $this->assertInstanceOf(ContainerTestEngine::class, $result['engine']);
    }

    public function test_call_uses_an_existing_object(): void
    {
        $controller = new ContainerTestController(new ContainerTestEngine());

        $this->assertSame('hello', (new Container())->call([$controller, 'slug'], ['slug' => 'hello']));
    }

    public function test_call_casts_numeric_strings_for_int_and_float_parameters(): void
    {
        $container = new Container();

        $this->assertSame(7, $container->call([ContainerTestController::class, 'page'], ['page' => '7']));
        $this->assertSame(9.5, $container->call([ContainerTestController::class, 'price'], ['amount' => '9.5']));
        $this->assertSame(10.0, $container->call([ContainerTestController::class, 'price'], ['amount' => '10']));
    }

    public function test_call_does_not_cast_values_for_string_parameters(): void
    {
        $this->assertSame('123', (new Container())->call([ContainerTestController::class, 'slug'], ['slug' => '123']));
    }

    public function test_call_uses_the_default_when_no_parameter_is_given(): void
    {
        $this->assertSame(1, (new Container())->call([ContainerTestController::class, 'page']));
    }

    public function test_call_supports_static_methods(): void
    {
        $this->assertSame('v1', (new Container())->call([ContainerTestController::class, 'version']));
    }

    public function test_call_supports_invokable_objects(): void
    {
        $this->assertSame(ContainerTestEngine::class, (new Container())->call(new ContainerTestInvokable()));
    }

    public function test_call_throws_for_a_missing_method(): void
    {
        $this->expectException(ContainerException::class);
        $this->expectExceptionMessage('Method [' . ContainerTestController::class . '::missing] does not exist.');

        (new Container())->call([ContainerTestController::class, 'missing']);
    }

    public function test_call_refuses_private_methods(): void
    {
        $this->expectException(ContainerException::class);
        $this->expectExceptionMessage('Method [' . ContainerTestController::class . '::secret] is not public.');

        (new Container())->call([ContainerTestController::class, 'secret']);
    }

    public function test_call_throws_for_an_unresolvable_method_parameter(): void
    {
        $this->expectException(ContainerException::class);
        $this->expectExceptionMessage('Unresolvable dependency [$id] in class [' . ContainerTestController::class . ']');

        (new Container())->call([ContainerTestController::class, 'show']);
    }

    public function test_call_throws_for_an_unresolvable_closure_parameter(): void
    {
        $this->expectException(ContainerException::class);
        $this->expectExceptionMessage('Unresolvable dependency [$name] in class [Closure]');

        (new Container())->call(fn (string $name) => $name);
    }

    public function test_get_instance_creates_a_container_when_none_was_set(): void
    {
        $container = Container::getInstance();

        $this->assertInstanceOf(Container::class, $container);
        $this->assertSame($container, Container::getInstance());
    }

    public function test_set_instance_replaces_and_clears_the_global_container(): void
    {
        $container = new Container();
        Container::setInstance($container);
        $this->assertSame($container, Container::getInstance());

        Container::setInstance(null);
        $this->assertNotSame($container, Container::getInstance());
    }

    public function test_container_exception_is_a_runtime_exception(): void
    {
        $this->assertInstanceOf(\RuntimeException::class, new ContainerException('x'));
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Create the exception class**

Create `system/ContainerException.php`:

```php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace Kanin;

/**
 * Thrown when the container cannot build or call something.
 *
 * Example: asking for an interface that was never bound, or a class whose
 * constructor needs a value the container cannot guess (like a plain string).
 */
class ContainerException extends \RuntimeException
{
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Implement the container**

Create `system/Container.php`:

```php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace Kanin;

use Closure;
use ReflectionClass;
use ReflectionFunction;
use ReflectionFunctionAbstract;
use ReflectionMethod;
use ReflectionNamedType;
use ReflectionParameter;

/**
 * A small dependency injection container.
 *
 * The container is a "factory with a memory": you tell it how to build things
 * (bind / singleton / instance) and later ask for them with make(). When you
 * ask for a class it does not know, it reads the constructor with reflection
 * and builds every dependency for you ("autowiring").
 */
class Container
{
    /** The globally shared container (see getInstance()). */
    protected static ?Container $instance = null;

    /** @var array<string, array{concrete: Closure|string, shared: bool}> */
    protected array $bindings = [];

    /** @var array<string, mixed> Objects that were already built and are reused. */
    protected array $instances = [];

    /** @var list<string> Classes currently being built, used to detect loops. */
    protected array $buildStack = [];

    /**
     * Get the global container, creating an empty one the first time.
     */
    public static function getInstance(): static
    {
        return static::$instance ??= new static();
    }

    /**
     * Replace the global container (pass null to forget it, e.g. in tests).
     */
    public static function setInstance(?Container $container): void
    {
        static::$instance = $container;
    }

    /**
     * Register a binding that builds a NEW object every time you make() it.
     *
     * $concrete may be a closure `fn (Container $c, array $params) => ...`,
     * a class name, or null (meaning "build $abstract itself").
     */
    public function bind(string $abstract, Closure|string|null $concrete = null): void
    {
        $this->register($abstract, $concrete, false);
    }

    /**
     * Register a binding that is built ONCE and then reused forever.
     */
    public function singleton(string $abstract, Closure|string|null $concrete = null): void
    {
        $this->register($abstract, $concrete, true);
    }

    /**
     * Store an already-built value under a name and return it.
     */
    public function instance(string $abstract, mixed $instance): mixed
    {
        $this->instances[$abstract] = $instance;

        return $instance;
    }

    /**
     * Check whether something was registered under this name.
     */
    public function has(string $abstract): bool
    {
        return isset($this->bindings[$abstract]) || array_key_exists($abstract, $this->instances);
    }

    /**
     * Build (or fetch) whatever is registered under $abstract.
     *
     * $parameters lets you pass values by constructor parameter name,
     * for example make(Mailer::class, ['from' => 'me@example.com']).
     */
    public function make(string $abstract, array $parameters = []): mixed
    {
        if (array_key_exists($abstract, $this->instances)) {
            return $this->instances[$abstract];
        }

        $binding = $this->bindings[$abstract] ?? null;

        if ($binding === null) {
            return $this->build($abstract, $parameters);
        }

        $object = $this->resolveBinding($abstract, $binding['concrete'], $parameters);

        if ($binding['shared']) {
            $this->instances[$abstract] = $object;
        }

        return $object;
    }

    /**
     * Call a closure or a [object|class-string, 'method'] pair, filling in
     * its parameters from $parameters (by name) or from the container.
     *
     * Numeric strings are turned into int/float when the parameter asks for
     * that type, so a route value "42" can reach `int $id`.
     */
    public function call(callable|array $callback, array $parameters = []): mixed
    {
        if (is_array($callback)) {
            return $this->callMethod($callback, $parameters);
        }

        if (is_object($callback) && ! $callback instanceof Closure) {
            return $this->callMethod([$callback, '__invoke'], $parameters);
        }

        if (is_string($callback) && str_contains($callback, '::')) {
            return $this->callMethod(explode('::', $callback, 2), $parameters);
        }

        $function = new ReflectionFunction(Closure::fromCallable($callback));
        $context = $callback instanceof Closure ? 'Closure' : (string) $callback;

        return $function->invokeArgs($this->resolveParameters($function, $parameters, $context));
    }

    /**
     * Save a binding, forgetting any object cached for the same name.
     */
    protected function register(string $abstract, Closure|string|null $concrete, bool $shared): void
    {
        unset($this->instances[$abstract]);

        $this->bindings[$abstract] = ['concrete' => $concrete ?? $abstract, 'shared' => $shared];
    }

    /**
     * Turn a binding's "recipe" into a value.
     */
    protected function resolveBinding(string $abstract, Closure|string $concrete, array $parameters): mixed
    {
        if ($concrete instanceof Closure) {
            return $concrete($this, $parameters);
        }

        return $concrete === $abstract
            ? $this->build($concrete, $parameters)
            : $this->make($concrete, $parameters);
    }

    /**
     * Create a class by reading its constructor and resolving each argument.
     */
    protected function build(string $class, array $parameters): object
    {
        if (! $this->isInstantiable($class)) {
            throw new ContainerException(
                "Target [{$class}] cannot be built: it is not bound and is not an instantiable class."
            );
        }

        if (in_array($class, $this->buildStack, true)) {
            $chain = implode(' -> ', [...$this->buildStack, $class]);

            throw new ContainerException("Circular dependency detected: {$chain}.");
        }

        $this->buildStack[] = $class;

        try {
            $reflector = new ReflectionClass($class);
            $constructor = $reflector->getConstructor();

            if ($constructor === null) {
                return $reflector->newInstance();
            }

            return $reflector->newInstanceArgs($this->resolveParameters($constructor, $parameters, $class));
        } finally {
            array_pop($this->buildStack);
        }
    }

    /**
     * Invoke an object (or class-string) method with resolved parameters.
     */
    protected function callMethod(array $callback, array $parameters): mixed
    {
        $callback = array_values($callback);

        if (count($callback) !== 2 || ! is_string($callback[1])) {
            throw new ContainerException('A method callback must look like [object|class-string, \'method\'].');
        }

        [$target, $method] = $callback;
        $object = is_string($target) ? $this->make($target) : $target;
        $class = is_object($object) ? $object::class : get_debug_type($target);

        if (! is_object($object) || ! method_exists($object, $method)) {
            throw new ContainerException("Method [{$class}::{$method}] does not exist.");
        }

        $reflector = new ReflectionMethod($object, $method);

        if (! $reflector->isPublic()) {
            throw new ContainerException("Method [{$class}::{$method}] is not public.");
        }

        $arguments = $this->resolveParameters($reflector, $parameters, $class);

        return $reflector->invokeArgs($reflector->isStatic() ? null : $object, $arguments);
    }

    /**
     * Resolve every parameter of a function, constructor or method, in order.
     *
     * @return list<mixed>
     */
    protected function resolveParameters(ReflectionFunctionAbstract $function, array $parameters, string $context): array
    {
        $arguments = [];

        foreach ($function->getParameters() as $parameter) {
            if ($parameter->isVariadic()) {
                break;
            }

            $arguments[] = $this->resolveParameter($parameter, $parameters, $context);
        }

        return $arguments;
    }

    /**
     * The 4 rules, tried in order: (1) a value passed by name, (2) a class or
     * interface type the container can build, (3) the default value,
     * (4) null for nullable types. Anything else is an error.
     */
    protected function resolveParameter(ReflectionParameter $parameter, array $parameters, string $context): mixed
    {
        $name = $parameter->getName();
        $type = $parameter->getType();
        $typeName = $type instanceof ReflectionNamedType ? $type->getName() : null;
        $isBuiltin = $type instanceof ReflectionNamedType && $type->isBuiltin();

        if (array_key_exists($name, $parameters)) {
            return $this->castNumeric($parameters[$name], $isBuiltin ? $typeName : null);
        }

        if ($typeName !== null && ! $isBuiltin && ($this->has($typeName) || $this->isInstantiable($typeName))) {
            return $this->make($typeName);
        }

        if ($parameter->isDefaultValueAvailable()) {
            return $parameter->getDefaultValue();
        }

        if ($type !== null && $type->allowsNull()) {
            return null;
        }

        throw new ContainerException("Unresolvable dependency [\${$name}] in class [{$context}]");
    }

    /**
     * Turn "42" into 42 for `int` parameters and "9.5" into 9.5 for `float`.
     */
    protected function castNumeric(mixed $value, ?string $type): mixed
    {
        if (! is_string($value) || ! is_numeric($value)) {
            return $value;
        }

        if ($type === 'int' && filter_var($value, FILTER_VALIDATE_INT) !== false) {
            return (int) $value;
        }

        return $type === 'float' ? (float) $value : $value;
    }

    /**
     * True when $class is a concrete class we can call `new` on.
     */
    protected function isInstantiable(string $class): bool
    {
        return class_exists($class) && (new ReflectionClass($class))->isInstantiable();
    }
}
```

### Task 3: Env and Config

**Files:**
- Create: `system/Support/Env.php`
- Create: `system/Support/Config.php`
- Test: `tests/Unit/Support/EnvTest.php`
- Test: `tests/Unit/Support/ConfigTest.php`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Composer autoloading from Task 1. (No runtime dependency on the Container.)
- Produces (exactly as the contract states):
  - `Kanin\Support\Env` (final, static only):
    - `public static function parse(string $contents): array` — returns `array<string, string>`.
    - `public static function load(string $path): void`
    - `public static function get(string $key, mixed $default = null): mixed`
  - `Kanin\Support\Config`:
    - `public function __construct(array $items = [])`
    - `public static function fromDirectory(string $path): static`
    - `public function get(string $key, mixed $default = null): mixed`
    - `public function set(string $key, mixed $value): void`
    - `public function has(string $key): bool`
    - `public function all(): array`
  - Behaviour later tasks may rely on:
    - `Env::parse` handles `\n`, `\r\n` and `\r`; trims spaces around the key and the value; skips blank lines, lines starting with `#`, lines without `=`, and keys not matching `/^[A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_.]*$/`; keeps any further `=` in the value; strips matching surrounding `"` or `'` (a `#` inside quotes is kept; a ` # comment` after the closing quote is dropped); removes a whitespace-preceded ` # comment` from unquoted values (`a#b` is kept); a later duplicate key wins. No escape processing.
    - `Env::load` never overwrites a key that already exists in `getenv()` or `$_ENV` (so `phpunit.xml`'s forced values and real server variables always win). Missing or unreadable file → silent no-op.
    - `Env::get` casts only string values; casting is case-insensitive (`TRUE` → `true`). An explicit `null`/`(null)` returns `null`, not `$default`.
    - `Config::get` returns a stored `null` (not the default); walking into a non-array returns the default. `has()` is `true` for keys whose value is `null`.
    - `Config::set` overwrites scalars that are in the way with arrays.
    - `Config::fromDirectory` loads `*.php` files in alphabetical order, each in an isolated scope; a missing directory yields an empty `Config`; a file that does not return an array → `\UnexpectedValueException('Config file [<name>.php] must return an array.')`. Config files may call `env()` (defined in Task 11); that works because Composer's `autoload.files` loads `helpers.php` as soon as `vendor/autoload.php` is required.

```php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace Tests\Unit\Support;

use Kanin\Support\Env;
use PHPUnit\Framework\TestCase;

final class EnvTest extends TestCase
{
    /** @var list<string> Environment keys this test touched, cleaned in tearDown(). */
    private array $keys = [];

    /** @var list<string> Temporary files to delete in tearDown(). */
    private array $files = [];

    protected function tearDown(): void
    {
        foreach ($this->keys as $key) {
            unset($_ENV[$key], $_SERVER[$key]);
            putenv($key);
        }

        foreach ($this->files as $file) {
            @unlink($file);
        }
    }

    public function test_parse_reads_key_value_pairs_as_strings(): void
    {
        $this->assertSame(
            ['APP_NAME' => 'KaninPHP', 'APP_DEBUG' => 'true', 'PORT' => '8000'],
            Env::parse("APP_NAME=KaninPHP\nAPP_DEBUG=true\nPORT=8000"),
        );
    }

    public function test_parse_ignores_blank_lines_and_comment_lines(): void
    {
        $contents = "# database settings\n\n   \nDB_CONNECTION=sqlite\n  # indented comment\n";

        $this->assertSame(['DB_CONNECTION' => 'sqlite'], Env::parse($contents));
    }

    public function test_parse_handles_windows_line_endings_and_spaces_around_equals(): void
    {
        $this->assertSame(['A' => '1', 'B' => '2'], Env::parse("A = 1\r\nB=2\r\n"));
    }

    public function test_parse_strips_matching_double_and_single_quotes(): void
    {
        $values = Env::parse("NAME=\"My API\"\nGREETING='hello world'\nEMPTY=\"\"");

        $this->assertSame(['NAME' => 'My API', 'GREETING' => 'hello world', 'EMPTY' => ''], $values);
    }

    public function test_parse_keeps_hash_signs_inside_quotes(): void
    {
        $this->assertSame(['COLOR' => '#ff0000'], Env::parse('COLOR="#ff0000" # brand colour'));
    }

    public function test_parse_keeps_mismatched_quotes_as_they_are(): void
    {
        $this->assertSame(['ODD' => '"half'], Env::parse('ODD="half'));
    }

    public function test_parse_removes_trailing_comments_from_unquoted_values(): void
    {
        $this->assertSame(
            ['DEBUG' => 'true', 'URL' => 'http://localhost#anchor'],
            Env::parse("DEBUG=true # turn off in production\nURL=http://localhost#anchor"),
        );
    }

    public function test_parse_keeps_equals_signs_inside_values(): void
    {
        $this->assertSame(['TOKEN' => 'abc=def=='], Env::parse('TOKEN=abc=def=='));
    }

    public function test_parse_skips_lines_without_equals_or_with_invalid_keys(): void
    {
        $this->assertSame(['GOOD' => 'yes'], Env::parse("just text\n1BAD=no\nBAD KEY=no\nGOOD=yes"));
    }

    public function test_parse_lets_later_lines_override_earlier_ones(): void
    {
        $this->assertSame(['A' => '2'], Env::parse("A=1\nA=2"));
    }

    public function test_load_sets_env_server_and_getenv(): void
    {
        $this->track('KANIN_TEST_LOAD');

        Env::load($this->envFile("KANIN_TEST_LOAD=loaded\n"));

        $this->assertSame('loaded', $_ENV['KANIN_TEST_LOAD']);
        $this->assertSame('loaded', $_SERVER['KANIN_TEST_LOAD']);
        $this->assertSame('loaded', getenv('KANIN_TEST_LOAD'));
    }

    public function test_load_does_not_override_a_real_environment_variable(): void
    {
        $this->track('KANIN_TEST_REAL');
        putenv('KANIN_TEST_REAL=from-shell');

        Env::load($this->envFile("KANIN_TEST_REAL=from-file\n"));

        $this->assertSame('from-shell', getenv('KANIN_TEST_REAL'));
        $this->assertArrayNotHasKey('KANIN_TEST_REAL', $_ENV);
    }

    public function test_load_does_not_override_an_existing_env_array_value(): void
    {
        $this->track('KANIN_TEST_ARRAY');
        $_ENV['KANIN_TEST_ARRAY'] = 'from-env-array';

        Env::load($this->envFile("KANIN_TEST_ARRAY=from-file\n"));

        $this->assertSame('from-env-array', $_ENV['KANIN_TEST_ARRAY']);
        $this->assertFalse(getenv('KANIN_TEST_ARRAY'));
    }

    public function test_load_does_nothing_when_the_file_is_missing(): void
    {
        $this->track('KANIN_TEST_MISSING');

        Env::load(sys_get_temp_dir() . '/kanin-does-not-exist-' . bin2hex(random_bytes(4)) . '.env');

        $this->assertNull(Env::get('KANIN_TEST_MISSING'));

    public function test_get_returns_the_default_when_the_key_is_missing(): void
    {
        $this->assertSame('fallback', Env::get('KANIN_TEST_NOT_SET', 'fallback'));
        $this->assertNull(Env::get('KANIN_TEST_NOT_SET'));

    public function test_get_reads_env_then_server_then_getenv(): void
    {
        $this->track('KANIN_TEST_ORDER');
        putenv('KANIN_TEST_ORDER=from-getenv');
        $this->assertSame('from-getenv', Env::get('KANIN_TEST_ORDER'));

        $_SERVER['KANIN_TEST_ORDER'] = 'from-server';
        $this->assertSame('from-server', Env::get('KANIN_TEST_ORDER'));

        $_ENV['KANIN_TEST_ORDER'] = 'from-env';
        $this->assertSame('from-env', Env::get('KANIN_TEST_ORDER'));
    }

    public function test_get_casts_special_words(): void
    {
        $cases = [
            'true' => true, '(true)' => true, 'TRUE' => true,
            'false' => false, '(false)' => false,
            'null' => null, '(null)' => null,
            'empty' => '', '(empty)' => '',
            'hello' => 'hello', '0' => '0',
        ];

        foreach ($cases as $raw => $expected) {
            $this->track('KANIN_TEST_CAST');
            $_ENV['KANIN_TEST_CAST'] = (string) $raw;

            $this->assertSame($expected, Env::get('KANIN_TEST_CAST', 'default'), "Casting [{$raw}]");
        }
    }

    public function test_get_returns_null_not_the_default_for_an_explicit_null(): void
    {
        $this->track('KANIN_TEST_NULL');
        $_ENV['KANIN_TEST_NULL'] = 'null';

        $this->assertNull(Env::get('KANIN_TEST_NULL', 'default'));
    }

    private function track(string $key): void
    {
        $this->keys[] = $key;
    }

    private function envFile(string $contents): string
    {
        $path = (string) tempnam(sys_get_temp_dir(), 'kanin-env-');
        file_put_contents($path, $contents);
        $this->files[] = $path;

        return $path;
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement Env**

Create `system/Support/Env.php`:

```php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace Kanin\Support;

/**
 * Reads `.env` files and environment variables.
 *
 * A `.env` file holds settings that change between machines (database
 * password, debug mode, API tokens) so they never have to be hard-coded:
 *
 *     APP_DEBUG=true
 *     APP_NAME="My API"   # quotes are optional
 */
final class Env
{
    /**
     * Turn the text of a `.env` file into a [KEY => value] array.
     *
     * Blank lines, `# comment` lines, lines without `=` and invalid key names
     * are skipped. Values always stay strings; get() does the casting.
     *
     * @return array<string, string>
     */
    public static function parse(string $contents): array
    {
        $values = [];

        foreach (preg_split('/\r\n|\r|\n/', $contents) ?: [] as $line) {
            $line = trim($line);

            if ($line === '' || str_starts_with($line, '#') || ! str_contains($line, '=')) {
                continue;
            }

            [$key, $value] = explode('=', $line, 2);
            $key = trim($key);

            if (preg_match('/^[A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_.]*$/', $key) !== 1) {
                continue;
            }

            $values[$key] = self::parseValue(trim($value));
        }

        return $values;
    }

    /**
     * Load a `.env` file into the environment.
     *
     * Variables that already exist (set by the server, the shell or
     * phpunit.xml) are NEVER overwritten, so real environment wins.
     */
    public static function load(string $path): void
    {
        if (! is_file($path) || ! is_readable($path)) {
            return;
        }

        foreach (self::parse((string) file_get_contents($path)) as $key => $value) {
            if (getenv($key) !== false || array_key_exists($key, $_ENV)) {
                continue;
            }

            $_ENV[$key] = $value;
            $_SERVER[$key] = $value;
            putenv("{$key}={$value}");
        }
    }

    /**
     * Read an environment variable, or $default when it is not set.
     *
     * The strings true/false/null/empty (also written as "(true)" etc.)
     * are converted to true, false, null and '' respectively.
     */
    public static function get(string $key, mixed $default = null): mixed
    {
        if (array_key_exists($key, $_ENV)) {
            $value = $_ENV[$key];
        } elseif (array_key_exists($key, $_SERVER)) {
            $value = $_SERVER[$key];
        } else {
            $value = getenv($key);

            if ($value === false) {
                return $default;
            }
        }

        return is_string($value) ? self::cast($value) : $value;
    }

    /**
     * Remove surrounding quotes, or a trailing comment from unquoted values.
     */
    private static function parseValue(string $value): string
    {
        if (preg_match('/^(["\'])(.*)\1\s*(#.*)?$/', $value, $matches) === 1) {
            return $matches[2];
        }

        return trim((string) preg_replace('/\s+#.*$/', '', $value));
    }

    /**
     * Convert the special words true/false/null/empty to real PHP values.
     */
    private static function cast(string $value): mixed
    {
        return match (strtolower($value)) {
            'true', '(true)' => true,
            'false', '(false)' => false,
            'null', '(null)' => null,
            'empty', '(empty)' => '',
            default => $value,
        };
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 7: Implement Config**

Create `system/Support/Config.php`:

```php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace Kanin\Support;

/**
 * Holds every configuration value and reads them with "dot notation".
 *
 * The file config/database.php becomes the key "database", so
 * get('database.connections.sqlite.driver') walks into nested arrays.
 */
class Config
{
    /**
     * @param array<string, mixed> $items
     */
    public function __construct(protected array $items = [])
    {
    }

    /**
     * Load every `*.php` file in a directory; each must return an array.
     *
     * A missing directory simply gives an empty Config.
     */
    public static function fromDirectory(string $path): static
    {
        $items = [];

        foreach (glob(rtrim($path, '/\\') . '/*.php') ?: [] as $file) {
            $values = (static fn (string $__file): mixed => require $__file)($file);

            if (! is_array($values)) {
                throw new \UnexpectedValueException(
                    sprintf('Config file [%s] must return an array.', basename($file))
                );
            }

            $items[basename($file, '.php')] = $values;
        }

        return new static($items);
    }

    /**
     * Read a value, e.g. get('app.name') or get('app.missing', 'fallback').
     */
    public function get(string $key, mixed $default = null): mixed
    {
        $value = $this->items;

        foreach (explode('.', $key) as $segment) {
            if (! is_array($value) || ! array_key_exists($segment, $value)) {
                return $default;
            }

            $value = $value[$segment];
        }

        return $value;
    }

    /**
     * Write a value, creating nested arrays on the way: set('a.b.c', 1).
     */
    public function set(string $key, mixed $value): void
    {
        $target = &$this->items;

        foreach (explode('.', $key) as $segment) {
            if (! isset($target[$segment]) || ! is_array($target[$segment])) {
                $target[$segment] = [];
            }

            $target = &$target[$segment];
        }

        $target = $value;
    }

    /**
     * Check whether a key exists (even when its value is null).
     */
    public function has(string $key): bool
    {
        $marker = new \stdClass();

        return $this->get($key, $marker) !== $marker;
    }

    /**
     * Get every configuration value as one nested array.
     *
     * @return array<string, mixed>
     */
    public function all(): array
    {
        return $this->items;
    }
}
```

### Task 4: HTTP Layer (Method, Middleware, Request, Response, JsonResponse, HTTP exceptions)

**Files:**
- Create: `system/Http/HttpException.php`
- Create: `system/Http/NotFoundHttpException.php`
- Create: `system/Http/MethodNotAllowedHttpException.php`
- Create: `system/Http/Response.php`
- Create: `system/Http/JsonResponse.php`
- Create: `system/Http/Method.php`
- Create: `system/Http/Request.php`
- Create: `system/Http/Middleware.php`
- Test: `tests/Unit/Http/HttpExceptionTest.php`
- Test: `tests/Unit/Http/ResponseTest.php`
- Test: `tests/Unit/Http/JsonResponseTest.php`
- Test: `tests/Unit/Http/MethodTest.php`
- Test: `tests/Unit/Http/RequestTest.php`
- Test: `tests/Unit/Http/MiddlewareTest.php`

**Interfaces:**

Consumes:
- Task 1 only: Composer PSR-4 autoload `Kanin\` → `system/` and `Tests\` → `tests/`, the `phpunit.xml` Unit suite, `vendor/bin/phpunit`. No framework classes from Tasks 2-3 are used.

Produces (exact, from the Interface Contract; `Request::validate()` is added later by Task 9):
- `enum Kanin\Http\Method: string` — cases `GET, HEAD, POST, PUT, PATCH, DELETE, OPTIONS`; `isReadOnly(): bool` (GET, HEAD, OPTIONS).
- `interface Kanin\Http\Middleware { public function handle(Request $request, \Closure $next): Response; }`
- `Kanin\Http\Request`:
  - `__construct(string $method, string $uri, array $query = [], array $request = [], array $server = [], array $headers = [], ?string $content = null)`
  - `static capture(): static`, `static create(string $uri, string $method = 'GET', array $parameters = [], array $headers = [], ?string $content = null, array $server = []): static`
  - `method(): Method`, `path(): string`, `input(?string $key = null, mixed $default = null): mixed`, `all(): array`, `only(string ...$keys): array`, `except(string ...$keys): array`, `has(string $key): bool`, `query(?string $key = null, mixed $default = null): mixed`
  - `header(string $name, ?string $default = null): ?string`, `bearerToken(): ?string`, `isJson(): bool`, `isSecure(): bool`, `host(): string`
  - `setRouteParameters(array $parameters): void`, `route(?string $key = null, mixed $default = null): mixed`
- `Kanin\Http\Response`: `__construct(string $content = '', int $status = 200, array $headers = [])`, `getContent(): string`, `setContent(string $content): static`, `getStatusCode(): int`, `setStatusCode(int $status): static`, `header(string $name, string $value): static`, `getHeader(string $name): ?string`, `headers(): array`, `send(): void`.
- `Kanin\Http\JsonResponse extends Response`: `__construct(mixed $data = null, int $status = 200, array $headers = [])`, `getData(): mixed`.
- `Kanin\Http\HttpException extends \RuntimeException`: `__construct(int $statusCode, string $message = '', array $headers = [], ?\Throwable $previous = null)`, `getStatusCode(): int`, `getHeaders(): array`.
- `Kanin\Http\NotFoundHttpException extends HttpException`: `__construct(string $message = 'Not Found')` → 404.
- `Kanin\Http\MethodNotAllowedHttpException extends HttpException`: `__construct(array $allowed)` → 405, message `Method Not Allowed`, header `Allow: GET, POST`.

Behaviour details later tasks may rely on (contract gaps resolved here):
- `Request::create()` derives headers from its `$server` array exactly like `capture()` does (`HTTP_*`, `CONTENT_TYPE`, `CONTENT_LENGTH`), then merges `$headers` over them, so a `create()`d request has `Host: localhost` by default.
- `input()` keys are plain top-level keys (no dot notation). A JSON body is merged over the form `$request` values, then the body is merged over the query (`array_replace`). Whitespace-only JSON content counts as empty (no 400).
- `method()` reads `_method` through `input()` (query, form or JSON body) and falls back to the `X-HTTP-Method-Override` header only when `_method` is absent; so a malformed JSON POST already throws the 400 from `method()` (that is, from routing).
- `path()` strips the query and fragment, accepts absolute URIs (`http://host/x`), uses `rawurldecode` (a `+` stays `+`), and trims surrounding slashes.
- `Response::header()` refuses names that are not RFC 7230 tokens and values containing CR, LF or NUL (`\InvalidArgumentException`; this guards against header injection). `setStatusCode()` and the constructor refuse codes outside 100-599 (`\InvalidArgumentException`). `headers()` keeps insertion order; replacing a header keeps its position and takes the new spelling.
- `JsonResponse` defaults to `Content-Type: application/json`; a caller-supplied `Content-Type` (any case) wins. It exposes `public const ENCODING_FLAGS` (`JSON_THROW_ON_ERROR | JSON_UNESCAPED_SLASHES | JSON_UNESCAPED_UNICODE`). Unencodable data throws `\JsonException`.
- `MethodNotAllowedHttpException` uppercases and de-duplicates the allowed list, keeping first-seen order.

```php
<?php
```
