```php
use PHPUnit\Framework\TestCase;

final class HttpExceptionTest extends TestCase
{
    public function test_http_exception_carries_status_message_headers_and_previous(): void
    {
        $previous = new \LogicException('root cause');
        $e = new HttpException(401, 'Unauthenticated.', ['WWW-Authenticate' => 'Bearer'], $previous);

        $this->assertInstanceOf(\RuntimeException::class, $e);
        $this->assertSame(401, $e->getStatusCode());
        $this->assertSame('Unauthenticated.', $e->getMessage());
        $this->assertSame(['WWW-Authenticate' => 'Bearer'], $e->getHeaders());
        $this->assertSame($previous, $e->getPrevious());
    }

    public function test_http_exception_defaults_to_empty_message_and_no_headers(): void
    {
        $e = new HttpException(503);

        $this->assertSame(503, $e->getStatusCode());
        $this->assertSame('', $e->getMessage());
        $this->assertSame([], $e->getHeaders());
        $this->assertNull($e->getPrevious());
    }

    public function test_http_exception_can_be_thrown_and_caught(): void
    {

        $this->expectExceptionMessage('Teapot');

        throw new HttpException(418, 'Teapot');

        $this->assertInstanceOf(HttpException::class, $e);

        $this->assertSame('Not Found', $e->getMessage());
        $this->assertSame([], $e->getHeaders());

        $this->assertSame(404, $e->getStatusCode());
        $this->assertSame('User not found.', $e->getMessage());

    {
        $e = new MethodNotAllowedHttpException(['GET', 'POST']);

        $this->assertInstanceOf(HttpException::class, $e);
        $this->assertSame(405, $e->getStatusCode());
        $this->assertSame('Method Not Allowed', $e->getMessage());
        $this->assertSame(['Allow' => 'GET, POST'], $e->getHeaders());
    }

    {

        $this->assertSame(['Allow' => 'GET, HEAD, DELETE'], $e->getHeaders());
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement HttpException**

Create `system/Http/HttpException.php`:

```php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace Kanin\Http;

/**
 * An exception that already knows which HTTP status code to answer with.
 *
 * Throw it anywhere (controller, middleware, model) and the exception
 * handler turns it into a JSON response such as {"message": "..."}.
 */
class HttpException extends \RuntimeException
{
    /**
     * @param int                   $statusCode HTTP status, e.g. 401 or 404.
     * @param string                $message    Text shown to the API client.
     * @param array<string, string> $headers    Extra response headers.
     * @param \Throwable|null       $previous   The original error, if any.
     */
    public function __construct(
        private readonly int $statusCode,
        string $message = '',
        private readonly array $headers = [],
        ?\Throwable $previous = null,
    ) {
        parent::__construct($message, 0, $previous);
    }

    /**
     * The HTTP status code the response should use.
     */
    public function getStatusCode(): int
    {
        return $this->statusCode;
    }

    /**
     * Extra headers to add to the response (name => value).
     *
     * @return array<string, string>
     */
    public function getHeaders(): array
    {
        return $this->headers;
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Implement NotFoundHttpException and MethodNotAllowedHttpException**

Create `system/Http/NotFoundHttpException.php`:

```php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace Kanin\Http;

/**
 * Thrown when nothing exists at the requested address (HTTP 404).
 */
class NotFoundHttpException extends HttpException
{
    /**
     * Create a 404 exception with an optional custom message.
     */
    public function __construct(string $message = 'Not Found')
    {
        parent::__construct(404, $message);
    }
}
```

Create `system/Http/MethodNotAllowedHttpException.php`:

```php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace Kanin\Http;

/**
 * Thrown when the URL exists but not for the HTTP method used (HTTP 405).
 *
 * The response carries an "Allow" header listing the methods that work,
 * for example "Allow: GET, POST".
 */
class MethodNotAllowedHttpException extends HttpException
{
    /**
     * @param list<string> $allowed The methods this URL accepts, e.g. ['GET', 'POST'].
     */
    public function __construct(array $allowed)
    {
        $methods = array_values(array_unique(array_map('strtoupper', $allowed)));

        parent::__construct(405, 'Method Not Allowed', ['Allow' => implode(', ', $methods)]);
    }
}
```

```php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace Tests\Unit\Http;

use Kanin\Http\Response;
use PHPUnit\Framework\TestCase;

final class ResponseTest extends TestCase
{
    public function test_defaults_to_empty_200_plain_text(): void
    {
        $response = new Response();

        $this->assertSame('', $response->getContent());
        $this->assertSame(200, $response->getStatusCode());
        $this->assertSame('text/plain; charset=UTF-8', $response->getHeader('Content-Type'));
        $this->assertSame(['Content-Type' => 'text/plain; charset=UTF-8'], $response->headers());
    }

    public function test_constructor_sets_content_status_and_headers(): void
    {
        $response = new Response('<p>hi</p>', 201, ['content-type' => 'text/html', 'X-Id' => '7']);

        $this->assertSame('<p>hi</p>', $response->getContent());
        $this->assertSame(201, $response->getStatusCode());
        $this->assertSame('text/html', $response->getHeader('Content-Type'));
        $this->assertSame(['content-type' => 'text/html', 'X-Id' => '7'], $response->headers());
    }

    public function test_setters_are_fluent(): void
    {
        $response = new Response();

        $result = $response->setContent('changed')->setStatusCode(202)->header('X-A', '1');

        $this->assertSame($response, $result);
        $this->assertSame('changed', $response->getContent());
        $this->assertSame(202, $response->getStatusCode());
    }

    public function test_header_replaces_existing_header_case_insensitively(): void
    {
        $response = new Response('', 200, ['X-Token' => 'old']);

        $response->header('x-token', 'new');

        $this->assertSame('new', $response->getHeader('X-TOKEN'));
        $this->assertSame(['x-token' => 'new', 'Content-Type' => 'text/plain; charset=UTF-8'], $response->headers());
    }

    public function test_get_header_returns_null_when_missing(): void
    {
        $this->assertNull((new Response())->getHeader('X-Missing'));
    }

    public function test_header_refuses_line_breaks_in_value(): void
    {
        $this->expectException(\InvalidArgumentException::class);
        $this->expectExceptionMessage('Header [X-Evil] contains a line break.');

        (new Response())->header('X-Evil', "ok\r\nSet-Cookie: stolen=1");
    }

    public function test_header_refuses_invalid_names(): void
    {
        $this->expectException(\InvalidArgumentException::class);
        $this->expectExceptionMessage('Invalid header name [Bad Name:].');

        (new Response())->header('Bad Name:', 'x');
    }

    public function test_status_code_below_100_is_refused(): void
    {
        $this->expectException(\InvalidArgumentException::class);
        $this->expectExceptionMessage('Invalid HTTP status code [99].');

        new Response('', 99);
    }

    public function test_status_code_above_599_is_refused(): void
    {
        $this->expectException(\InvalidArgumentException::class);
        $this->expectExceptionMessage('Invalid HTTP status code [600].');

        (new Response())->setStatusCode(600);
    }

    public function test_send_echoes_the_content(): void
    {
        $response = new Response('hello', 201);

        ob_start();
        $response->send();
        $output = ob_get_clean();

        $this->assertSame('hello', $output);
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 10: Implement Response**

Create `system/Http/Response.php`:

```php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace Kanin\Http;

/**
 * An HTTP response: a status code, some headers and a body.
 *
 * Build one, adjust it as needed, then call send() to deliver it to the
 * browser or API client.
 */
class Response
{
    /** The status code, e.g. 200 (OK) or 404 (Not Found). */
    protected int $statusCode = 200;

    /**
     * Headers keyed by lowercase name, so lookups ignore case.
     * Each entry keeps the original spelling: ['content-type' => ['Content-Type', 'text/plain']].
     *
     * @var array<string, array{0: string, 1: string}>
     */
    protected array $headers = [];

    /**
     * @param string                $content The response body.
     * @param int                   $status  The HTTP status code (100-599).
     * @param array<string, string> $headers Headers as name => value.
     */
    public function __construct(protected string $content = '', int $status = 200, array $headers = [])
    {
        $this->setStatusCode($status);

        foreach ($headers as $name => $value) {
            $this->header((string) $name, (string) $value);
        }

        if ($this->getHeader('Content-Type') === null) {
            $this->header('Content-Type', 'text/plain; charset=UTF-8');
        }
    }

    /**
     * Get the response body.
     */
    public function getContent(): string
    {
        return $this->content;
    }

    /**
     * Replace the response body.
     */
    public function setContent(string $content): static
    {
        $this->content = $content;

        return $this;
    }

    /**
     * Get the HTTP status code.
     */
    public function getStatusCode(): int
    {
        return $this->statusCode;
    }

    /**
     * Change the HTTP status code. Only 100-599 are valid HTTP statuses.
     *
     * @throws \InvalidArgumentException When the code is outside 100-599.
     */
    public function setStatusCode(int $status): static
    {
        if ($status < 100 || $status > 599) {
            throw new \InvalidArgumentException("Invalid HTTP status code [{$status}].");
        }

        $this->statusCode = $status;

        return $this;
    }

    /**
     * Set a header, replacing any existing header with the same name (any case).
     *
     * Line breaks are refused because they would let an attacker inject
     * extra headers into the response ("header injection").
     *
     * @throws \InvalidArgumentException When the name is invalid or the value contains a line break.
     */
    public function header(string $name, string $value): static
    {
        if (preg_match('/^[A-Za-z0-9!#$%&\'*+.^_`|~-]+$/', $name) !== 1) {
            throw new \InvalidArgumentException("Invalid header name [{$name}].");
        }

        if (preg_match('/[\r\n\0]/', $value) === 1) {
            throw new \InvalidArgumentException("Header [{$name}] contains a line break.");
        }

        $this->headers[strtolower($name)] = [$name, $value];

        return $this;
    }

    /**
     * Read one header value (case-insensitive), or null when it is not set.
     */
    public function getHeader(string $name): ?string
    {
        return $this->headers[strtolower($name)][1] ?? null;
    }

    /**
     * Get every header as name => value, using the spelling they were set with.
     *
     * @return array<string, string>
     */
    public function headers(): array
    {
        $headers = [];

        foreach ($this->headers as [$name, $value]) {
            $headers[$name] = $value;
        }

        return $headers;
    }

    /**
     * Send the status code, headers and body to the client.
     */
    public function send(): void
    {
        if (! headers_sent()) {
            http_response_code($this->statusCode);

            foreach ($this->headers() as $name => $value) {
                header("{$name}: {$value}", true);
            }
        }

        echo $this->content;
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 11: Implement JsonResponse**

Create `system/Http/JsonResponse.php`:

```php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace Kanin\Http;

/**
 * A response whose body is JSON. This is what a KaninPHP API returns most.
 *
 *     return new JsonResponse(['name' => 'Ada'], 201);
 */
class JsonResponse extends Response
{
    /** Flags used for every encode: throw on failure, keep "/" and unicode readable. */
    public const ENCODING_FLAGS = JSON_THROW_ON_ERROR | JSON_UNESCAPED_SLASHES | JSON_UNESCAPED_UNICODE;

    /**
     * @param mixed                 $data    Anything json_encode() accepts (arrays, models, ...).
     * @param int                   $status  The HTTP status code. 204 always has an empty body.
     * @param array<string, string> $headers Extra headers as name => value. A Content-Type
     *                                       given here replaces the default application/json.
     *
     * @throws \JsonException When $data cannot be encoded (e.g. invalid UTF-8 or INF).
     */
    public function __construct(mixed $data = null, int $status = 200, array $headers = [])
    {
        $content = $status === 204 ? '' : json_encode($data, self::ENCODING_FLAGS);

        parent::__construct($content, $status, array_merge(['Content-Type' => 'application/json'], $headers));
    }

    /**
     * Decode the JSON body back into PHP data (objects become arrays).
     *
     * An empty body (e.g. a 204 response) gives null.
     *
     * @throws \JsonException When the body is not valid JSON.
     */
    public function getData(): mixed
    {
        if ($this->content === '') {
            return null;
        }

        return json_decode($this->content, true, 512, JSON_THROW_ON_ERROR);
    }
}
```

```php
<?php

namespace Tests\Unit\Http;

final class MethodTest extends TestCase
{
```

```php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace Tests\Unit\Http;

use Kanin\Http\HttpException;
use Kanin\Http\Method;
use Kanin\Http\Request;
use PHPUnit\Framework\TestCase;

final class RequestTest extends TestCase
{
    /** @var array<string, mixed> */
    private array $savedServer = [];

    /** @var array<string, mixed> */
    private array $savedGet = [];

    /** @var array<string, mixed> */
    private array $savedPost = [];

    protected function setUp(): void
    {
        $this->savedServer = $_SERVER;
        $this->savedGet = $_GET;
        $this->savedPost = $_POST;
    }

    protected function tearDown(): void
    {
        $_SERVER = $this->savedServer;
        $_GET = $this->savedGet;
        $_POST = $this->savedPost;
    }

    public function test_constructor_lowercases_header_names(): void
    {
        $request = new Request('get', '/', [], [], [], ['X-Custom' => 'a', 'ACCEPT' => ['text/html', 'application/json']]);

        $this->assertSame(Method::GET, $request->method());
        $this->assertSame('a', $request->header('x-custom'));
        $this->assertSame('text/html, application/json', $request->header('Accept'));
    }

    public function test_create_parses_query_string_from_uri(): void
    {
        $request = Request::create('/api/users?page=2&per_page=5');

        $this->assertSame(Method::GET, $request->method());
        $this->assertSame('/api/users', $request->path());
        $this->assertSame(['page' => '2', 'per_page' => '5'], $request->query());
        $this->assertSame('2', $request->query('page'));
        $this->assertSame('x', $request->query('missing', 'x'));
    }

    public function test_create_puts_parameters_in_query_for_get_and_head(): void
    {
        $get = Request::create('/search?q=old', 'GET', ['q' => 'new', 'sort' => 'name']);
        $head = Request::create('/search', 'HEAD', ['q' => 'kanin']);

        $this->assertSame(['q' => 'new', 'sort' => 'name'], $get->query());
        $this->assertSame(['q' => 'kanin'], $head->query());
        $this->assertSame(Method::HEAD, $head->method());
    }

    public function test_create_puts_parameters_in_body_for_other_methods(): void
    {
        $request = Request::create('/users?page=1', 'post', ['name' => 'Ada']);

        $this->assertSame(Method::POST, $request->method());
        $this->assertSame(['page' => '1'], $request->query());
        $this->assertSame(['page' => '1', 'name' => 'Ada'], $request->all());
    }

    public function test_create_sets_default_server_values_and_host(): void
    {
        $request = Request::create('/x', 'DELETE');

        $this->assertSame('localhost', $request->host());
        $this->assertSame('localhost', $request->header('Host'));
        $this->assertSame(Method::DELETE, $request->method());
    }

    public function test_create_accepts_server_overrides(): void
    {
        $request = Request::create('/', 'GET', [], [], null, ['HTTP_HOST' => 'api.example.com', 'HTTPS' => 'on']);

        $this->assertSame('api.example.com', $request->host());
        $this->assertTrue($request->isSecure());
    }

    public function test_capture_reads_superglobals(): void
    {
        $_SERVER = [
            'REQUEST_METHOD' => 'PUT',
            'REQUEST_URI' => '/api/users/3?debug=1',
            'HTTP_X_REQUESTED_WITH' => 'XMLHttpRequest',
            'HTTP_AUTHORIZATION' => 'Bearer secret',
            'CONTENT_TYPE' => 'application/x-www-form-urlencoded',
            'CONTENT_LENGTH' => '8',
        ];
        $_GET = ['debug' => '1'];
        $_POST = ['name' => 'Ada'];

        $request = Request::capture();

        $this->assertSame(Method::PUT, $request->method());
        $this->assertSame('/api/users/3', $request->path());
        $this->assertSame('XMLHttpRequest', $request->header('X-Requested-With'));
        $this->assertSame('application/x-www-form-urlencoded', $request->header('Content-Type'));
        $this->assertSame('8', $request->header('Content-Length'));
        $this->assertSame('secret', $request->bearerToken());
        $this->assertSame(['debug' => '1', 'name' => 'Ada'], $request->all());
    }

    public function test_capture_defaults_when_server_is_empty(): void
    {
        $_SERVER = [];
        $_GET = [];
        $_POST = [];

        $request = Request::capture();

        $this->assertSame(Method::GET, $request->method());
        $this->assertSame('/', $request->path());
        $this->assertSame('localhost', $request->host());
    }

    public function test_unknown_method_is_refused_with_405(): void
    {
        $request = Request::create('/', 'TRACE');

        try {
            $request->method();
            $this->fail('Expected HttpException.');
        } catch (HttpException $e) {
            $this->assertSame(405, $e->getStatusCode());
            $this->assertSame('Method Not Allowed', $e->getMessage());
        }
    }

    public function test_post_can_be_overridden_by_method_input(): void
    {
        $this->assertSame(Method::PUT, Request::create('/', 'POST', ['_method' => 'PUT'])->method());
        $this->assertSame(Method::PATCH, Request::create('/', 'POST', ['_method' => 'patch'])->method());
        $this->assertSame(Method::DELETE, Request::create('/', 'POST', ['_method' => ' Delete '])->method());
    }

    public function test_post_can_be_overridden_by_header(): void
    {
        $request = Request::create('/', 'POST', [], ['X-HTTP-Method-Override' => 'delete']);

        $this->assertSame(Method::DELETE, $request->method());
    }

    public function test_override_from_json_body_is_honoured(): void
    {
        $request = Request::create('/', 'POST', [], ['Content-Type' => 'application/json'], '{"_method":"PATCH"}');

        $this->assertSame(Method::PATCH, $request->method());
    }

    public function test_override_to_other_methods_is_ignored(): void
    {
        $this->assertSame(Method::POST, Request::create('/', 'POST', ['_method' => 'GET'])->method());
        $this->assertSame(Method::POST, Request::create('/', 'POST', ['_method' => 'OPTIONS'])->method());
        $this->assertSame(Method::POST, Request::create('/', 'POST', ['_method' => ['PUT']])->method());
        $this->assertSame(
            Method::POST,
            Request::create('/', 'POST', [], ['X-HTTP-Method-Override' => 'TRACE'])->method(),
        );
    }

    public function test_override_only_applies_to_post(): void
    {
        $get = Request::create('/', 'GET', ['_method' => 'DELETE']);
        $put = Request::create('/', 'PUT', [], ['X-HTTP-Method-Override' => 'DELETE']);

        $this->assertSame(Method::GET, $get->method());
        $this->assertSame(Method::PUT, $put->method());
    }

    public function test_path_is_normalized(): void
    {
        $this->assertSame('/', Request::create('')->path());
        $this->assertSame('/', Request::create('/')->path());
        $this->assertSame('/', Request::create('/?a=1')->path());
        $this->assertSame('/api/users', Request::create('/api/users/')->path());
        $this->assertSame('/api/users', Request::create('api/users#top')->path());
        $this->assertSame('/files/my report', Request::create('/files/my%20report')->path());
        $this->assertSame('/a+b', Request::create('/a+b')->path());
        $this->assertSame('/api', Request::create('http://example.com/api/?x=1')->path());
    }

    public function test_json_body_is_decoded_and_merged_over_query(): void
    {
        $request = Request::create(
            '/users?name=query&page=3',
            'POST',
            [],
            ['Content-Type' => 'application/json; charset=utf-8'],
            '{"name":"Ada","tags":["math"]}',
        );

        $this->assertTrue($request->isJson());
        $this->assertSame('Ada', $request->input('name'));
        $this->assertSame(['math'], $request->input('tags'));
        $this->assertSame('3', $request->input('page'));
        $this->assertSame(['name' => 'Ada', 'page' => '3', 'tags' => ['math']], $request->input());
    }

    public function test_plus_json_content_types_are_json(): void
    {
        $request = Request::create('/', 'POST', [], ['Content-Type' => 'application/vnd.api+json'], '{"a":1}');

        $this->assertTrue($request->isJson());
        $this->assertSame(1, $request->input('a'));
    }

    public function test_non_json_content_type_ignores_raw_content(): void
    {
        $request = Request::create('/', 'POST', ['a' => '1'], ['Content-Type' => 'text/plain'], '{"b":2}');

        $this->assertFalse($request->isJson());
        $this->assertSame(['a' => '1'], $request->all());
    }

    public function test_empty_json_body_gives_form_input(): void
    {
        $request = Request::create('/', 'POST', ['a' => '1'], ['Content-Type' => 'application/json'], '');

        $this->assertSame(['a' => '1'], $request->all());
    }

    public function test_malformed_json_body_is_a_400(): void
    {
        $request = Request::create('/', 'POST', [], ['Content-Type' => 'application/json'], '{"name": ');

        try {
            $request->input('name');
            $this->fail('Expected HttpException.');
        } catch (HttpException $e) {
            $this->assertSame(400, $e->getStatusCode());
            $this->assertSame('Malformed JSON body.', $e->getMessage());
            $this->assertInstanceOf(\JsonException::class, $e->getPrevious());
        }
    }

    public function test_json_scalar_body_is_a_400(): void
    {
        $request = Request::create('/', 'POST', [], ['Content-Type' => 'application/json'], '"just a string"');

        $this->expectException(HttpException::class);
        $this->expectExceptionMessage('Malformed JSON body.');

        $request->all();
    }

    public function test_malformed_json_breaks_method_override_lookup_with_400(): void
    {
        $request = Request::create('/', 'POST', [], ['Content-Type' => 'application/json'], 'not json');

        $this->expectException(HttpException::class);
        $this->expectExceptionMessage('Malformed JSON body.');

        $request->method();
    }

    public function test_input_returns_default_for_missing_key_but_keeps_null_values(): void
    {
        $request = Request::create('/', 'POST', [], ['Content-Type' => 'application/json'], '{"nick":null}');

        $this->assertSame('fallback', $request->input('missing', 'fallback'));
        $this->assertNull($request->input('nick', 'fallback'));
    }

    public function test_only_except_and_has(): void
    {
        $request = Request::create('/', 'POST', ['name' => 'Ada', 'email' => 'ada@example.com', 'role' => '']);

        $this->assertSame(['name' => 'Ada'], $request->only('name', 'missing'));
        $this->assertSame(['name' => 'Ada', 'role' => ''], $request->except('email'));
        $this->assertTrue($request->has('role'));
        $this->assertFalse($request->has('missing'));
    }

    public function test_header_is_case_insensitive_with_default(): void
    {
        $request = Request::create('/', 'GET', [], ['Accept' => 'application/json']);

        $this->assertSame('application/json', $request->header('ACCEPT'));
        $this->assertNull($request->header('X-Missing'));
        $this->assertSame('none', $request->header('X-Missing', 'none'));
    }

    public function test_bearer_token_is_extracted(): void
    {
        $request = Request::create('/', 'GET', [], ['Authorization' => 'Bearer abc123']);

        $this->assertSame('abc123', $request->bearerToken());
    }

    public function test_bearer_scheme_is_case_insensitive_and_token_trimmed(): void
    {
        $request = Request::create('/', 'GET', [], ['authorization' => '  bearer    abc123  ']);

        $this->assertSame('abc123', $request->bearerToken());
    }

    public function test_bearer_token_is_null_when_missing_empty_or_other_scheme(): void
    {
        $this->assertNull(Request::create('/')->bearerToken());
        $this->assertNull(Request::create('/', 'GET', [], ['Authorization' => 'Bearer'])->bearerToken());
        $this->assertNull(Request::create('/', 'GET', [], ['Authorization' => 'Bearer    '])->bearerToken());
        $this->assertNull(Request::create('/', 'GET', [], ['Authorization' => 'Basic dXNlcjpwYXNz'])->bearerToken());
        $this->assertNull(Request::create('/', 'GET', [], ['Authorization' => 'Bearerabc'])->bearerToken());
    }

    public function test_is_secure_checks_https_server_value(): void
    {
        $this->assertFalse(Request::create('/')->isSecure());
        $this->assertFalse(Request::create('/', 'GET', [], [], null, ['HTTPS' => 'off'])->isSecure());
        $this->assertFalse(Request::create('/', 'GET', [], [], null, ['HTTPS' => ''])->isSecure());
        $this->assertTrue(Request::create('/', 'GET', [], [], null, ['HTTPS' => '1'])->isSecure());
    }

    public function test_host_falls_back_to_server_name_then_localhost(): void
    {
        $fromHeader = new Request('GET', '/', [], [], ['SERVER_NAME' => 'server.test'], ['Host' => 'header.test:8000']);
        $fromServer = new Request('GET', '/', [], [], ['SERVER_NAME' => 'server.test']);
        $fallback = new Request('GET', '/');

        $this->assertSame('header.test:8000', $fromHeader->host());
        $this->assertSame('server.test', $fromServer->host());
        $this->assertSame('localhost', $fallback->host());
    }

    public function test_route_parameters(): void
    {
        $request = Request::create('/users/5');

        $this->assertSame([], $request->route());
        $this->assertNull($request->route('id'));

        $request->setRouteParameters(['id' => '5']);

        $this->assertSame(['id' => '5'], $request->route());
        $this->assertSame('5', $request->route('id'));
        $this->assertSame('d', $request->route('missing', 'd'));
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 17: Implement the Method enum**

Create `system/Http/Method.php`:

```php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace Kanin\Http;

/**
 * The HTTP methods (verbs) KaninPHP understands.
 *
 * Using an enum instead of plain strings means a typo such as "GTE"
 * cannot sneak through: PHP only accepts the cases listed here.
 */
enum Method: string
{
    case GET = 'GET';
    case HEAD = 'HEAD';
    case POST = 'POST';
    case PUT = 'PUT';
    case PATCH = 'PATCH';
    case DELETE = 'DELETE';
    case OPTIONS = 'OPTIONS';

    /**
     * Tell whether this method only reads data and never changes it.
     *
     * GET, HEAD and OPTIONS are "safe" methods; the rest may modify data.
     */
    public function isReadOnly(): bool
    {
        return match ($this) {
            self::GET, self::HEAD, self::OPTIONS => true,
            default => false,
        };
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 18: Implement Request**

Create `system/Http/Request.php` (`validate()` is intentionally absent; Task 9 adds it):

```php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace Kanin\Http;

/**
 * An incoming HTTP request: method, path, query string, body and headers.
 *
 * In production it is built from PHP's superglobals with Request::capture().
 * In tests you build one by hand with Request::create('/api/users', 'POST', [...]).
 */
class Request
{
    /** The HTTP method exactly as the client sent it, uppercased (e.g. "POST"). */
    protected string $realMethod;

    /** @var array<string, string> Headers keyed by lowercase name. */
    protected array $headers = [];

    /** @var array<string, mixed>|null The decoded body, filled in on first use. */
    protected ?array $body = null;

    /** @var array<string, mixed> Values captured from the URL by the router, e.g. ['id' => '5']. */
    protected array $routeParameters = [];

    /**
     * @param string               $method  HTTP method, e.g. "GET".
     * @param string               $uri     Request URI, e.g. "/api/users?page=2".
     * @param array<string, mixed> $query   Query string values ($_GET).
     * @param array<string, mixed> $request Form body values ($_POST).
     * @param array<string, mixed> $server  Server values ($_SERVER).
     * @param array<string, mixed> $headers Headers as name => value (any case).
     * @param string|null          $content The raw body (php://input).
     */
    public function __construct(
        string $method,
        protected string $uri,
        protected array $query = [],
        protected array $request = [],
        protected array $server = [],
        array $headers = [],
        protected ?string $content = null,
    ) {
        $this->realMethod = strtoupper($method);

        foreach ($headers as $name => $value) {
            $this->headers[strtolower((string) $name)] = is_array($value)
                ? implode(', ', array_map('strval', $value))
                : (string) $value;
        }
    }

    /**
     * Build a Request from PHP's superglobals ($_SERVER, $_GET, $_POST, php://input).
     */
    public static function capture(): static
    {
        $content = file_get_contents('php://input');

        return new static(
            (string) ($_SERVER['REQUEST_METHOD'] ?? 'GET'),
            (string) ($_SERVER['REQUEST_URI'] ?? '/'),
            $_GET,
            $_POST,
            $_SERVER,
            static::headersFromServer($_SERVER),
            $content === false ? '' : $content,
        );
    }

    /**
     * Build a Request by hand. Handy in tests and scripts.
     *
     *     Request::create('/api/users?page=2');
     *     Request::create('/api/users', 'POST', ['name' => 'Ada']);
     *
     * @param array<string, mixed> $parameters Sent as query for GET/HEAD, as body otherwise.
     * @param array<string, mixed> $headers    Headers as name => value.
     * @param array<string, mixed> $server     Extra or overriding $_SERVER values.
     */
    public static function create(
        string $uri,
        string $method = 'GET',
        array $parameters = [],
        array $headers = [],
        ?string $content = null,
        array $server = [],
    ): static {
        $method = strtoupper($method);
        $server = array_replace(
            ['HTTP_HOST' => 'localhost', 'REQUEST_METHOD' => $method, 'REQUEST_URI' => $uri],
            $server,
        );

        $query = [];
        $queryString = parse_url($uri, PHP_URL_QUERY);
        if (is_string($queryString)) {
            parse_str($queryString, $query);
        }

        $isRead = in_array($method, ['GET', 'HEAD'], true);
        $query = $isRead ? array_replace($query, $parameters) : $query;
        $body = $isRead ? [] : $parameters;
        $headers = array_replace(static::headersFromServer($server), array_change_key_case($headers));

        return new static($method, $uri, $query, $body, $server, $headers, $content);
    }

    /**
     * The HTTP method as a Method enum.
     *
     * HTML forms can only send GET and POST, so a POST may ask to be treated
     * as PUT, PATCH or DELETE with an "_method" input or an
     * "X-HTTP-Method-Override" header.
     *
     * @throws HttpException 405 when the client used a method KaninPHP does not know.
     */
    public function method(): Method
    {
        $method = Method::tryFrom($this->realMethod)
            ?? throw new HttpException(405, 'Method Not Allowed');

        if ($method !== Method::POST) {
            return $method;
        }

        $override = $this->input('_method') ?? $this->header('X-HTTP-Method-Override');
        $override = is_string($override) ? strtoupper(trim($override)) : '';

        return match ($override) {
            'PUT' => Method::PUT,
            'PATCH' => Method::PATCH,
            'DELETE' => Method::DELETE,
            default => Method::POST,
        };
    }

    /**
     * The decoded URL path without the query string: "/api/users", or "/" for the root.
     */
    public function path(): string
    {
        $path = substr($this->uri, 0, strcspn($this->uri, '?#'));

        if (preg_match('#^[A-Za-z][A-Za-z0-9+.-]*://#', $path) === 1) {
            $path = (string) parse_url($path, PHP_URL_PATH);
        }

        return '/' . trim(rawurldecode($path), '/');
    }

    /**
     * Read one input value, or all input when $key is null.
     *
     * Body values win over query string values with the same name.
     *
     * @throws HttpException 400 when the body claims to be JSON but is not valid JSON.
     */
    public function input(?string $key = null, mixed $default = null): mixed
    {
        $input = array_replace($this->query, $this->body());

        if ($key === null) {
            return $input;
        }

        return array_key_exists($key, $input) ? $input[$key] : $default;
    }

    /**
     * Every input value (query string plus body).
     *
     * @return array<string, mixed>
     */
    public function all(): array
    {
        return $this->input();
    }

    /**
     * Only the listed input keys that are actually present.
     *
     * @return array<string, mixed>
     */
    public function only(string ...$keys): array
    {
        return array_intersect_key($this->all(), array_flip($keys));
    }

    /**
     * Every input value except the listed keys.
     *
     * @return array<string, mixed>
     */
    public function except(string ...$keys): array
    {
        return array_diff_key($this->all(), array_flip($keys));
    }

    /**
     * Tell whether the input contains $key (even if its value is null or "").
     */
    public function has(string $key): bool
    {
        return array_key_exists($key, $this->all());
    }

    /**
     * Read one query string value, or the whole query string when $key is null.
     */
    public function query(?string $key = null, mixed $default = null): mixed
    {
        if ($key === null) {
            return $this->query;
        }

        return array_key_exists($key, $this->query) ? $this->query[$key] : $default;
    }

    /**
     * Read a header (the name is case-insensitive).
     */
    public function header(string $name, ?string $default = null): ?string
    {
        return $this->headers[strtolower($name)] ?? $default;
    }

    /**
     * The token from an "Authorization: Bearer <token>" header, or null.
     */
    public function bearerToken(): ?string
    {
        $header = trim((string) $this->header('Authorization', ''));

        if (preg_match('/^Bearer\s+(.*)$/is', $header, $matches) !== 1) {
            return null;
        }

        $token = trim($matches[1]);

        return $token === '' ? null : $token;
    }

    /**
     * Tell whether the body is JSON (Content-Type like application/json or application/vnd.api+json).
     */
    public function isJson(): bool
    {
        $type = strtolower((string) $this->header('Content-Type', ''));

        return str_contains($type, '/json') || str_contains($type, '+json');
    }

    /**
     * Tell whether the request came in over HTTPS.
     */
    public function isSecure(): bool
    {
        $https = strtolower((string) ($this->server['HTTPS'] ?? ''));

        return $https !== '' && $https !== 'off';
    }

    /**
     * The host name: the Host header, else SERVER_NAME, else "localhost".
     */
    public function host(): string
    {
        $host = $this->header('Host') ?? (string) ($this->server['SERVER_NAME'] ?? '');

        return $host === '' ? 'localhost' : $host;
    }

    /**
     * Store the values the router captured from the URL (e.g. {id}).
     *
     * @param array<string, mixed> $parameters
     */
    public function setRouteParameters(array $parameters): void
    {
        $this->routeParameters = $parameters;
    }

    /**
     * Read one route parameter, or all of them when $key is null.
     */
    public function route(?string $key = null, mixed $default = null): mixed
    {
        if ($key === null) {
            return $this->routeParameters;
        }

        return array_key_exists($key, $this->routeParameters) ? $this->routeParameters[$key] : $default;
    }

    /**
     * The request body as an array, decoding JSON the first time it is needed.
     *
     * @return array<string, mixed>
     *
     * @throws HttpException 400 when a JSON body is malformed or is not an object/array.
     */
    protected function body(): array
    {
        if ($this->body !== null) {
            return $this->body;
        }

        if (! $this->isJson() || $this->content === null || trim($this->content) === '') {
            return $this->body = $this->request;
        }

        try {
            $decoded = json_decode($this->content, true, 512, JSON_THROW_ON_ERROR);
        } catch (\JsonException $e) {
            throw new HttpException(400, 'Malformed JSON body.', [], $e);
        }

        if (! is_array($decoded)) {
            throw new HttpException(400, 'Malformed JSON body.');
        }

        return $this->body = array_replace($this->request, $decoded);
    }

    /**
     * Turn $_SERVER entries (HTTP_ACCEPT, CONTENT_TYPE, ...) into lowercase header names.
     *
     * @param array<string, mixed> $server
     *
     * @return array<string, string>
     */
    protected static function headersFromServer(array $server): array
    {
        $headers = [];

        foreach ($server as $key => $value) {
            $key = (string) $key;

            if (str_starts_with($key, 'HTTP_')) {
                $headers[strtolower(str_replace('_', '-', substr($key, 5)))] = (string) $value;
            } elseif ($key === 'CONTENT_TYPE' || $key === 'CONTENT_LENGTH') {
                $headers[strtolower(str_replace('_', '-', $key))] = (string) $value;
            }
        }

        return $headers;
    }
}
```

```php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace Tests\Unit\Http;

use Kanin\Http\Middleware;
use Kanin\Http\Request;
use Kanin\Http\Response;
use PHPUnit\Framework\TestCase;

final class MiddlewareTest extends TestCase
{
    public function test_middleware_interface_can_short_circuit_or_continue(): void
    {
        $middleware = new class implements Middleware {
            public function handle(Request $request, \Closure $next): Response
            {
                if ($request->header('X-Block') !== null) {
                    return new Response('blocked', 403);
                }

                return $next($request)->header('X-Seen', 'yes');
            }
        };
        $next = fn (Request $request): Response => new Response('ok');

        $passed = $middleware->handle(Request::create('/'), $next);
        $blocked = $middleware->handle(Request::create('/', 'GET', [], ['X-Block' => '1']), $next);

        $this->assertSame('ok', $passed->getContent());
        $this->assertSame('yes', $passed->getHeader('X-Seen'));
        $this->assertSame(403, $blocked->getStatusCode());
        $this->assertSame('blocked', $blocked->getContent());
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 23: Implement the Middleware interface**

Create `system/Http/Middleware.php`:

```php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace Kanin\Http;

/**
 * A middleware wraps the handling of a request, like a layer of an onion.
 *
 * It can inspect the request, stop early by returning its own Response,
 * or pass the request on by calling $next($request) and then adjust the
 * Response that comes back.
 */
interface Middleware
{
    /**
     * Handle the request, calling $next($request) to continue down the chain.
     */
    public function handle(Request $request, \Closure $next): Response;
}
```

Consumes (exact, from earlier tasks):
- Task 2 `Kanin\Container`: `make(string $abstract, array $parameters = []): mixed`, `call(callable|array $callback, array $parameters = []): mixed` (casts numeric-string named params to `int`/`float` for `int`/`float` params), `instance(string $abstract, mixed $instance): mixed`, `static getInstance(): static`, `static setInstance(?Container $container): void`.
- Task 4 `Kanin\Http\Request`: `static create(string $uri, string $method = 'GET', array $parameters = [], array $headers = [], ?string $content = null, array $server = []): static`, `method(): Method` (honours `_method` / `X-HTTP-Method-Override`, unknown method → `HttpException(405)`), `path(): string`, `input(?string $key = null, mixed $default = null): mixed`, `setRouteParameters(array $parameters): void`, `route(?string $key = null, mixed $default = null): mixed`.
- Task 4 `Kanin\Http\Method` enum (`Method::HEAD`, `->value`), `Kanin\Http\Middleware` interface (`handle(Request $request, \Closure $next): Response`).
- Task 4 `Kanin\Http\Response` (`__construct(string $content = '', int $status = 200, array $headers = [])`, `getContent()`, `getStatusCode()`), `Kanin\Http\JsonResponse` (`__construct(mixed $data = null, int $status = 200, array $headers = [])`, `getData()`).
- Task 4 `Kanin\Http\NotFoundHttpException` (`__construct(string $message = 'Not Found')`), `Kanin\Http\MethodNotAllowedHttpException` (`__construct(array $allowed)`, header `Allow`), `HttpException::getStatusCode()`, `HttpException::getHeaders()`.

Produces (later tasks rely on these exactly):
- `Kanin\Routing\Route`: `__construct(array $methods, string $uri, \Closure|array|string $action)`; `methods(): array`; `uri(): string`; `getAction(): \Closure|array|string`; `name(string $name): static`; `getName(): ?string`; `middleware(string|array $middleware): static`; `getMiddleware(): array`; `where(string|array $name, ?string $pattern = null): static`; `matches(string $path): ?array`; static facade `get/post/put/patch/delete(string $uri, \Closure|array|string $action): Route`, `match(array $methods, string $uri, \Closure|array|string $action): Route`, `any(string $uri, \Closure|array|string $action): Route`, `group(array $attributes, \Closure $routes): void`, `apiResource(string $name, string $controller, array $options = []): void` (all proxy to `Container::getInstance()->make(Router::class)`).
- `Kanin\Routing\Router`: `__construct(Container $container)`; `get/post/put/patch/delete(string $uri, \Closure|array|string $action): Route`; `match(array $methods, string $uri, \Closure|array|string $action): Route`; `any(string $uri, \Closure|array|string $action): Route`; `group(array $attributes, \Closure $routes): void`; `apiResource(string $name, string $controller, array $options = []): void`; `aliasMiddleware(string $alias, string $class): void`; `routes(): array`; `findRoute(Request $request): Route`; `dispatch(Request $request): Response`; `url(string $name, array $parameters = []): string`; `static toResponse(mixed $result): Response`. Used by Task 11 (`Application` boot/handle, `route()` helper), Task 12 (`kanin routes`), Task 13 (`routes/api.php`).
- `Kanin\Routing\Pipeline`: `__construct(Container $container)`; `send(Request $request): static`; `through(array $middleware): static`; `then(\Closure $destination): Response`. Used by Task 11 (`Application::handle`).
- Error messages later tasks/tests may assert: unknown route name → `\InvalidArgumentException('Route [x] is not defined.')`; missing URL param → `\InvalidArgumentException('Missing required parameter [id] for route [users.show].')`; unknown middleware alias → `\InvalidArgumentException('Middleware [x] is not an alias or a class.')`; non-middleware in a pipeline → `\InvalidArgumentException('Middleware [X] must implement Kanin\Http\Middleware.')`; unsupported action return → `\UnexpectedValueException('A route action returned an unsupported value of type [int].')`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the Pipeline test fixtures**

Create `tests/Unit/Routing/Fixtures/TraceMiddleware.php`:

```php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace Tests\Unit\Routing\Fixtures;

use Kanin\Http\Middleware;
use Kanin\Http\Request;
use Kanin\Http\Response;

/**
 * Test middleware that records when it runs, so tests can check the order.
 */
class TraceMiddleware implements Middleware
{
    /** @var list<string> */
    public static array $log = [];

    public function __construct(private string $name = 'trace')
    {
    }

    public function handle(Request $request, \Closure $next): Response
    {
        self::$log[] = $this->name . ':before';
        $response = $next($request);
        self::$log[] = $this->name . ':after';

        return $response;
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Write the failing Pipeline test**

Create `tests/Unit/Routing/PipelineTest.php`:

```php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace Tests\Unit\Routing;

use Kanin\Container;
use Kanin\Http\Request;
use Kanin\Http\Response;
use Kanin\Routing\Pipeline;
use PHPUnit\Framework\TestCase;
use Tests\Unit\Routing\Fixtures\BlockMiddleware;
use Tests\Unit\Routing\Fixtures\TraceMiddleware;

class PipelineTest extends TestCase
{
    protected function setUp(): void
    {
        TraceMiddleware::$log = [];
    }

    public function test_without_middleware_the_destination_receives_the_request(): void
    {
        $request = Request::create('/hello');

        $response = (new Pipeline(new Container()))
            ->send($request)
            ->through([])
            ->then(fn (Request $received) => new Response($received === $request ? 'same' : 'other'));

        $this->assertSame('same', $response->getContent());
    }

    public function test_first_middleware_runs_outermost(): void
    {
        $response = (new Pipeline(new Container()))
            ->send(Request::create('/'))
            ->through([new TraceMiddleware('first'), new TraceMiddleware('second')])
            ->then(function (): Response {
                TraceMiddleware::$log[] = 'destination';

                return new Response('done');
            });

        $this->assertSame('done', $response->getContent());
        $this->assertSame(
            ['first:before', 'second:before', 'destination', 'second:after', 'first:after'],
            TraceMiddleware::$log,
        );
    }

    public function test_class_names_are_built_by_the_container(): void
    {
        (new Pipeline(new Container()))
            ->send(Request::create('/'))
            ->through([TraceMiddleware::class])
            ->then(fn () => new Response());

        $this->assertSame(['trace:before', 'trace:after'], TraceMiddleware::$log);
    }

    public function test_middleware_can_stop_the_request(): void
    {
        $reached = false;

        $response = (new Pipeline(new Container()))
            ->send(Request::create('/'))
            ->through([BlockMiddleware::class, new TraceMiddleware('inner')])
            ->then(function () use (&$reached): Response {
                $reached = true;

                return new Response('ok');
            });

        $this->assertFalse($reached);
        $this->assertSame([], TraceMiddleware::$log);
        $this->assertSame(403, $response->getStatusCode());
        $this->assertSame('blocked', $response->getContent());
    }

    public function test_objects_that_are_not_middleware_are_refused(): void
    {
        $this->expectException(\InvalidArgumentException::class);
        $this->expectExceptionMessage('Middleware [stdClass] must implement Kanin\Http\Middleware.');

        (new Pipeline(new Container()))
            ->send(Request::create('/'))
            ->through([new \stdClass()])
            ->then(fn () => new Response());
    }

    public function test_class_names_that_are_not_middleware_are_refused(): void
    {
        $this->expectException(\InvalidArgumentException::class);
        $this->expectExceptionMessage('Middleware [ArrayObject] must implement Kanin\Http\Middleware.');

        (new Pipeline(new Container()))
            ->send(Request::create('/'))
            ->through([\ArrayObject::class])
            ->then(fn () => new Response());
    }

    public function test_then_without_send_is_refused(): void
    {
        $this->expectException(\LogicException::class);
        $this->expectExceptionMessage('Call send() with a request before then().');

        (new Pipeline(new Container()))->through([])->then(fn () => new Response());
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Implement Pipeline**

Create `system/Routing/Pipeline.php`:

```php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace Kanin\Routing;

use Kanin\Container;
use Kanin\Http\Middleware;
use Kanin\Http\Request;
use Kanin\Http\Response;

/**
 * Sends a request through a list of middleware, like layers of an onion:
 *
 *   (new Pipeline($container))
 *       ->send($request)
 *       ->through([First::class, Second::class])
 *       ->then(fn (Request $request) => new Response('Hello'));
 *
 * First runs outermost: First (before) -> Second (before) -> destination
 * -> Second (after) -> First (after). A middleware can also stop early by
 * returning a Response without calling $next.
 */
class Pipeline
{
    private ?Request $request = null;

    /** @var list<string|object> Class names or middleware objects. */
    private array $middleware = [];

    /**
     * The container is used to build middleware given as class names.
     */
    public function __construct(private Container $container)
    {
    }

    /**
     * Set the request that will travel through the middleware.
     */
    public function send(Request $request): static
    {
        $this->request = $request;

        return $this;
    }

    /**
     * Set the middleware: class names (built by the container) or objects.
     * The first one in the list runs first (outermost).
     */
    public function through(array $middleware): static
    {
        $this->middleware = array_values($middleware);

        return $this;
    }

    /**
     * Run the request through every middleware and finally into
     * $destination, returning the Response that comes back out.
     */
    public function then(\Closure $destination): Response
    {
        if ($this->request === null) {
            throw new \LogicException('Call send() with a request before then().');
        }

        // Wrap from the inside out, so the first middleware ends up outermost.
        $next = $destination;
        foreach (array_reverse($this->middleware) as $middleware) {
            $next = function (Request $request) use ($middleware, $next): Response {
                return $this->resolve($middleware)->handle($request, $next);
            };
        }

        return $next($this->request);
    }

    /**
     * Turn a class name into an object and check it really is middleware.
     */
    private function resolve(string|object $middleware): Middleware
    {
        $instance = is_string($middleware) ? $this->container->make($middleware) : $middleware;

        if (!$instance instanceof Middleware) {
            $name = is_string($middleware) ? $middleware : $middleware::class;

            throw new \InvalidArgumentException("Middleware [{$name}] must implement " . Middleware::class . '.');
        }

        return $instance;
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 9: Implement Route (value object + static facade)**

Create `system/Routing/Route.php`. The facade methods reference `Router`, which is created in Step 14; nothing in `RouteTest` calls them, so this step's tests do not need it.

```php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace Kanin\Routing;

use Kanin\Container;

/**
 * One route: "when a request with one of these HTTP methods hits this URI,
 * run this action".
 *
 * A URI may contain parameters:
 *   /users/{id}       {id} matches one path segment (anything except "/")
 *   /posts/{slug?}    a trailing "?" makes the parameter optional
 *
 * Route also works as a static "facade": Route::get('/users', ...) is a
 * shortcut for app(Router::class)->get('/users', ...).
 */
class Route
{
    /** The regex a parameter uses when where() did not give it one. */
    public const DEFAULT_PATTERN = '[^/]+';

    /** @var list<string> Uppercase HTTP methods, e.g. ['GET']. */
    private array $methods;

    private string $uri;

    private \Closure|array|string $action;

    private ?string $name = null;

    /** @var list<string> Middleware aliases or class names, in run order. */
    private array $middleware = [];

    /** @var array<string, string> Parameter name => regex. */
    private array $wheres = [];

    /**
     * Create a route. The URI is normalized: "users/" becomes "/users" and
     * "" becomes "/". Methods are stored in uppercase.
     */
    public function __construct(array $methods, string $uri, \Closure|array|string $action)
    {
        $this->methods = array_values(array_unique(array_map('strtoupper', $methods)));
        $this->uri = '/' . trim($uri, '/');
        $this->action = $action;
    }

    /** The HTTP methods this route answers to, e.g. ['PUT', 'PATCH']. */
    public function methods(): array
    {
        return $this->methods;
    }

    /** The normalized URI pattern, e.g. "/users/{id}". */
    public function uri(): string
    {
        return $this->uri;
    }

    /**
     * What runs when the route matches: a closure, [Controller::class, 'method']
     * or 'Controller@method'.
     */
    public function getAction(): \Closure|array|string
    {
        return $this->action;
    }

    /** Give the route a name so you can build its URL later with route('name'). */
    public function name(string $name): static
    {
        $this->name = $name;

        return $this;
    }

    /** The route's name, or null when it has none. */
    public function getName(): ?string
    {
        return $this->name;
    }

    /**
     * Add middleware (an alias like 'auth.token' or a class name). Calling it
     * again adds more; it never removes what is already there.
     */
    public function middleware(string|array $middleware): static
    {
        foreach ((array) $middleware as $item) {
            $this->middleware[] = $item;
        }

        return $this;
    }

    /** The route's middleware in the order it will run. */
    public function getMiddleware(): array
    {
        return $this->middleware;
    }

    /**
     * Restrict what a parameter may look like, using a regex:
     *   ->where('id', '[0-9]+')  or  ->where(['id' => '[0-9]+', 'slug' => '[a-z-]+'])
     */
    public function where(string|array $name, ?string $pattern = null): static
    {
        $patterns = is_array($name) ? $name : [$name => $pattern];

        foreach ($patterns as $parameter => $regex) {
            if (!is_string($regex) || $regex === '') {
                throw new \InvalidArgumentException("A pattern is required for route parameter [{$parameter}].");
            }

            $this->wheres[$parameter] = $regex;
        }

        return $this;
    }

    /**
     * Check a request path against this route. Returns the parameters it found
     * (e.g. ['id' => '5']), [] for a match without parameters, or null when
     * the path does not match. A missing optional parameter is left out.
     */
    public function matches(string $path): ?array
    {
        $path = rtrim($path, '/');

        if (preg_match($this->compile(), $path, $found, PREG_UNMATCHED_AS_NULL) !== 1) {
            return null;
        }

        $parameters = [];
        foreach ($found as $key => $value) {
            if (is_string($key) && $value !== null) {
                $parameters[$key] = $value;
            }
        }

        return $parameters;
    }

    /**
     * Turn the URI pattern into a regex, e.g. "/users/{id}" into
     * "#^/users/(?P<id>[^/]+)$#". The root "/" compiles to an empty string,
     * which is why matches() trims the trailing "/" from the path too.
     */
    private function compile(): string
    {
        // Split "/users/{id}" into ["/users", "/{id}"]: plain text and placeholders.
        $pieces = preg_split(
            '#(/?\{[A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_]*\??\})#',
            rtrim($this->uri, '/'),
            -1,
            PREG_SPLIT_DELIM_CAPTURE | PREG_SPLIT_NO_EMPTY,
        );

        $regex = '';
        foreach ($pieces as $piece) {
            if (!str_contains($piece, '{')) {
                $regex .= preg_quote($piece, '#');
                continue;
            }

            $slash = str_starts_with($piece, '/') ? '/' : '';
            $parameter = trim($piece, '/{?}');
            $pattern = str_replace('#', '\#', $this->wheres[$parameter] ?? self::DEFAULT_PATTERN);
            $group = $slash . '(?P<' . $parameter . '>' . $pattern . ')';

            // Optional: the whole "/value" part may be missing.
            $regex .= str_ends_with($piece, '?}') ? '(?:' . $group . ')?' : $group;
        }

        return '#^' . $regex . '$#';
    }

    /** Facade: register a GET route on the application's router. */
    public static function get(string $uri, \Closure|array|string $action): Route
    {
        return self::router()->get($uri, $action);
    }

    /** Facade: register a POST route on the application's router. */
    public static function post(string $uri, \Closure|array|string $action): Route
    {
        return self::router()->post($uri, $action);
    }

    /** Facade: register a PUT route on the application's router. */
    public static function put(string $uri, \Closure|array|string $action): Route
    {
        return self::router()->put($uri, $action);
    }

    /** Facade: register a PATCH route on the application's router. */
    public static function patch(string $uri, \Closure|array|string $action): Route
    {
        return self::router()->patch($uri, $action);
    }

    /** Facade: register a DELETE route on the application's router. */
    public static function delete(string $uri, \Closure|array|string $action): Route
    {
        return self::router()->delete($uri, $action);
    }

    /** Facade: register a route for several methods, e.g. ['PUT', 'PATCH']. */
    public static function match(array $methods, string $uri, \Closure|array|string $action): Route
    {
        return self::router()->match($methods, $uri, $action);
    }

    /** Facade: register a route for GET, POST, PUT, PATCH and DELETE. */
    public static function any(string $uri, \Closure|array|string $action): Route
    {
        return self::router()->any($uri, $action);
    }

    /** Facade: share a prefix and/or middleware between several routes. */
    public static function group(array $attributes, \Closure $routes): void
    {
        self::router()->group($attributes, $routes);
    }

    /** Facade: register the five REST routes for a controller. */
    public static function apiResource(string $name, string $controller, array $options = []): void
    {
        self::router()->apiResource($name, $controller, $options);
    }

    /** The router the facade talks to (the one stored in the container). */
    private static function router(): Router
    {
        return Container::getInstance()->make(Router::class);
    }
}
```

Create `tests/Unit/Routing/RouterTest.php`:

```php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace Tests\Unit\Routing;

use Kanin\Container;
use Kanin\Http\JsonResponse;
use Kanin\Http\MethodNotAllowedHttpException;
use Kanin\Http\NotFoundHttpException;
use Kanin\Http\Request;
use Kanin\Http\Response;
use Kanin\Routing\Route;
use Kanin\Routing\Router;
use PHPUnit\Framework\TestCase;
use Tests\Unit\Routing\Fixtures\BlockMiddleware;
use Tests\Unit\Routing\Fixtures\PostController;
use Tests\Unit\Routing\Fixtures\TraceMiddleware;

class RouterTest extends TestCase
{
    private Container $container;

    private Router $router;

    protected function setUp(): void
    {
        TraceMiddleware::$log = [];
        $this->container = new Container();
        $this->router = new Router($this->container);
    }

    private function send(string $method, string $uri, array $parameters = []): Response
    {
        return $this->router->dispatch(Request::create($uri, $method, $parameters));
    }

    public function test_verb_methods_register_routes_with_the_right_methods(): void
    {
        $action = fn () => null;

        $this->assertSame(['GET'], $this->router->get('/a', $action)->methods());
        $this->assertSame(['POST'], $this->router->post('/a', $action)->methods());
        $this->assertSame(['PUT'], $this->router->put('/a', $action)->methods());
        $this->assertSame(['PATCH'], $this->router->patch('/a', $action)->methods());
        $this->assertSame(['DELETE'], $this->router->delete('/a', $action)->methods());
        $this->assertSame(['PUT', 'PATCH'], $this->router->match(['put', 'patch'], '/a', $action)->methods());
        $this->assertSame(['GET', 'POST', 'PUT', 'PATCH', 'DELETE'], $this->router->any('/a', $action)->methods());
        $this->assertCount(7, $this->router->routes());
        $this->assertContainsOnlyInstancesOf(Route::class, $this->router->routes());
    }

    public function test_it_dispatches_a_closure_and_converts_arrays_to_json(): void
    {
        $this->router->get('/hello', fn () => ['message' => 'hi']);

        $response = $this->send('GET', '/hello');

        $this->assertInstanceOf(JsonResponse::class, $response);
        $this->assertSame(200, $response->getStatusCode());
        $this->assertSame(['message' => 'hi'], $response->getData());
    }

    public function test_route_parameters_are_injected_and_cast(): void
    {
        $this->router->get('/users/{id}', fn (int $id) => ['id' => $id, 'type' => get_debug_type($id)]);

        $this->assertSame(['id' => 5, 'type' => 'int'], $this->send('GET', '/users/5')->getData());
    }

    public function test_route_parameters_are_stored_on_the_request(): void
    {
        $this->router->get('/users/{id}', fn (Request $request) => ['id' => $request->route('id')]);

        $request = Request::create('/users/9');
        $response = $this->router->dispatch($request);

        $this->assertSame(['id' => '9'], $response->getData());
        $this->assertSame(['id' => '9'], $request->route());
        $this->assertSame($request, $this->container->make(Request::class));
    }

    public function test_missing_optional_parameter_uses_the_default(): void
    {
        $this->router->get('/greet/{name?}', [PostController::class, 'greet']);

        $this->assertSame('Hello, guest', $this->send('GET', '/greet')->getContent());
        $this->assertSame('Hello, Ada', $this->send('GET', '/greet/Ada')->getContent());
    }

    public function test_where_constraint_failure_is_a_404(): void
    {
        $this->router->get('/users/{id}', fn () => 'found')->where('id', '[0-9]+');

        $this->expectException(NotFoundHttpException::class);
        $this->expectExceptionMessage('Not Found');

        $this->send('GET', '/users/abc');
    }

    public function test_unknown_path_is_a_404(): void
    {
        $this->router->get('/users', fn () => 'users');

        try {
            $this->send('GET', '/nope');
            $this->fail('Expected a NotFoundHttpException.');
        } catch (NotFoundHttpException $e) {
            $this->assertSame(404, $e->getStatusCode());
            $this->assertSame('Not Found', $e->getMessage());
        }
    }

    public function test_known_path_with_wrong_method_is_a_405_listing_allowed_methods(): void
    {
        $this->router->get('/users', fn () => 'list');
        $this->router->post('/users', fn () => 'create');
        $this->router->get('/users/{id}', fn () => 'show');

        try {
            $this->send('DELETE', '/users');
            $this->fail('Expected a MethodNotAllowedHttpException.');
        } catch (MethodNotAllowedHttpException $e) {
            $this->assertSame(405, $e->getStatusCode());
            $this->assertSame('Method Not Allowed', $e->getMessage());
            $this->assertSame(['Allow' => 'GET, POST'], $e->getHeaders());
        }
    }

    public function test_head_requests_use_get_routes(): void
    {
        $this->router->get('/ping', fn () => 'pong');

        $this->assertSame('pong', $this->send('HEAD', '/ping')->getContent());
    }

    public function test_method_override_reaches_put_routes(): void
    {
        $this->router->put('/posts/{id}', fn (int $id) => "updated {$id}");

        $this->assertSame('updated 3', $this->send('POST', '/posts/3', ['_method' => 'PUT'])->getContent());
    }

    public function test_first_matching_route_wins(): void
    {
        $this->router->get('/users/me', fn () => 'me');
        $this->router->get('/users/{id}', fn () => 'by id');

        $this->assertSame('me', $this->send('GET', '/users/me')->getContent());
        $this->assertSame('by id', $this->send('GET', '/users/4')->getContent());
    }

    public function test_array_and_at_sign_controller_actions(): void
    {
        $this->router->get('/a/{id}', [PostController::class, 'show']);
        $this->router->get('/b/{id}', PostController::class . '@show');

        $this->assertSame(['action' => 'show', 'id' => 1], $this->send('GET', '/a/1')->getData());
        $this->assertSame(['action' => 'show', 'id' => 2], $this->send('GET', '/b/2')->getData());
    }

    public function test_action_string_without_at_sign_is_refused(): void
    {
        $this->router->get('/x', 'PostController');

        $this->expectException(\InvalidArgumentException::class);
        $this->expectExceptionMessage('Route action [PostController] must look like [Controller@method].');

        $this->send('GET', '/x');
    }

    public function test_malformed_action_array_is_refused(): void
    {
        $this->router->get('/x', [PostController::class]);

        $this->expectException(\InvalidArgumentException::class);
        $this->expectExceptionMessage("Route action arrays must look like [Controller::class, 'method'].");

        $this->send('GET', '/x');
    }

    public function test_unknown_controller_is_refused(): void
    {
        $this->router->get('/x', 'Missing\\Controller@index');

        $this->expectException(\InvalidArgumentException::class);
        $this->expectExceptionMessage('Controller [Missing\\Controller] does not exist.');

        $this->send('GET', '/x');
    }

    public function test_unknown_controller_method_is_refused(): void
    {
        $this->router->get('/x', [PostController::class, 'missing']);

        $this->expectException(\InvalidArgumentException::class);
        $this->expectExceptionMessage('Method [' . PostController::class . '::missing] does not exist or is not public.');

        $this->send('GET', '/x');
    }

    public function test_private_controller_method_is_refused(): void
    {
        $this->router->get('/x', [PostController::class, 'secret']);

        $this->expectException(\InvalidArgumentException::class);
        $this->expectExceptionMessage('Method [' . PostController::class . '::secret] does not exist or is not public.');

        $this->send('GET', '/x');
    }

    public function test_group_prefixes_nest(): void
    {
        $this->router->group(['prefix' => 'api'], function (Router $router): void {
            $router->get('/', fn () => 'api root');
            $router->group(['prefix' => '/v1/'], function (Router $router): void {
                $router->get('users', fn () => 'v1 users');
            });
            $router->get('status', fn () => 'status');
        });
        $this->router->get('/outside', fn () => 'outside');

        $uris = array_map(fn (Route $route) => $route->uri(), $this->router->routes());

        $this->assertSame(['/api', '/api/v1/users', '/api/status', '/outside'], $uris);
        $this->assertSame('v1 users', $this->send('GET', '/api/v1/users')->getContent());
    }

    public function test_group_closure_receives_the_router(): void
    {
        $received = null;
        $this->router->group([], function (Router $router) use (&$received): void {
            $received = $router;
        });

        $this->assertSame($this->router, $received);
    }

    public function test_group_middleware_runs_before_route_middleware(): void
    {
        $this->router->group(['middleware' => 'outer'], function (Router $router): void {
            $router->group(['middleware' => ['inner']], function (Router $router): void {
                $router->get('/x', fn () => 'x')->middleware('route');
            });
        });

        $this->assertSame(['outer', 'inner', 'route'], $this->router->routes()[0]->getMiddleware());
    }

    public function test_group_state_is_restored_after_an_exception(): void
    {
        try {
            $this->router->group(['prefix' => 'broken', 'middleware' => 'm'], function (): void {
                throw new \RuntimeException('boom');
            });
        } catch (\RuntimeException) {
        }

        $route = $this->router->get('/after', fn () => null);

        $this->assertSame('/after', $route->uri());
        $this->assertSame([], $route->getMiddleware());
    }

    public function test_route_middleware_aliases_are_resolved(): void
    {
        $this->router->aliasMiddleware('block', BlockMiddleware::class);
        $this->router->get('/secret', fn () => 'secret')->middleware('block');

        $response = $this->send('GET', '/secret');

        $this->assertSame(403, $response->getStatusCode());
        $this->assertSame('blocked', $response->getContent());
    }

    public function test_route_middleware_may_be_a_class_name_and_runs_in_order(): void
    {
        $this->router->group(['middleware' => TraceMiddleware::class], function (Router $router): void {
            $router->get('/x', function (): string {
                TraceMiddleware::$log[] = 'action';

                return 'x';
            });
        });

        $this->assertSame('x', $this->send('GET', '/x')->getContent());
        $this->assertSame(['trace:before', 'action', 'trace:after'], TraceMiddleware::$log);
    }

    public function test_unknown_middleware_alias_is_refused(): void
    {
        $this->router->get('/x', fn () => 'x')->middleware('nope');

        $this->expectException(\InvalidArgumentException::class);
        $this->expectExceptionMessage('Middleware [nope] is not an alias or a class.');

        $this->send('GET', '/x');
    }

    public function test_api_resource_registers_five_named_routes(): void
    {
        $this->router->apiResource('posts', PostController::class);

        $summary = array_map(
            fn (Route $route) => [implode('|', $route->methods()), $route->uri(), $route->getName()],
            $this->router->routes(),
        );

        $this->assertSame([
            ['GET', '/posts', 'posts.index'],
            ['POST', '/posts', 'posts.store'],
            ['GET', '/posts/{id}', 'posts.show'],
            ['PUT|PATCH', '/posts/{id}', 'posts.update'],
            ['DELETE', '/posts/{id}', 'posts.destroy'],
        ], $summary);
    }

    public function test_api_resource_routes_dispatch_to_the_controller(): void
    {
        $this->router->apiResource('posts', PostController::class);

        $this->assertSame(['action' => 'index'], $this->send('GET', '/posts')->getData());

        $store = $this->send('POST', '/posts', ['title' => 'Hi']);
        $this->assertSame(201, $store->getStatusCode());
        $this->assertSame(['action' => 'store', 'title' => 'Hi'], $store->getData());

        $this->assertSame(['action' => 'show', 'id' => 4], $this->send('GET', '/posts/4')->getData());
        $this->assertSame(['action' => 'update', 'id' => 4, 'method' => 'PUT'], $this->send('PUT', '/posts/4')->getData());
        $this->assertSame(['action' => 'update', 'id' => 4, 'method' => 'PATCH'], $this->send('PATCH', '/posts/4')->getData());

        $destroy = $this->send('DELETE', '/posts/4');
        $this->assertSame(204, $destroy->getStatusCode());
        $this->assertSame('', $destroy->getContent());
    }

    public function test_api_resource_id_must_be_numeric(): void
    {
        $this->router->apiResource('posts', PostController::class);

        $this->expectException(NotFoundHttpException::class);

        $this->send('GET', '/posts/abc');
    }

    public function test_api_resource_only_option(): void
    {
        $this->router->apiResource('posts', PostController::class, ['only' => ['index', 'show']]);

        $names = array_map(fn (Route $route) => $route->getName(), $this->router->routes());
        $this->assertSame(['posts.index', 'posts.show'], $names);

        try {
            $this->send('POST', '/posts');
            $this->fail('Expected a MethodNotAllowedHttpException.');
        } catch (MethodNotAllowedHttpException $e) {
            $this->assertSame(['Allow' => 'GET'], $e->getHeaders());
        }
    }

    public function test_api_resource_middleware_option_applies_to_every_route(): void
    {
        $this->router->apiResource('posts', PostController::class, ['middleware' => 'auth.token']);

        foreach ($this->router->routes() as $route) {
            $this->assertSame(['auth.token'], $route->getMiddleware());
        }
    }

    public function test_api_resource_inside_a_group_gets_prefix_and_group_middleware(): void
    {
        $this->router->group(['prefix' => 'api', 'middleware' => 'outer'], function (Router $router): void {
            $router->apiResource('posts', PostController::class, ['only' => ['store'], 'middleware' => ['inner']]);
        });

        $route = $this->router->routes()[0];
        $this->assertSame('/api/posts', $route->uri());
        $this->assertSame('posts.store', $route->getName());
        $this->assertSame(['outer', 'inner'], $route->getMiddleware());
    }

    public function test_api_resource_unknown_action_is_refused(): void
    {
        $this->expectException(\InvalidArgumentException::class);
        $this->expectExceptionMessage('Unknown resource action(s) [shwo].');

        $this->router->apiResource('posts', PostController::class, ['only' => ['index', 'shwo']]);
    }

    public function test_url_builds_paths_for_named_routes(): void
    {
        $this->router->get('/', fn () => null)->name('home');
        $this->router->get('/users/{id}', fn () => null)->name('users.show');
        $this->router->get('/posts/{slug?}', fn () => null)->name('posts');

        $this->assertSame('/', $this->router->url('home'));
        $this->assertSame('/users/5', $this->router->url('users.show', ['id' => 5]));
        $this->assertSame('/posts', $this->router->url('posts'));
        $this->assertSame('/posts/intro', $this->router->url('posts', ['slug' => 'intro']));
    }

    public function test_url_encodes_values_and_appends_leftovers_as_query_string(): void
    {
        $this->router->get('/users/{name}', fn () => null)->name('users.show');

        $this->assertSame(
            '/users/Ada%20Lovelace?page=2&sort=name',
            $this->router->url('users.show', ['name' => 'Ada Lovelace', 'page' => 2, 'sort' => 'name']),
        );
    }

    public function test_url_includes_group_prefix(): void
    {
        $this->router->group(['prefix' => 'api'], function (Router $router): void {
            $router->apiResource('users', PostController::class);
        });

        $this->assertSame('/api/users/3', $this->router->url('users.show', ['id' => 3]));
        $this->assertSame('/api/users', $this->router->url('users.index'));
    }

    public function test_url_for_unknown_name_is_refused(): void
    {
        $this->expectException(\InvalidArgumentException::class);
        $this->expectExceptionMessage('Route [missing] is not defined.');

        $this->router->url('missing');
    }

    public function test_url_without_a_required_parameter_is_refused(): void
    {
        $this->router->get('/users/{id}', fn () => null)->name('users.show');

        $this->expectException(\InvalidArgumentException::class);
        $this->expectExceptionMessage('Missing required parameter [id] for route [users.show].');

        $this->router->url('users.show');
    }

    public function test_url_with_a_non_scalar_parameter_is_refused(): void
    {
        $this->router->get('/users/{id}', fn () => null)->name('users.show');

        $this->expectException(\InvalidArgumentException::class);
        $this->expectExceptionMessage('Route parameter [id] must be a string or number.');

        $this->router->url('users.show', ['id' => [1, 2]]);
    }

    public function test_to_response_returns_responses_unchanged(): void
    {
        $response = new Response('hi', 202);

        $this->assertSame($response, Router::toResponse($response));
    }

    public function test_to_response_converts_arrays_and_json_serializable_to_json(): void
    {
        $fromArray = Router::toResponse(['a' => 1]);
        $fromObject = Router::toResponse(new class () implements \JsonSerializable {
            public function jsonSerialize(): array
            {
                return ['b' => 2];
            }
        });

        $this->assertInstanceOf(JsonResponse::class, $fromArray);
        $this->assertSame(['a' => 1], $fromArray->getData());
        $this->assertInstanceOf(JsonResponse::class, $fromObject);
        $this->assertSame(['b' => 2], $fromObject->getData());
    }

    public function test_to_response_converts_strings_and_stringables_to_text(): void
    {
        $stringable = new class () implements \Stringable {
            public function __toString(): string
            {
                return 'from object';
            }
        };

        $fromString = Router::toResponse('plain');
        $fromObject = Router::toResponse($stringable);

        $this->assertNotInstanceOf(JsonResponse::class, $fromString);
        $this->assertSame('plain', $fromString->getContent());
        $this->assertSame(200, $fromString->getStatusCode());
        $this->assertSame('from object', $fromObject->getContent());
    }

    public function test_to_response_converts_null_to_204(): void
    {
        $response = Router::toResponse(null);

        $this->assertSame(204, $response->getStatusCode());
        $this->assertSame('', $response->getContent());
    }

    public function test_to_response_refuses_other_values(): void
    {
        $this->expectException(\UnexpectedValueException::class);
        $this->expectExceptionMessage('A route action returned an unsupported value of type [int].');

        Router::toResponse(42);
    }
}
```
