```php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace Tests\Unit\Routing;

use Kanin\Container;
use Kanin\Routing\Route;
use Kanin\Routing\Router;
use PHPUnit\Framework\TestCase;
use Tests\Unit\Routing\Fixtures\PostController;

class RouteFacadeTest extends TestCase
{
    private Router $router;

    protected function setUp(): void
    {
        $container = new Container();
        $this->router = new Router($container);
        $container->instance(Router::class, $this->router);
        Container::setInstance($container);
    }

    protected function tearDown(): void
    {
        Container::setInstance(null);
    }

    public function test_verb_methods_register_on_the_container_router(): void
    {
        $action = fn () => null;

        $get = Route::get('/a', $action);
        Route::post('/b', $action);
        Route::put('/c', $action);
        Route::patch('/d', $action);
        Route::delete('/e', $action);
        Route::match(['PUT', 'PATCH'], '/f', $action);
        Route::any('/g', $action);

        $summary = array_map(
            fn (Route $route) => implode('|', $route->methods()) . ' ' . $route->uri(),
            $this->router->routes(),
        );

        $this->assertSame($get, $this->router->routes()[0]);
        $this->assertSame([
            'GET /a',
            'POST /b',
            'PUT /c',
            'PATCH /d',
            'DELETE /e',
            'PUT|PATCH /f',
            'GET|POST|PUT|PATCH|DELETE /g',
        ], $summary);
    }

    public function test_group_and_api_resource_go_through_the_facade(): void
    {
        Route::group(['prefix' => 'api', 'middleware' => 'auth.token'], function (): void {
            Route::apiResource('posts', PostController::class, ['only' => ['index']]);
        });

        $route = $this->router->routes()[0];

        $this->assertSame('/api/posts', $route->uri());
        $this->assertSame('posts.index', $route->getName());
        $this->assertSame(['auth.token'], $route->getMiddleware());
    }
}
```

Create `system/Routing/UrlGenerator.php`:

```php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace Kanin\Routing;

/**
 * Builds the path of a route from its URI pattern. Used by Router::url()
 * and the route() helper:
 *
 *   "/users/{id}"     + ['id' => 5]               -> "/users/5"
 *   "/posts/{slug?}"  + []                         -> "/posts"
 *   "/users/{id}"     + ['id' => 5, 'page' => 2]   -> "/users/5?page=2"
 */
final class UrlGenerator
{
    /**
     * Fill in the route's {parameters}. Values are URL-encoded; parameters
     * the URI does not use are added as a query string.
     */
    public static function path(Route $route, array $parameters = []): string
    {
        $name = $route->getName() ?? $route->uri();

        $path = preg_replace_callback(
            '#(/?)\{([A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_]*)(\?)?\}#',
            function (array $match) use (&$parameters, $name): string {
                [, $slash, $key] = $match;
                $optional = ($match[3] ?? '') === '?';

                if (!isset($parameters[$key])) {
                    if ($optional) {
                        return '';
                    }

                    throw new \InvalidArgumentException("Missing required parameter [{$key}] for route [{$name}].");
                }

                if (!is_scalar($parameters[$key])) {
                    throw new \InvalidArgumentException("Route parameter [{$key}] must be a string or number.");
                }

                $value = rawurlencode((string) $parameters[$key]);
                unset($parameters[$key]);

                return $slash . $value;
            },
            $route->uri(),
        );

        $path = $path === '' ? '/' : $path;

        return $parameters === [] ? $path : $path . '?' . http_build_query($parameters);
    }
}
```

Create `system/Routing/Router.php`:

```php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace Kanin\Routing;

use Kanin\Container;
use Kanin\Http\JsonResponse;
use Kanin\Http\Method;
use Kanin\Http\MethodNotAllowedHttpException;
use Kanin\Http\NotFoundHttpException;
use Kanin\Http\Request;
use Kanin\Http\Response;

/**
 * Keeps the list of routes and turns a Request into a Response:
 * find the matching route, run its middleware, call its action and
 * convert whatever the action returned into a Response.
 */
class Router
{
    /** The actions apiResource() knows about, in registration order. */
    private const RESOURCE_ACTIONS = ['index', 'store', 'show', 'update', 'destroy'];

    /** @var list<Route> */
    private array $routes = [];

    /** @var list<array{prefix: string, middleware: list<string>}> Open group() calls. */
    private array $groupStack = [];

    /** @var array<string, string> Alias => middleware class name. */
    private array $middlewareAliases = [];

    /** The container builds controllers and middleware and calls actions. */
    public function __construct(private Container $container)
    {
    }

    /** Register a route that answers GET requests (and HEAD requests). */
    public function get(string $uri, \Closure|array|string $action): Route
    {
        return $this->match(['GET'], $uri, $action);
    }

    /** Register a route that answers POST requests. */
    public function post(string $uri, \Closure|array|string $action): Route
    {
        return $this->match(['POST'], $uri, $action);
    }

    /** Register a route that answers PUT requests. */
    public function put(string $uri, \Closure|array|string $action): Route
    {
        return $this->match(['PUT'], $uri, $action);
    }

    /** Register a route that answers PATCH requests. */
    public function patch(string $uri, \Closure|array|string $action): Route
    {
        return $this->match(['PATCH'], $uri, $action);
    }

    /** Register a route that answers DELETE requests. */
    public function delete(string $uri, \Closure|array|string $action): Route
    {
        return $this->match(['DELETE'], $uri, $action);
    }

    /**
     * Register a route for several methods at once, e.g. ['PUT', 'PATCH'].
     * The prefix and middleware of any surrounding group() are applied.
     */
    public function match(array $methods, string $uri, \Closure|array|string $action): Route
    {
        $group = $this->currentGroup();

        $route = new Route($methods, $group['prefix'] . '/' . trim($uri, '/'), $action);
        $route->middleware($group['middleware']);

        $this->routes[] = $route;

        return $route;
    }

    /** Register a route for GET, POST, PUT, PATCH and DELETE. */
    public function any(string $uri, \Closure|array|string $action): Route
    {
        return $this->match(['GET', 'POST', 'PUT', 'PATCH', 'DELETE'], $uri, $action);
    }

    /**
     * Share attributes between routes. Supported keys:
     *   'prefix'     => 'admin'               (URI prefix, nests: "api/admin")
     *   'middleware' => 'auth.token' or [...]  (runs before the routes' own)
     * The closure receives this router.
     */
    public function group(array $attributes, \Closure $routes): void
    {
        $parent = $this->currentGroup();
        $prefix = trim((string) ($attributes['prefix'] ?? ''), '/');

        $this->groupStack[] = [
            'prefix' => trim($parent['prefix'] . '/' . $prefix, '/'),
            'middleware' => [...$parent['middleware'], ...(array) ($attributes['middleware'] ?? [])],
        ];

        try {
            $routes($this);
        } finally {
            array_pop($this->groupStack);
        }
    }

    /**
     * Register the five REST routes of a controller:
     *   GET /{name} index, POST /{name} store, GET /{name}/{id} show,
     *   PUT|PATCH /{name}/{id} update, DELETE /{name}/{id} destroy.
     * Routes are named "{name}.index" etc. and {id} must be digits.
     * Options: 'only' => ['index', 'show'], 'middleware' => 'auth.token'.
     */
    public function apiResource(string $name, string $controller, array $options = []): void
    {
        $only = $options['only'] ?? self::RESOURCE_ACTIONS;
        $unknown = array_diff($only, self::RESOURCE_ACTIONS);
        if ($unknown !== []) {
            throw new \InvalidArgumentException('Unknown resource action(s) [' . implode(', ', $unknown) . '].');
        }

        $base = '/' . trim($name, '/');
        $definitions = [
            'index' => [['GET'], $base],
            'store' => [['POST'], $base],
            'show' => [['GET'], $base . '/{id}'],
            'update' => [['PUT', 'PATCH'], $base . '/{id}'],
            'destroy' => [['DELETE'], $base . '/{id}'],
        ];

        foreach ($definitions as $action => [$methods, $uri]) {
            if (!in_array($action, $only, true)) {
                continue;
            }

            $this->match($methods, $uri, [$controller, $action])
                ->name(str_replace('/', '.', trim($name, '/')) . '.' . $action)
                ->where('id', '[0-9]+')
                ->middleware($options['middleware'] ?? []);
        }
    }

    /** Give a middleware class a short name, e.g. 'auth.token'. */
    public function aliasMiddleware(string $alias, string $class): void
    {
        $this->middlewareAliases[$alias] = $class;
    }

    /** Every registered route, in registration order. */
    public function routes(): array
    {
        return $this->routes;
    }

    /**
     * Find the route for a request. HEAD requests use GET routes.
     * Throws a 405 when the path exists for other methods, else a 404.
     */
    public function findRoute(Request $request): Route
    {
        $method = $request->method();
        $path = $request->path();
        $allowed = [];

        foreach ($this->routes as $route) {
            if ($route->matches($path) === null) {
                continue;
            }

            $methods = $route->methods();
            if (in_array($method->value, $methods, true) || ($method === Method::HEAD && in_array('GET', $methods, true))) {
                return $route;
            }

            array_push($allowed, ...$methods);
        }

        if ($allowed !== []) {
            throw new MethodNotAllowedHttpException(array_values(array_unique($allowed)));
        }

        throw new NotFoundHttpException();
    }

    /**
     * Handle a request: find its route, store the route parameters on the
     * request, run the route middleware, then call the action.
     */
    public function dispatch(Request $request): Response
    {
        $route = $this->findRoute($request);
        $parameters = $route->matches($request->path()) ?? [];

        $request->setRouteParameters($parameters);
        $this->container->instance(Request::class, $request);

        return (new Pipeline($this->container))
            ->send($request)
            ->through($this->resolveMiddleware($route->getMiddleware()))
            ->then(function (Request $request) use ($route, $parameters): Response {
                $this->container->instance(Request::class, $request);
                $action = $this->resolveAction($route->getAction());

                return static::toResponse($this->container->call($action, $parameters));
            });
    }

    /**
     * Build the path of a named route, e.g. url('users.show', ['id' => 5])
     * gives "/users/5". Extra parameters become the query string.
     */
    public function url(string $name, array $parameters = []): string
    {
        return UrlGenerator::path($this->findByName($name), $parameters);
    }

    /**
     * Convert what an action returned into a Response:
     * Response as-is, array/JsonSerializable as JSON, string/Stringable as
     * text, null as an empty 204 response.
     */
    public static function toResponse(mixed $result): Response
    {
        return match (true) {
            $result instanceof Response => $result,
            is_array($result), $result instanceof \JsonSerializable => new JsonResponse($result),
            is_string($result), $result instanceof \Stringable => new Response((string) $result),
            $result === null => new Response('', 204),
            default => throw new \UnexpectedValueException(
                'A route action returned an unsupported value of type [' . get_debug_type($result) . '].',
            ),
        };
    }

    /** The innermost open group, or an empty one outside any group. */
    private function currentGroup(): array
    {
        return $this->groupStack === []
            ? ['prefix' => '', 'middleware' => []]
            : $this->groupStack[array_key_last($this->groupStack)];
    }

    /** The first route registered with this name. */
    private function findByName(string $name): Route
    {
        foreach ($this->routes as $route) {
            if ($route->getName() === $name) {
                return $route;
            }
        }

        throw new \InvalidArgumentException("Route [{$name}] is not defined.");
    }

    /** Replace aliases such as 'auth.token' with their class names. */
    private function resolveMiddleware(array $middleware): array
    {
        return array_map(function (string $name): string {
            if (isset($this->middlewareAliases[$name])) {
                return $this->middlewareAliases[$name];
            }

            if (!class_exists($name)) {
                throw new \InvalidArgumentException("Middleware [{$name}] is not an alias or a class.");
            }

            return $name;
        }, $middleware);
    }

    /**
     * Turn 'Controller@method' or [Controller::class, 'method'] into a
     * callable the container can call. Only public methods are allowed.
     */
    private function resolveAction(\Closure|array|string $action): \Closure|array
    {
        if ($action instanceof \Closure) {
            return $action;
        }

        if (is_string($action)) {
            if (substr_count($action, '@') !== 1) {
                throw new \InvalidArgumentException("Route action [{$action}] must look like [Controller@method].");
            }

            $action = explode('@', $action);
        }

        if (!array_is_list($action) || count($action) !== 2 || !is_string($action[0]) || !is_string($action[1])) {
            throw new \InvalidArgumentException('Route action arrays must look like [Controller::class, \'method\'].');
        }

        [$class, $method] = $action;
        if (!class_exists($class)) {
            throw new \InvalidArgumentException("Controller [{$class}] does not exist.");
        }

        if (!method_exists($class, $method) || !(new \ReflectionMethod($class, $method))->isPublic()) {
            throw new \InvalidArgumentException("Method [{$class}::{$method}] does not exist or is not public.");
        }

        return [$class, $method];
    }
}
```

- `Kanin\Http\Response::__construct(string $content = '', int $status = 200, array $headers = [])`, `Response::header(string $name, string $value): static`, `Response::getHeader(string $name): ?string`, `getContent(): string`, `getStatusCode(): int` (Task 4)

```php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace Tests\Unit\Middleware;

use Kanin\Http\Request;
use Kanin\Http\Response;
use Kanin\Middleware\HandleCors;
use Kanin\Support\Config;
use PHPUnit\Framework\TestCase;

final class HandleCorsTest extends TestCase
{
    private int $nextCalls = 0;

    /**
     * Build the middleware with the defaults from config/cors.php, overriding some keys.
     */
    private function cors(array $overrides = []): HandleCors
    {
        $defaults = [
            'allowed_origins' => ['*'],
            'allowed_methods' => ['GET', 'POST', 'PUT', 'PATCH', 'DELETE', 'OPTIONS'],
            'allowed_headers' => ['Content-Type', 'Authorization', 'Accept', 'X-Requested-With'],
            'exposed_headers' => [],
            'max_age' => 600,
            'supports_credentials' => false,
        ];

        return new HandleCors(new Config(['cors' => array_replace($defaults, $overrides)]));
    }

    /**
     * A "next" closure that records the call and returns the given response.
     */
    private function next(?Response $response = null): \Closure
    {
        $response ??= new Response('ok', 200);

        return function (Request $request) use ($response): Response {
            $this->nextCalls++;

            return $response;
        };
    }

    private function preflight(string $origin, array $extraHeaders = []): Request
    {
        return Request::create('/api/users', 'OPTIONS', [], array_merge([
            'Origin' => $origin,
            'Access-Control-Request-Method' => 'POST',
        ], $extraHeaders));
    }

    {

        $this->assertSame($original, $response);

        $this->assertSame('https://app.example.com', $response->getHeader('Access-Control-Allow-Origin'));

    public function test_preflight_from_disallowed_origin_returns_bare_204(): void
    {
        $middleware = $this->cors(['allowed_origins' => ['https://app.example.com']]);

        $response = $middleware->handle($this->preflight('https://evil.example.com'), $this->next());

        $this->assertSame(0, $this->nextCalls);
        $this->assertSame(204, $response->getStatusCode());
        $this->assertNull($response->getHeader('Access-Control-Allow-Origin'));
        $this->assertNull($response->getHeader('Access-Control-Allow-Methods'));
        $this->assertNull($response->getHeader('Access-Control-Allow-Headers'));
        $this->assertNull($response->getHeader('Access-Control-Max-Age'));

    public function test_options_without_request_method_header_is_not_a_preflight(): void
    {
        $request = Request::create('/api/users', 'OPTIONS', [], ['Origin' => 'https://app.example.com']);

        $response = $this->cors()->handle($request, $this->next());

        $this->assertSame('*', $response->getHeader('Access-Control-Allow-Origin'));
        $this->assertNull($response->getHeader('Access-Control-Allow-Methods'));
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement HandleCors**

Create `system/Middleware/HandleCors.php`:

```php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace Kanin\Middleware;

use Closure;
use Kanin\Http\Method;
use Kanin\Http\Middleware;
use Kanin\Http\Request;
use Kanin\Http\Response;
use Kanin\Support\Config;

/**
 * Adds CORS (Cross-Origin Resource Sharing) headers so that JavaScript
 * running on another domain is allowed to call your API from a browser.
 *
 * Every setting lives in config/cors.php. Requests that carry no "Origin"
 * header (curl, Postman, server-to-server calls) are passed through untouched,
 * because CORS is a browser-only concept.
 */
class HandleCors implements Middleware
{
    /**
     * Create the middleware.
     *
     * @param Config $config The application configuration; the "cors.*" keys are read.
     */
    public function __construct(private readonly Config $config)
    {
    }

    /**
     * Answer a browser "preflight" request directly, or add CORS headers to
     * the normal response produced by the rest of the application.
     *
     * @param Request $request The incoming request.
     * @param Closure $next    Calls the next middleware (and finally your route).
     */
    public function handle(Request $request, Closure $next): Response
    {
        $origin = $request->header('Origin');

        if ($origin === null || $origin === '') {
            return $next($request);
        }

        $allowed = $this->isAllowedOrigin($origin);

        if ($this->isPreflight($request)) {
            // A preflight never reaches the router: we answer it right here.
            $response = new Response('', 204);

            return $allowed ? $this->addPreflightHeaders($request, $response, $origin) : $response;
        }

        $response = $next($request);

        return $allowed ? $this->addActualRequestHeaders($response, $origin) : $response;
    }

    /**
     * A preflight is an OPTIONS request that asks "may I send method X?".
     */
    private function isPreflight(Request $request): bool
    {
        return $request->method() === Method::OPTIONS
            && $request->header('Access-Control-Request-Method') !== null;
    }

    /**
     * The origin is allowed when the list contains "*" or the exact origin.
     */
    private function isAllowedOrigin(string $origin): bool
    {
        $origins = $this->list('allowed_origins');

        return in_array('*', $origins, true) || in_array($origin, $origins, true);
    }

    /**
     * Headers sent back on a successful preflight.
     */
    private function addPreflightHeaders(Request $request, Response $response, string $origin): Response
    {
        $this->addOriginHeaders($response, $origin);
        $this->headerIfNotEmpty($response, 'Access-Control-Allow-Methods', implode(', ', $this->list('allowed_methods')));
        $this->headerIfNotEmpty($response, 'Access-Control-Allow-Headers', $this->allowedHeadersFor($request));
        $response->header('Access-Control-Max-Age', (string) $this->maxAge());

        return $response;
    }

    /**
     * Headers added to a normal (non-preflight) response.
     */
    private function addActualRequestHeaders(Response $response, string $origin): Response
    {
        $this->addOriginHeaders($response, $origin);
        $this->headerIfNotEmpty($response, 'Access-Control-Expose-Headers', implode(', ', $this->list('exposed_headers')));

        return $response;
    }

    /**
     * Allow-Origin (plus Vary and Allow-Credentials when needed).
     */
    private function addOriginHeaders(Response $response, string $origin): void
    {
        if (in_array('*', $this->list('allowed_origins'), true) && ! $this->supportsCredentials()) {
            $response->header('Access-Control-Allow-Origin', '*');
        } else {
            // Echoing the origin means the answer differs per origin, so caches must know.
            $response->header('Access-Control-Allow-Origin', $origin);
            $this->addVaryOrigin($response);
        }

        if ($this->supportsCredentials()) {
            $response->header('Access-Control-Allow-Credentials', 'true');
        }
    }

    /**
     * The configured header list, or the browser's own request when the list is ['*'].
     */
    private function allowedHeadersFor(Request $request): string
    {
        $headers = $this->list('allowed_headers');

        if ($headers === ['*']) {
            return trim((string) $request->header('Access-Control-Request-Headers', ''));
        }

        return implode(', ', $headers);
    }

    /**
     * Add "Origin" to the Vary header without losing values already there.
     */
    private function addVaryOrigin(Response $response): void
    {
        $vary = trim((string) $response->getHeader('Vary'));

        if ($vary === '') {
            $response->header('Vary', 'Origin');

            return;
        }

        $parts = array_map(static fn (string $part): string => strtolower(trim($part)), explode(',', $vary));

        if (! in_array('origin', $parts, true)) {
            $response->header('Vary', $vary . ', Origin');
        }
    }

    private function headerIfNotEmpty(Response $response, string $name, string $value): void
    {
        if ($value !== '') {
            $response->header($name, $value);
        }
    }

    /**
     * Read a "cors.*" list, keeping only string entries (missing → empty list).
     *
     * @return list<string>
     */
    private function list(string $key): array
    {
        $value = $this->config->get('cors.' . $key, []);

        return is_array($value) ? array_values(array_filter($value, 'is_string')) : [];
    }

    private function maxAge(): int
    {
        return (int) $this->config->get('cors.max_age', 0);
    }

    private function supportsCredentials(): bool
    {
        return $this->config->get('cors.supports_credentials', false) === true;
    }
}
```

```php
<?php

namespace Tests\Unit\Middleware;

use Kanin\Http\Request;
use Kanin\Http\Response;
use Kanin\Middleware\AuthenticateToken;

    {

    }

    private function next(): \Closure
    {
        return function (Request $request): Response {
            $this->nextCalls++;

            return new Response('secret area', 200);
        };
    }

    private function requestWithAuthorization(?string $authorization): Request
    {
        $headers = $authorization === null ? [] : ['Authorization' => $authorization];

        return Request::create('/api/users', 'POST', [], $headers);
    }

    /**

            $this->assertSame('Unauthenticated.', $e->getMessage());
            $this->assertSame(['WWW-Authenticate' => 'Bearer'], $e->getHeaders());
        }

        $this->assertSame(1, $this->nextCalls);
```

- [ ] **Step 8: Implement AuthenticateToken**

Create `system/Middleware/AuthenticateToken.php`:

```php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace Kanin\Middleware;

use Closure;
use Kanin\Http\HttpException;
use Kanin\Http\Middleware;
use Kanin\Http\Request;
use Kanin\Http\Response;
use Kanin\Support\Config;

/**
 * Protects routes with a simple API token.
 *
 * Clients send "Authorization: Bearer <token>". The token must match one of
 * the tokens in config/auth.php (filled from API_TOKENS in .env). The check
 * fails closed: no configured tokens means nobody gets in.
 *
 * Use it on a route with ->middleware('auth.token').
 */
class AuthenticateToken implements Middleware
{
    /**
     * Create the middleware.
     *
     * @param Config $config The application configuration; "auth.tokens" is read.
     */
    public function __construct(private readonly Config $config)
    {
    }

    /**
     * Let the request through only when it carries a valid bearer token.
     *
     * @param Request $request The incoming request.
     * @param Closure $next    Calls the next middleware (and finally your route).
     *
     * @throws HttpException 401 "Unauthenticated." with a "WWW-Authenticate: Bearer" header.
     */
    public function handle(Request $request, Closure $next): Response
    {
        $token = $request->bearerToken();

        if ($token === null || ! $this->matchesAnyToken($token)) {
            throw new HttpException(401, 'Unauthenticated.', ['WWW-Authenticate' => 'Bearer']);
        }

        return $next($request);
    }

    /**
     * Compare against every configured token with hash_equals().
     *
     * hash_equals() takes the same time whether the strings differ early or
     * late, and we never stop at the first match, so response timing does not
     * reveal anything about the secret tokens.
     */
    private function matchesAnyToken(string $given): bool
    {
        $matched = false;

        foreach ($this->tokens() as $known) {
            // hash_equals() runs first so it is evaluated for every token.
            $matched = hash_equals($known, $given) || $matched;
        }

        return $matched;
    }

    /**
     * The configured tokens: non-empty strings only (anything else is ignored).
     *
     * @return list<string>
     */
    private function tokens(): array
    {
        $tokens = $this->config->get('auth.tokens', []);

        if (! is_array($tokens)) {
            return [];
        }

        return array_values(array_filter(
            $tokens,
            static fn (mixed $token): bool => is_string($token) && $token !== '',
        ));
    }
}
```

### Task 7: Database Connection + QueryBuilder

**Files:**
- Create: `system/Database/Connection.php`
- Create: `system/Database/QueryBuilder.php`
- Test: `tests/Unit/Database/ConnectionTest.php`
- Test: `tests/Unit/Database/QueryBuilderTest.php`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Composer PSR-4 autoloading of `Kanin\` → `system/` and `Tests\` → `tests/` (Task 1). No class from Tasks 2–6 is used. `Kanin\Database\Model` (Task 8) appears only in the return types `array|Model|null` of `first()` / `find()`; PHP does not load it until a model instance is actually returned, so Task 7 is green on its own.
- Produces:
  - `Kanin\Database\Connection`: `__construct(array $config)` (keys `driver` sqlite|mysql|pgsql, `database`, `host`, `port`, `username`, `password`, `charset` default `utf8mb4`; an unknown or missing driver throws `\InvalidArgumentException("Unsupported database driver [x].")` at construction, before any connection is opened); `getPdo(): \PDO` (lazy, cached; `ERRMODE_EXCEPTION`, `FETCH_ASSOC`, `EMULATE_PREPARES false`; sqlite runs `PRAGMA foreign_keys = ON`); `getDriverName(): string`; `select(string $sql, array $bindings = []): array`; `selectOne(string $sql, array $bindings = []): ?array`; `insert(string $sql, array $bindings = []): bool`; `update(string $sql, array $bindings = []): int`; `delete(string $sql, array $bindings = []): int`; `statement(string $sql, array $bindings = []): bool`; `unprepared(string $sql): bool`; `lastInsertId(): string`; `transaction(\Closure $callback): mixed`; `table(string $table): QueryBuilder`. Bindings may be a list (positional `?`) or a map (named, key with or without a leading `:`); ints, bools and nulls are bound as `PARAM_INT` / `PARAM_BOOL` / `PARAM_NULL`, everything else as `PARAM_STR`.
  - `Kanin\Database\QueryBuilder`: `__construct(Connection $connection, string $table)`; `setModel(string $modelClass): static`; `select(string ...$columns): static`; `where(string $column, mixed $operator = null, mixed $value = null): static`; `orWhere(string $column, mixed $operator = null, mixed $value = null): static`; `whereIn(string $column, array $values): static`; `whereNull(string $column): static`; `whereNotNull(string $column): static`; `orderBy(string $column, string $direction = 'asc'): static`; `limit(int $limit): static`; `offset(int $offset): static`; `get(): array`; `first(): array|Model|null`; `find(int|string $id, string $column = 'id'): array|Model|null`; `count(): int`; `exists(): bool`; `insert(array $values): int|string`; `update(array $values): int`; `delete(): int`; `paginate(int $perPage = 15, int $page = 1): array`; `toSql(): string`; `getBindings(): array`.
  - Exact refusal messages (other parts and the README may quote them): `\InvalidArgumentException`: `Invalid identifier [x].`, `Invalid column alias [x].`, `Invalid operator [x].` (operator shown trimmed + lowercased), `Invalid order direction [x]. Use asc or desc.`, `Query values must be scalars or null, <type> given.`, `Insert values must not be empty.`, `Update values must not be empty.`, `Limit must not be negative.`, `Offset must not be negative.`, `Model class [X] must define newFromRow().`; `\LogicException`: `Refusing to update without a where clause.`, `Refusing to delete without a where clause.`
  - Behaviour notes: `first()` / `find()` / `count()` / `paginate()` work on clones, so they never change the builder they are called on. `count()` ignores order/limit/offset. `offset()` without `limit()` compiles to `limit -1 offset N` (sqlite), `limit PHP_INT_MAX offset N` (mysql), `offset N` (pgsql). `update()` binds the SET values first, then the where bindings; it ignores order/limit.

```php
        }

        $this->assertSame(['total' => 0], $this->db->selectOne('SELECT COUNT(*) AS total FROM users'));
        $this->assertFalse($this->db->getPdo()->inTransaction());
    }
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the Connection**

Create `system/Database/Connection.php`:

```php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace Kanin\Database;

use PDO;
use PDOStatement;

/**
 * A thin, friendly wrapper around PHP's PDO.
 *
 * It opens the database connection only when it is first needed ("lazy"),
 * always uses prepared statements with bound values, and hands out
 * QueryBuilder instances through table().
 */
class Connection
{
    /** Drivers KaninPHP knows how to build a DSN for. */
    private const DRIVERS = ['sqlite', 'mysql', 'pgsql'];

    /** Default TCP ports for the network drivers. */
    private const DEFAULT_PORTS = ['mysql' => 3306, 'pgsql' => 5432];

    private array $config;

    private ?PDO $pdo = null;

    /**
     * Create a connection from a config array.
     *
     * Keys: driver (sqlite|mysql|pgsql), database, host, port, username,
     * password and charset (defaults to utf8mb4). Nothing connects yet.
     */
    public function __construct(array $config)
    {
        $driver = (string) ($config['driver'] ?? '');

        if (! in_array($driver, self::DRIVERS, true)) {
            throw new \InvalidArgumentException("Unsupported database driver [{$driver}].");
        }

        $this->config = $config + ['charset' => 'utf8mb4'];
    }

    /** Get the underlying PDO object, connecting on the first call. */
    public function getPdo(): PDO
    {
        if ($this->pdo === null) {
            $this->pdo = $this->connect();
        }

        return $this->pdo;
    }

    /** The configured driver name: "sqlite", "mysql" or "pgsql". */
    public function getDriverName(): string
    {
        return $this->config['driver'];
    }

    /** Run a SELECT and return every row as an associative array. */
    public function select(string $sql, array $bindings = []): array
    {
        return $this->run($sql, $bindings)->fetchAll();
    }

    /** Run a SELECT and return the first row, or null when there is none. */
    public function selectOne(string $sql, array $bindings = []): ?array
    {
        $row = $this->run($sql, $bindings)->fetch();

        return is_array($row) ? $row : null;
    }

    /** Run an INSERT statement. Returns true on success. */
    public function insert(string $sql, array $bindings = []): bool
    {
        return $this->statement($sql, $bindings);
    }

    /** Run an UPDATE statement and return the number of affected rows. */
    public function update(string $sql, array $bindings = []): int
    {
        return $this->run($sql, $bindings)->rowCount();
    }

    /** Run a DELETE statement and return the number of deleted rows. */
    public function delete(string $sql, array $bindings = []): int
    {
        return $this->run($sql, $bindings)->rowCount();
    }

    /** Run any other prepared statement (CREATE TABLE, ...). Returns true on success. */
    public function statement(string $sql, array $bindings = []): bool
    {
        $this->run($sql, $bindings);

        return true;
    }

    /**
     * Run raw SQL without bindings (may contain several statements).
     *
     * Only use this with trusted SQL such as database/seed.sql,
     * never with anything that came from a request.
     */
    public function unprepared(string $sql): bool
    {
        $this->getPdo()->exec($sql);

        return true;
    }

    /** The id generated by the most recent INSERT, as a string. */
    public function lastInsertId(): string
    {
        return (string) $this->getPdo()->lastInsertId();
    }

    /**
     * Run the callback inside a transaction.
     *
     * Commits when the callback returns, rolls back and re-throws when it
     * throws. The callback receives this connection; its return value is returned.
     */
    public function transaction(\Closure $callback): mixed
    {
        $pdo = $this->getPdo();
        $pdo->beginTransaction();

        try {
            $result = $callback($this);
            $pdo->commit();

            return $result;
        } catch (\Throwable $e) {
            if ($pdo->inTransaction()) {
                $pdo->rollBack();
            }

            throw $e;
        }
    }

    /** Start a fluent query against a table: $db->table('users')->where('id', 1)->first(). */
    public function table(string $table): QueryBuilder
    {
        return new QueryBuilder($this, $table);
    }

    /** Prepare the SQL, bind every value with a matching PDO type and execute it. */
    private function run(string $sql, array $bindings): PDOStatement
    {
        $statement = $this->getPdo()->prepare($sql);

        foreach ($bindings as $key => $value) {
            $parameter = is_int($key) ? $key + 1 : ':' . ltrim((string) $key, ':');
            $statement->bindValue($parameter, $value, $this->pdoType($value));
        }

        $statement->execute();

        return $statement;
    }

    /** Pick the PDO parameter type for a PHP value. */
    private function pdoType(mixed $value): int
    {
        return match (true) {
            is_int($value) => PDO::PARAM_INT,
            is_bool($value) => PDO::PARAM_BOOL,
            $value === null => PDO::PARAM_NULL,
            default => PDO::PARAM_STR,
        };
    }

    /** Open the real PDO connection. */
    private function connect(): PDO
    {
        $pdo = new PDO(
            $this->dsn(),
            $this->config['username'] ?? null,
            $this->config['password'] ?? null,
            [
                PDO::ATTR_ERRMODE => PDO::ERRMODE_EXCEPTION,
                PDO::ATTR_DEFAULT_FETCH_MODE => PDO::FETCH_ASSOC,
                PDO::ATTR_EMULATE_PREPARES => false,
            ],
        );

        if ($this->getDriverName() === 'sqlite') {
            $pdo->exec('PRAGMA foreign_keys = ON');
        }

        return $pdo;
    }

    /** Build the PDO DSN string for the configured driver. */
    private function dsn(): string
    {
        $driver = $this->getDriverName();
        $database = (string) ($this->config['database'] ?? '');

        if ($driver === 'sqlite') {
            return 'sqlite:' . $database;
        }

        $host = (string) ($this->config['host'] ?? '127.0.0.1');
        $port = (int) ($this->config['port'] ?? self::DEFAULT_PORTS[$driver]);

        if ($driver === 'mysql') {
            return "mysql:host={$host};port={$port};dbname={$database};charset={$this->config['charset']}";
        }

        return "pgsql:host={$host};port={$port};dbname={$database}";
    }
}
```

```php
    public function test_order_by_where_in_and_null_checks_reject_bad_columns(): void

                $this->fail('Expected InvalidArgumentException.');
            } catch (\InvalidArgumentException $e) {
                $this->assertStringStartsWith('Invalid identifier [', $e->getMessage());
            }
        }
    }

    public function test_unknown_operator_is_rejected(): void
    {
        $this->expectException(\InvalidArgumentException::class);
        $this->expectExceptionMessage('Invalid operator [or 1=1 --].');

        $this->users()->where('id', 'or 1=1 --', 1);
    }

    public function test_or_where_rejects_unknown_operator(): void
    {
        $this->expectException(\InvalidArgumentException::class);
        $this->expectExceptionMessage('Invalid operator [in].');

        $this->users()->orWhere('id', 'in', 1);
    }

    public function test_invalid_direction_is_rejected(): void
    {
        $this->expectException(\InvalidArgumentException::class);
        $this->expectExceptionMessage('Invalid order direction [sideways]. Use asc or desc.');

        $this->users()->orderBy('id', 'sideways');
    }

    public function test_non_scalar_values_are_rejected(): void
    {
        $this->expectException(\InvalidArgumentException::class);
        $this->expectExceptionMessage('Query values must be scalars or null, array given.');

        $this->users()->where('id', [1, 2]);
    }

    public function test_negative_limit_and_offset_are_rejected(): void
    {
        try {
            $this->users()->limit(-1);
            $this->fail('Expected InvalidArgumentException.');
        } catch (\InvalidArgumentException $e) {
            $this->assertSame('Limit must not be negative.', $e->getMessage());
        }

        $this->expectException(\InvalidArgumentException::class);
        $this->expectExceptionMessage('Offset must not be negative.');

        $this->users()->offset(-5);
    }

    public function test_get_first_and_find_run_real_queries(): void
    {
        $this->assertSame(['Alan', 'Grace'], array_column($this->users()->where('age', '>', 40)->orderBy('id')->get(), 'name'));
        $this->assertSame('Ada', $this->users()->orderBy('id')->first()['name']);
        $this->assertSame('Grace', $this->users()->find(3)['name']);
        $this->assertSame('Alan', $this->users()->find('alan@example.com', 'email')['name']);
        $this->assertNull($this->users()->find(99));
        $this->assertNull($this->users()->where('name', 'Nobody')->first());
    }

    public function test_first_does_not_change_the_builder(): void
    {
        $query = $this->users();
        $query->first();

        $this->assertSame('select * from "users"', $query->toSql());
        $this->assertCount(3, $query->get());
    }

    public function test_injection_attempts_in_values_are_harmless(): void
    {
        $rows = $this->users()->where('name', "' OR '1'='1")->get();

        $this->assertSame([], $rows);
        $this->assertSame(3, $this->users()->count());
    }

    public function test_count_and_exists(): void
    {
        $this->assertSame(3, $this->users()->count());
        $this->assertSame(1, $this->users()->whereNull('email')->count());
        $this->assertSame(2, $this->users()->where('name', 'like', 'A%')->limit(1)->count());
        $this->assertTrue($this->users()->where('id', 1)->exists());
        $this->assertFalse($this->users()->where('id', 99)->exists());
    }

    public function test_insert_returns_integer_id(): void
    {
        $id = $this->users()->insert(['name' => 'Linus', 'email' => 'linus@example.com', 'age' => null]);

        $this->assertSame(4, $id);
        $this->assertSame(['id' => 4, 'name' => 'Linus', 'email' => 'linus@example.com', 'age' => null], $this->users()->find(4));
    }

    public function test_insert_rejects_empty_values(): void
    {
        $this->expectException(\InvalidArgumentException::class);
        $this->expectExceptionMessage('Insert values must not be empty.');

        $this->users()->insert([]);
    }

    public function test_insert_rejects_bad_column_names(): void
    {
        $this->expectException(\InvalidArgumentException::class);
        $this->expectExceptionMessage('Invalid identifier [name) VALUES (1); --].');

        $this->users()->insert(['name) VALUES (1); --' => 'x']);
    }

    public function test_update_changes_only_matching_rows(): void
    {
        $changed = $this->users()->where('id', 1)->update(['name' => 'Ada Lovelace', 'age' => 37]);

        $this->assertSame(1, $changed);
        $this->assertSame('Ada Lovelace', $this->users()->find(1)['name']);
        $this->assertSame('Alan', $this->users()->find(2)['name']);
    }

    public function test_update_without_where_is_refused(): void
    {
        $this->expectException(\LogicException::class);
        $this->expectExceptionMessage('Refusing to update without a where clause.');

        $this->users()->update(['name' => 'Everyone']);
    }

    public function test_update_rejects_empty_values(): void
    {
        $this->expectException(\InvalidArgumentException::class);
        $this->expectExceptionMessage('Update values must not be empty.');

        $this->users()->where('id', 1)->update([]);
    }

    public function test_delete_removes_only_matching_rows(): void
    {
        $this->assertSame(1, $this->users()->where('id', 2)->delete());
        $this->assertSame(2, $this->users()->count());
    }

    public function test_delete_without_where_is_refused(): void
    {
        try {
            $this->users()->orderBy('id')->limit(1)->delete();
            $this->fail('Expected LogicException.');
        } catch (\LogicException $e) {
            $this->assertSame('Refusing to delete without a where clause.', $e->getMessage());
        }

        $this->assertSame(3, $this->users()->count());
    }

    public function test_paginate_returns_data_and_meta(): void
    {
        $page = $this->users()->orderBy('id')->paginate(2, 2);

        $this->assertSame(['Grace'], array_column($page['data'], 'name'));
        $this->assertSame(['current_page' => 2, 'per_page' => 2, 'total' => 3, 'last_page' => 2], $page['meta']);
    }

    public function test_paginate_shares_where_clauses_between_count_and_page(): void
    {
        $page = $this->users()->where('name', 'like', 'A%')->orderBy('id')->paginate(1, 1);

        $this->assertSame(['Ada'], array_column($page['data'], 'name'));
        $this->assertSame(['current_page' => 1, 'per_page' => 1, 'total' => 2, 'last_page' => 2], $page['meta']);
    }

    public function test_paginate_clamps_per_page_and_page(): void
    {
        $tooSmall = $this->users()->orderBy('id')->paginate(0, -3);
        $this->assertSame(['current_page' => 1, 'per_page' => 1, 'total' => 3, 'last_page' => 3], $tooSmall['meta']);
        $this->assertSame(['Ada'], array_column($tooSmall['data'], 'name'));

        $tooBig = $this->users()->paginate(500);
        $this->assertSame(100, $tooBig['meta']['per_page']);
        $this->assertCount(3, $tooBig['data']);
    }

    public function test_paginate_empty_result_has_last_page_one(): void
    {
        $page = $this->users()->where('id', 99)->paginate();

        $this->assertSame([], $page['data']);
        $this->assertSame(['current_page' => 1, 'per_page' => 15, 'total' => 0, 'last_page' => 1], $page['meta']);
    }

    public function test_paginate_past_the_last_page_returns_no_rows(): void
    {
        $page = $this->users()->paginate(2, 9);

        $this->assertSame([], $page['data']);
        $this->assertSame(9, $page['meta']['current_page']);
        $this->assertSame(2, $page['meta']['last_page']);
    }

    public function test_set_model_hydrates_rows_through_new_from_row(): void
    {
        $model = new class {
            public static function newFromRow(array $row): object
            {
                return (object) ['hydrated' => $row['name']];
            }
        };

        $rows = $this->users()->setModel($model::class)->orderBy('id')->limit(2)->get();

        $this->assertEquals([(object) ['hydrated' => 'Ada'], (object) ['hydrated' => 'Alan']], $rows);
    }

    public function test_set_model_rejects_classes_without_new_from_row(): void
    {
        $this->expectException(\InvalidArgumentException::class);
        $this->expectExceptionMessage('Model class [stdClass] must define newFromRow().');

        $this->users()->setModel(\stdClass::class);
    }

    public function test_set_model_rejects_unknown_classes(): void
    {
        $this->expectException(\InvalidArgumentException::class);
        $this->expectExceptionMessage('Model class [App\Models\Missing] must define newFromRow().');

        $this->users()->setModel('App\Models\Missing');
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 7: Write the QueryBuilder**

Create `system/Database/QueryBuilder.php`:

```php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace Kanin\Database;

/**
 * Builds safe SQL queries one method call at a time.
 *
 * Values are ALWAYS sent to the database as bound parameters (the "?" marks),
 * never glued into the SQL text. Table and column names cannot be bound, so
 * they are checked against a strict pattern and quoted instead.
 */
class QueryBuilder
{
    /** A name like `users`, `email` or `users.email`. */
    private const IDENTIFIER = '/^[A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_]*(\.[A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_]*)?$/';

    /** The only comparison operators where() accepts. */
    private const OPERATORS = ['=', '!=', '<>', '<', '>', '<=', '>=', 'like', 'not like'];

    private array $columns = ['*'];
    /** @var list<array{boolean: string, sql: string, bindings: array}> */
    private array $wheres = [];
    private array $orders = [];
    private ?int $limit = null;
    private ?int $offset = null;
    private ?string $model = null;

    /** Start a query for one table. Prefer $connection->table('users'). */
    public function __construct(private Connection $connection, private string $table)
    {
        $this->wrap($table); // validates the table name
    }

    /** Turn each fetched row into an instance of this model class (used by Model::query()). */
    public function setModel(string $modelClass): static
    {
        if (! class_exists($modelClass) || ! method_exists($modelClass, 'newFromRow')) {
            throw new \InvalidArgumentException("Model class [{$modelClass}] must define newFromRow().");
        }

        $this->model = $modelClass;

        return $this;
    }

    /** Choose the columns to fetch: select('id', 'name', 'email as contact'). */
    public function select(string ...$columns): static
    {
        $this->columns = $columns === [] ? ['*'] : array_map($this->compileColumn(...), $columns);

        return $this;
    }

    /** Add an "and" condition: where('id', 5) or where('age', '>=', 18). */
    public function where(string $column, mixed $operator = null, mixed $value = null): static
    {
        if (func_num_args() === 2) {
            [$operator, $value] = ['=', $operator];
        }

        return $this->addComparison('and', $column, $operator, $value);
    }

    /** Add an "or" condition. Same arguments as where(). */
    public function orWhere(string $column, mixed $operator = null, mixed $value = null): static
    {
        if (func_num_args() === 2) {
            [$operator, $value] = ['=', $operator];
        }

        return $this->addComparison('or', $column, $operator, $value);
    }

    /** Keep rows whose column is one of the values. An empty list matches nothing. */
    public function whereIn(string $column, array $values): static
    {
        if ($values === []) {
            return $this->addWhere('and', '0 = 1', []);
        }

        $values = array_values($values);
        array_walk($values, fn (mixed $value) => $this->assertBindable($value));
        $marks = implode(', ', array_fill(0, count($values), '?'));

        return $this->addWhere('and', $this->wrap($column) . " in ({$marks})", $values);
    }

    /** Keep rows whose column IS NULL. */
    public function whereNull(string $column): static
    {
        return $this->addWhere('and', $this->wrap($column) . ' is null', []);
    }

    /** Keep rows whose column IS NOT NULL. */
    public function whereNotNull(string $column): static
    {
        return $this->addWhere('and', $this->wrap($column) . ' is not null', []);
    }

    /** Sort the results. Direction must be "asc" or "desc". */
    public function orderBy(string $column, string $direction = 'asc'): static
    {
        $direction = strtolower($direction);

        if (! in_array($direction, ['asc', 'desc'], true)) {
            throw new \InvalidArgumentException("Invalid order direction [{$direction}]. Use asc or desc.");
        }

        $this->orders[] = $this->wrap($column) . ' ' . $direction;

        return $this;
    }

    /** Return at most this many rows. */
    public function limit(int $limit): static
    {
        $this->limit = $limit >= 0 ? $limit : throw new \InvalidArgumentException('Limit must not be negative.');

        return $this;
    }

    /** Skip this many rows first (used for paging). */
    public function offset(int $offset): static
    {
        $this->offset = $offset >= 0 ? $offset : throw new \InvalidArgumentException('Offset must not be negative.');

        return $this;
    }

    /** Run the query and return all rows (or model instances when a model is set). */
    public function get(): array
    {
        $rows = $this->connection->select($this->toSql(), $this->getBindings());

        if ($this->model === null) {
            return $rows;
        }

        return array_map(fn (array $row) => $this->model::newFromRow($row), $rows);
    }

    /** Return the first matching row, or null. The builder itself is not changed. */
    public function first(): array|Model|null
    {
        return (clone $this)->limit(1)->get()[0] ?? null;
    }

    /** Find one row by its id (or another column): find(5), find('ada', 'name'). */
    public function find(int|string $id, string $column = 'id'): array|Model|null
    {
        return (clone $this)->where($column, '=', $id)->first();
    }

    /** Count the matching rows (ignores order, limit and offset). */
    public function count(): int
    {
        $sql = 'select count(*) as ' . $this->wrap('aggregate') . ' from ' . $this->wrap($this->table)
            . $this->compileWheres();
        $row = $this->connection->selectOne($sql, $this->getBindings());

        return (int) ($row['aggregate'] ?? 0);
    }

    /** True when at least one row matches. */
    public function exists(): bool
    {
        return $this->count() > 0;
    }

    /** Insert one row: insert(['name' => 'Ada']). Returns the new id (int when numeric). */
    public function insert(array $values): int|string
    {
        $this->assertValues($values, 'Insert');

        $columns = implode(', ', array_map($this->wrap(...), array_keys($values)));
        $marks = implode(', ', array_fill(0, count($values), '?'));
        $sql = 'insert into ' . $this->wrap($this->table) . " ({$columns}) values ({$marks})";

        $this->connection->insert($sql, array_values($values));
        $id = $this->connection->lastInsertId();

        return ctype_digit($id) ? (int) $id : $id;
    }

    /** Update the matching rows and return how many changed. Requires a where clause. */
    public function update(array $values): int
    {
        $this->assertValues($values, 'Update');
        $this->assertScoped('update');

        $sets = array_map(fn (string $column) => $this->wrap($column) . ' = ?', array_keys($values));
        $sql = 'update ' . $this->wrap($this->table) . ' set ' . implode(', ', $sets) . $this->compileWheres();

        return $this->connection->update($sql, [...array_values($values), ...$this->getBindings()]);
    }

    /** Delete the matching rows and return how many were removed. Requires a where clause. */
    public function delete(): int
    {
        $this->assertScoped('delete');

        $sql = 'delete from ' . $this->wrap($this->table) . $this->compileWheres();

        return $this->connection->delete($sql, $this->getBindings());
    }

    /**
     * Fetch one page of results plus paging details.
     *
     * Returns ['data' => [...], 'meta' => ['current_page', 'per_page', 'total', 'last_page']].
     * $perPage is kept between 1 and 100, $page is at least 1.
     */
    public function paginate(int $perPage = 15, int $page = 1): array
    {
        $perPage = max(1, min(100, $perPage));
        $page = max(1, $page);
        $total = (clone $this)->count();

        $data = (clone $this)->limit($perPage)->offset(($page - 1) * $perPage)->get();

        return [
            'data' => $data,
            'meta' => [
                'current_page' => $page,
                'per_page' => $perPage,
                'total' => $total,
                'last_page' => max(1, (int) ceil($total / $perPage)),
            ],
        ];
    }

    /** The SELECT statement this builder will run, with "?" placeholders. */
    public function toSql(): string
    {
        $sql = 'select ' . implode(', ', $this->columns) . ' from ' . $this->wrap($this->table);
        $sql .= $this->compileWheres();

        if ($this->orders !== []) {
            $sql .= ' order by ' . implode(', ', $this->orders);
        }

        return $sql . $this->compileLimitAndOffset();
    }

    /** The values that will be bound to the "?" placeholders, in order. */
    public function getBindings(): array
    {
        return array_merge([], ...array_column($this->wheres, 'bindings'));
    }

    /** Validate and store a "column operator ?" condition. */
    private function addComparison(string $boolean, string $column, mixed $operator, mixed $value): static
    {
        $operator = strtolower(trim((string) $operator));

        if (! in_array($operator, self::OPERATORS, true)) {
            throw new \InvalidArgumentException("Invalid operator [{$operator}].");
        }

        $this->assertBindable($value);

        return $this->addWhere($boolean, $this->wrap($column) . " {$operator} ?", [$value]);
    }

    /** Store one compiled where fragment with its bindings. */
    private function addWhere(string $boolean, string $sql, array $bindings): static
    {
        $this->wheres[] = ['boolean' => $boolean, 'sql' => $sql, 'bindings' => $bindings];

        return $this;
    }

    /** Join the where fragments: ' where a = ? and b = ? or c is null'. */
    private function compileWheres(): string
    {
        $sql = '';

        foreach ($this->wheres as $index => $where) {
            $sql .= ($index === 0 ? ' where ' : " {$where['boolean']} ") . $where['sql'];
        }

        return $sql;
    }

    /** Limit and offset are validated integers, so they are written inline. */
    private function compileLimitAndOffset(): string
    {
        $limit = $this->limit;

        if ($limit === null && $this->offset !== null) {
            // SQLite and MySQL need a LIMIT before OFFSET; these mean "no limit".
            $limit = match ($this->connection->getDriverName()) {
                'sqlite' => -1,
                'mysql' => PHP_INT_MAX,
                default => null,
            };
        }

        $sql = $limit === null ? '' : " limit {$limit}";

        return $sql . ($this->offset === null ? '' : " offset {$this->offset}");
    }

    /** Validate one select() entry: '*', 'name', 'users.name' or 'name as alias'. */
    private function compileColumn(string $column): string
    {
        $column = trim($column);

        if ($column === '*') {
            return '*';
        }

        if (preg_match('/^(\S+)\s+as\s+(\S+)$/i', $column, $parts) === 1) {
            if (str_contains($parts[2], '.')) {
                throw new \InvalidArgumentException("Invalid column alias [{$parts[2]}].");
            }

            return $this->wrap($parts[1]) . ' as ' . $this->wrap($parts[2]);
        }

        return $this->wrap($column);
    }

    /** Validate an identifier and quote each dotted part: users.id -> "users"."id". */
    private function wrap(string $identifier): string
    {
        if (preg_match(self::IDENTIFIER, $identifier) !== 1) {
            throw new \InvalidArgumentException("Invalid identifier [{$identifier}].");
        }

        $quote = $this->connection->getDriverName() === 'mysql' ? '`' : '"';

        return $quote . str_replace('.', $quote . '.' . $quote, $identifier) . $quote;
    }

    /** Only scalars and null can be bound as query values. */
    private function assertBindable(mixed $value): void
    {
        if ($value !== null && ! is_scalar($value)) {
            $type = get_debug_type($value);

            throw new \InvalidArgumentException("Query values must be scalars or null, {$type} given.");
        }
    }

    /** insert()/update() need a non-empty column => value map of bindable values. */
    private function assertValues(array $values, string $action): void
    {
        if ($values === []) {
            throw new \InvalidArgumentException("{$action} values must not be empty.");
        }

        foreach ($values as $column => $value) {
            $this->wrap((string) $column);
            $this->assertBindable($value);
        }
    }

    /** Refuse UPDATE/DELETE without a where clause so a whole table is never changed by accident. */
    private function assertScoped(string $action): void
    {
        if ($this->wheres === []) {
            throw new \LogicException("Refusing to {$action} without a where clause.");
        }
    }
}
```

```bash
git commit -F - <<'EOF'
feat(database): add pdo connection and safe query builder

Lazy PDO connection for sqlite, mysql and pgsql with typed bound values,
transactions and unprepared seed support. The query builder validates and
quotes identifiers, allowlists operators and directions, refuses unscoped
update/delete, and paginates with data + meta.
```

### Task 8: Model

**Files:**

- Create: `tests/Unit/Database/Fixtures/User.php`
- Create: `tests/Unit/Database/Fixtures/BlogPost.php`
- Create: `tests/Unit/Database/Fixtures/Category.php`
- Create: `tests/Unit/Database/Fixtures/Box.php`
- Create: `tests/Unit/Database/Fixtures/Account.php`
- Test: `tests/Unit/Database/ModelTest.php`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes:
  - Task 2: `Kanin\Container::getInstance(): static`, `Kanin\Container::setInstance(?Container $container): void`, `Container::instance(string $abstract, mixed $instance): mixed`, `Container::make(string $abstract, array $parameters = []): mixed`.
  - Task 4: `Kanin\Http\NotFoundHttpException::__construct(string $message = 'Not Found')` (status 404) and `HttpException::getStatusCode(): int`.
  - Task 7: `Connection::__construct(array $config)`, `Connection::table(string $table): QueryBuilder`, `Connection::unprepared(string $sql): bool`, `QueryBuilder::setModel(string $modelClass): static`, `where(string $column, mixed $operator = null, mixed $value = null): static`, `find(int|string $id, string $column = 'id'): array|Model|null`, `first(): array|Model|null`, `get(): array`, `insert(array $values): int|string`, `update(array $values): int`, `delete(): int`, `count(): int`, `paginate(int $perPage = 15, int $page = 1): array`, `toSql(): string`.
- Produces: `abstract class Kanin\Database\Model implements \JsonSerializable` with `protected string $table = ''`, `protected string $primaryKey = 'id'`, `protected array $fillable = []`, `protected array $hidden = []`, `protected array $attributes = []`, `public bool $exists = false`; `__construct(array $attributes = [])` (fills `$fillable` keys only); static `setConnection(?Connection $c): void`, `getConnection(): Connection`, `query(): QueryBuilder`, `all(): array`, `find(int|string $id): ?static`, `findOrFail(int|string $id): static` (throws `NotFoundHttpException('<ShortClassName> not found.')`, e.g. `User not found.`), `where(string $column, mixed $operator = null, mixed $value = null): QueryBuilder`, `create(array $attributes): static`, `newFromRow(array $row): static`; instance `fill(array $attributes): static`, `save(): bool`, `delete(): bool`, `getKey(): int|string|null`, `getTable(): string`, `toArray(): array`, `jsonSerialize(): array`, `__get`, `__set`, `__isset`, `__unset`. Relied on by Task 12 (`model.stub` extends `Model`) and Task 13 (`App\Models\User`, `Tests\TestCase` calls `Model::setConnection(null)`, `UserController` uses `query()->orderBy()->paginate()`, `findOrFail`, `create`, `fill()->save()`, `delete`).
  - Behaviour notes: `save()` on a new model inserts all attributes and sets the key from `insert()` only when the key was not set explicitly; on an existing model it updates every attribute except the primary key (no-op when there is nothing else) and always returns `true`. It does NOT re-read the row, so database defaults such as `created_at` are not on the returned model. `delete()` returns `false` for a model that does not exist (never saved / already deleted) and otherwise `true` when a row was removed, then sets `exists = false`. `__set` is not limited by `$fillable` (that rule is for mass assignment only).

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the model fixtures used by the test**

These tiny classes live under `tests/` (autoload-dev `Tests\` → `tests/`). PHPUnit only runs files ending in `Test.php`, so they are never executed as tests.

```php
final class User extends Model
{

    protected array $hidden = ['password'];
}
```

```php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace Tests\Unit\Database;

use Kanin\Container;
use Kanin\Database\Connection;
use Kanin\Database\Model;
use Kanin\Database\QueryBuilder;
use Kanin\Http\NotFoundHttpException;
use PHPUnit\Framework\TestCase;
use Tests\Unit\Database\Fixtures\Account;
use Tests\Unit\Database\Fixtures\BlogPost;
use Tests\Unit\Database\Fixtures\Box;
use Tests\Unit\Database\Fixtures\Category;
use Tests\Unit\Database\Fixtures\User;

final class ModelTest extends TestCase
{
    private Connection $db;

    protected function setUp(): void
    {
        $this->db = new Connection(['driver' => 'sqlite', 'database' => ':memory:']);
        $this->db->unprepared(
            'CREATE TABLE users (id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT, name TEXT NOT NULL, email TEXT NOT NULL, password TEXT);'
            . "INSERT INTO users (name, email, password) VALUES ('Ada', 'ada@example.com', 'secret-1');"
            . "INSERT INTO users (name, email, password) VALUES ('Alan', 'alan@example.com', 'secret-2');"
            . 'CREATE TABLE legacy_accounts (account_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, owner TEXT);'
        );
        Model::setConnection($this->db);
    }

    protected function tearDown(): void
    {
        Model::setConnection(null);
        Container::setInstance(null);
    }

    public function test_table_name_is_guessed_from_the_class_name(): void
    {
        $this->assertSame('users', (new User())->getTable());
        $this->assertSame('blog_posts', (new BlogPost())->getTable());
        $this->assertSame('categories', (new Category())->getTable());
        $this->assertSame('boxes', (new Box())->getTable());
    }

    public function test_table_property_overrides_the_guess(): void
    {
        $this->assertSame('legacy_accounts', (new Account())->getTable());
    }

    public function test_get_connection_returns_the_static_connection(): void
    {
        $this->assertSame($this->db, User::getConnection());
    }

    public function test_get_connection_falls_back_to_the_container(): void
    {
        $fromContainer = new Connection(['driver' => 'sqlite', 'database' => ':memory:']);
        $container = new Container();
        $container->instance(Connection::class, $fromContainer);
        Container::setInstance($container);

        Model::setConnection(null);

        $this->assertSame($fromContainer, User::getConnection());
    }

    public function test_query_returns_a_builder_for_the_model_table(): void
    {
        $query = User::query();

        $this->assertInstanceOf(QueryBuilder::class, $query);
        $this->assertSame('select * from "users"', $query->toSql());
    }

    public function test_all_returns_hydrated_models(): void
    {
        $users = User::all();

        $this->assertCount(2, $users);
        $this->assertContainsOnlyInstancesOf(User::class, $users);
        $this->assertTrue($users[0]->exists);
        $this->assertSame('Ada', $users[0]->name);
    }

    public function test_find_returns_model_or_null(): void
    {
        $user = User::find(2);

        $this->assertInstanceOf(User::class, $user);
        $this->assertSame(2, $user->getKey());
        $this->assertSame('Alan', $user->name);
        $this->assertNull(User::find(99));
    }

    public function test_find_uses_the_custom_primary_key(): void
    {
        Account::create(['account_id' => 'ACC-1', 'owner' => 'Ada']);

        $account = Account::find('ACC-1');

        $this->assertInstanceOf(Account::class, $account);
        $this->assertSame('ACC-1', $account->getKey());
        $this->assertSame('Ada', $account->owner);
    }

    public function test_find_or_fail_returns_the_model(): void
    {
        $this->assertSame('Ada', User::findOrFail(1)->name);
    }

    public function test_find_or_fail_throws_not_found(): void
    {
        try {
            User::findOrFail(99);
            $this->fail('Expected NotFoundHttpException.');
        } catch (NotFoundHttpException $e) {
            $this->assertSame(404, $e->getStatusCode());
            $this->assertSame('User not found.', $e->getMessage());
        }
    }

    public function test_where_starts_a_model_query(): void
    {
        $this->assertSame('Alan', User::where('email', 'alan@example.com')->first()->name);
        $this->assertSame(['Alan'], array_map(fn (User $u) => $u->name, User::where('id', '>', 1)->get()));
    }

    public function test_where_rejects_bad_operators_like_the_builder(): void
    {
        $this->expectException(\InvalidArgumentException::class);
        $this->expectExceptionMessage('Invalid operator [drop].');

        User::where('id', 'drop', 1);
    }

    public function test_create_inserts_and_sets_the_key(): void
    {
        $user = User::create(['name' => 'Grace', 'email' => 'grace@example.com']);

        $this->assertTrue($user->exists);
        $this->assertSame(3, $user->getKey());
        $this->assertSame(['name' => 'Grace', 'email' => 'grace@example.com', 'id' => 3], $user->toArray());
        $this->assertSame('Grace', User::find(3)->name);
    }

    public function test_create_ignores_attributes_that_are_not_fillable(): void
    {
        $user = User::create(['name' => 'Eve', 'email' => 'eve@example.com', 'password' => 'hacked', 'id' => 500]);

        $this->assertSame(3, $user->getKey());
        $this->assertNull(User::find(3)->password);
        $this->assertNull(User::find(500));
    }

    public function test_constructor_and_fill_only_accept_fillable_keys(): void
    {
        $user = new User(['name' => 'Ada', 'is_admin' => true]);
        $user->fill(['email' => 'ada@example.com', 'id' => 7]);

        $this->assertFalse($user->exists);
        $this->assertNull($user->getKey());
        $this->assertSame(['name' => 'Ada', 'email' => 'ada@example.com'], $user->toArray());
    }

    public function test_fill_returns_the_same_model(): void
    {
        $user = new User();

        $this->assertSame($user, $user->fill(['name' => 'Ada']));
    }

    public function test_new_from_row_keeps_every_column_and_marks_existing(): void
    {
        $user = User::newFromRow(['id' => 9, 'name' => 'Ada', 'password' => 'x']);

        $this->assertTrue($user->exists);
        $this->assertSame(9, $user->getKey());
        $this->assertSame('x', $user->password);
    }

    public function test_save_updates_an_existing_model(): void
    {
        $user = User::find(1);
        $user->name = 'Ada Lovelace';

        $this->assertTrue($user->save());
        $this->assertSame('Ada Lovelace', User::find(1)->name);
        $this->assertSame('Alan', User::find(2)->name);
    }

    public function test_fill_then_save_updates_only_fillable_columns(): void
    {
        $user = User::find(1);
        $user->fill(['email' => 'lovelace@example.com', 'password' => 'nope'])->save();

        $fresh = User::find(1);
        $this->assertSame('lovelace@example.com', $fresh->email);
        $this->assertSame('secret-1', $fresh->password);
    }

    public function test_save_with_only_a_key_is_a_no_op(): void
    {
        $user = User::newFromRow(['id' => 1]);

        $this->assertTrue($user->save());
        $this->assertSame('Ada', User::find(1)->name);
    }

    public function test_save_inserts_a_new_model(): void
    {
        $user = new User(['name' => 'Linus', 'email' => 'linus@example.com']);

        $this->assertTrue($user->save());
        $this->assertTrue($user->exists);
        $this->assertSame(3, $user->getKey());
    }

    public function test_save_keeps_an_explicit_non_numeric_key(): void
    {
        $account = Account::create(['account_id' => 'ACC-7', 'owner' => 'Grace']);

        $this->assertSame('ACC-7', $account->getKey());
        $account->owner = 'Grace Hopper';
        $account->save();

        $this->assertSame('Grace Hopper', Account::find('ACC-7')->owner);
    }

    public function test_delete_removes_the_row(): void
    {
        $user = User::find(1);

        $this->assertTrue($user->delete());
        $this->assertFalse($user->exists);
        $this->assertNull(User::find(1));
        $this->assertNotNull(User::find(2));
    }

    public function test_delete_returns_false_for_unsaved_or_already_deleted_models(): void
    {
        $this->assertFalse((new User(['name' => 'Nobody']))->delete());

        $user = User::find(2);
        $user->delete();

        $this->assertFalse($user->delete());
        $this->assertSame(1, $this->db->table('users')->count());
    }

    public function test_hidden_attributes_never_appear_in_array_or_json(): void
    {
        $user = User::find(1);

        $this->assertSame(['id' => 1, 'name' => 'Ada', 'email' => 'ada@example.com'], $user->toArray());
        $this->assertSame($user->toArray(), $user->jsonSerialize());
        $this->assertSame('{"id":1,"name":"Ada","email":"ada@example.com"}', json_encode($user));
        $this->assertStringNotContainsString('secret', json_encode(User::all()));
    }

    public function test_hidden_attributes_are_still_readable_in_php(): void
    {
        $this->assertSame('secret-1', User::find(1)->password);
    }

    public function test_magic_get_set_isset_and_unset(): void
    {
        $user = new User();

        $this->assertNull($user->nickname);
        $this->assertFalse(isset($user->nickname));

        $user->nickname = 'Countess';
        $this->assertSame('Countess', $user->nickname);
        $this->assertTrue(isset($user->nickname));

        unset($user->nickname);
        $this->assertFalse(isset($user->nickname));
        $this->assertSame([], $user->toArray());
    }

    public function test_paginate_through_a_model_query_returns_models(): void
    {
        $page = User::query()->orderBy('id')->paginate(1, 2);

        $this->assertContainsOnlyInstancesOf(User::class, $page['data']);
        $this->assertSame('Alan', $page['data'][0]->name);
        $this->assertSame(['current_page' => 2, 'per_page' => 1, 'total' => 2, 'last_page' => 2], $page['meta']);
        $this->assertSame(
            '{"data":[{"id":2,"name":"Alan","email":"alan@example.com"}],"meta":{"current_page":2,"per_page":1,"total":2,"last_page":2}}',
            json_encode($page),
        );
    }
}
```
