- [ ] **Step 4: Write the Model**

Create `system/Database/Model.php`:

```php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace Kanin\Database;

use Kanin\Container;
use Kanin\Http\NotFoundHttpException;

/**
 * A small "active record" model: one class per table, one object per row.
 *
 *     class User extends Model
 *     {
 *         protected array $fillable = ['name', 'email'];
 *     }
 *
 *     $user = User::create(['name' => 'Ada', 'email' => 'ada@example.com']);
 *     $user->name = 'Ada Lovelace';
 *     $user->save();
 */
abstract class Model implements \JsonSerializable
{
    /** Table name. Empty means "guess it from the class name" (User -> users). */
    protected string $table = '';

    /** The primary key column. */
    protected string $primaryKey = 'id';

    /** Columns that fill()/create() may set from user input (mass assignment). */
    protected array $fillable = [];

    /** Columns that are never included in toArray() or JSON output. */
    protected array $hidden = [];

    /** The row's column values. */
    protected array $attributes = [];

    /** True once this model has been loaded from, or saved to, the database. */
    public bool $exists = false;

    /** Connection shared by all models (null = ask the container). */
    private static ?Connection $connection = null;

    /** Build a new, unsaved model. Only $fillable keys are taken from $attributes. */
    public function __construct(array $attributes = [])
    {
        $this->fill($attributes);
    }

    /** Use this connection for every model (null goes back to the container's connection). */
    public static function setConnection(?Connection $connection): void
    {
        self::$connection = $connection;
    }

    /** The connection models use: the one set with setConnection(), else the container's. */
    public static function getConnection(): Connection
    {
        return self::$connection ?? Container::getInstance()->make(Connection::class);
    }

    /** Start a query whose results come back as models: User::query()->where(...)->get(). */
    public static function query(): QueryBuilder
    {
        $table = (new static())->getTable();

        return static::getConnection()->table($table)->setModel(static::class);
    }

    /** Every row of the table as models. */
    public static function all(): array
    {
        return static::query()->get();
    }

    /** Find a model by primary key, or null when it does not exist. */
    public static function find(int|string $id): ?static
    {
        $primaryKey = (new static())->primaryKey;

        return static::query()->find($id, $primaryKey);
    }

    /** Find a model by primary key, or throw a 404 NotFoundHttpException. */
    public static function findOrFail(int|string $id): static
    {
        return static::find($id) ?? throw new NotFoundHttpException(static::shortName() . ' not found.');
    }

    /** Start a model query with a where clause: User::where('email', $email)->first(). */
    public static function where(string $column, mixed $operator = null, mixed $value = null): QueryBuilder
    {
        return static::query()->where(...func_get_args());
    }

    /** Fill (fillable keys only), insert and return a new model. */
    public static function create(array $attributes): static
    {
        $model = new static($attributes);
        $model->save();

        return $model;
    }

    /** Build a model from a database row. All columns are kept (fillable is not applied). */
    public static function newFromRow(array $row): static
    {
        $model = new static();
        $model->attributes = $row;
        $model->exists = true;

        return $model;
    }

    /** Set several attributes at once. Keys not listed in $fillable are ignored. */
    public function fill(array $attributes): static
    {
        foreach ($attributes as $key => $value) {
            if (in_array($key, $this->fillable, true)) {
                $this->attributes[$key] = $value;
            }
        }

        return $this;
    }

    /** Insert the model (new) or update its row (existing). Returns true. */
    public function save(): bool
    {
        $values = $this->attributes;
        unset($values[$this->primaryKey]);

        if ($this->exists) {
            if ($values !== []) {
                $this->newQuery()->where($this->primaryKey, $this->getKey())->update($values);
            }

            return true;
        }

        $id = $this->newQuery()->insert($this->attributes);
        $this->attributes[$this->primaryKey] ??= $id;
        $this->exists = true;

        return true;
    }

    /** Delete this model's row. Returns false when the model was never saved. */
    public function delete(): bool
    {
        if (! $this->exists || $this->getKey() === null) {
            return false;
        }

        $deleted = $this->newQuery()->where($this->primaryKey, $this->getKey())->delete();
        $this->exists = false;

        return $deleted > 0;
    }

    /** The primary key value, or null before the model is saved. */
    public function getKey(): int|string|null
    {
        return $this->attributes[$this->primaryKey] ?? null;
    }

    /** The table name: $table, or the snake_case plural of the class name. */
    public function getTable(): string
    {
        return $this->table !== '' ? $this->table : static::guessTable(static::shortName());
    }

    /** The attributes as an array, without the $hidden ones. */
    public function toArray(): array
    {
        return array_diff_key($this->attributes, array_flip($this->hidden));
    }

    /** Called by json_encode(): the same as toArray(), so hidden fields never leak. */
    public function jsonSerialize(): array
    {
        return $this->toArray();
    }

    /** Read an attribute: $user->name (null when missing). */
    public function __get(string $key): mixed
    {
        return $this->attributes[$key] ?? null;
    }

    /** Write an attribute: $user->name = 'Ada' (not limited by $fillable). */
    public function __set(string $key, mixed $value): void
    {
        $this->attributes[$key] = $value;
    }

    /** Support isset($user->name). */
    public function __isset(string $key): bool
    {
        return isset($this->attributes[$key]);
    }

    /** Support unset($user->name). */
    public function __unset(string $key): void
    {
        unset($this->attributes[$key]);
    }

    /** A plain (non-model) query builder for this model's table. */
    private function newQuery(): QueryBuilder
    {
        return static::getConnection()->table($this->getTable());
    }

    /** The class name without its namespace: App\Models\BlogPost -> BlogPost. */
    private static function shortName(): string
    {
        $class = static::class;
        $position = strrpos($class, '\\');

        return $position === false ? $class : substr($class, $position + 1);
    }

    /** BlogPost -> blog_posts, Category -> categories, Box -> boxes. */
    private static function guessTable(string $class): string
    {
        $snake = strtolower((string) preg_replace('/(?<!^)[A-Z]/', '_$0', $class));

        return match (true) {
            preg_match('/[^aeiou]y$/', $snake) === 1 => substr($snake, 0, -1) . 'ies',
            preg_match('/(s|x|z|ch|sh)$/', $snake) === 1 => $snake . 'es',
            default => $snake . 's',
        };
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write ValidationException**

Create `system/Validation/ValidationException.php`:

```php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace Kanin\Validation;

/**
 * Thrown when submitted data breaks one or more validation rules.
 *
 * The exception handler turns it into a 422 JSON response shaped like:
 * {"message": "The given data was invalid.", "errors": {"email": ["..."]}}
 */
class ValidationException extends \RuntimeException
{
    /**
     * @param array<string, list<string>> $errors Field name => list of error messages.
     */
    public function __construct(
        private readonly array $errors,
        string $message = 'The given data was invalid.',
    ) {
        parent::__construct($message);
    }

    /**
     * The failed fields and their messages, e.g. ['email' => ['The email field is required.']].
     *
     * @return array<string, list<string>>
     */
    public function errors(): array
    {
        return $this->errors;
    }

    /**
     * The HTTP status code to respond with: 422 Unprocessable Content.
     */
    public function getStatusCode(): int
    {
        return 422;
    }
}
```

```php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace Tests\Unit\Validation;

use Kanin\Container;
use Kanin\Database\Connection;
use Kanin\Validation\ValidationException;
use Kanin\Validation\Validator;
use PHPUnit\Framework\Attributes\DataProvider;
use PHPUnit\Framework\TestCase;

final class ValidatorTest extends TestCase
{
    private Connection $connection;

    protected function setUp(): void
    {
        $this->connection = new Connection(['driver' => 'sqlite', 'database' => ':memory:']);
        $this->connection->unprepared(
            'CREATE TABLE users (id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT, name TEXT NOT NULL, email TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE);'
            . "INSERT INTO users (name, email) VALUES ('Ada', 'ada@example.com');"
            . "INSERT INTO users (name, email) VALUES ('Alan', 'alan@example.com');"
        );
    }

    protected function tearDown(): void
    {
        Container::setInstance(null);
    }

    /**
     * Validate a single field "field" holding $value against $rules.
     */
    private function errorsFor(mixed $value, string $rules, array $extra = []): array
    {
        $validator = new Validator(['field' => $value] + $extra, ['field' => $rules], [], $this->connection);

        return $validator->errors()['field'] ?? [];
    }

    public static function passingValues(): array
    {
        return [
            'required string' => ['Ada', 'required'],
            'required zero string' => ['0', 'required'],
            'required int zero' => [0, 'required'],
            'required false' => [false, 'required'],
            'string' => ['hello', 'string'],
            'email' => ['ada@example.com', 'email'],
            'numeric int' => [42, 'numeric'],
            'numeric float' => [4.2, 'numeric'],
            'numeric string' => ['4.2', 'numeric'],
            'integer int' => [7, 'integer'],
            'integer string' => ['-7', 'integer'],
            'min string' => ['abc', 'min:3'],
            'min multibyte' => ['ñññ', 'min:3'],
            'max string' => ['abc', 'max:3'],
            'min numeric' => ['10', 'numeric|min:10'],
            'max integer' => [5, 'integer|max:5'],
            'between string' => ['abcd', 'between:2,4'],
            'between numeric' => [3, 'numeric|between:1,5'],
            'min array' => [['a', 'b'], 'min:2'],
            'max array' => [['a'], 'max:1'],
            'in' => ['admin', 'in:admin,editor'],
            'in int' => [2, 'in:1,2,3'],
            'alpha_num' => ['abc123', 'alpha_num'],
            'alpha_num unicode' => ['Ñandú9', 'alpha_num'],
            'url http' => ['http://example.com', 'url'],
            'url https path' => ['https://example.com/a?b=c', 'url'],
            'nullable' => ['anything', 'nullable'],
        ];
    }

    #[DataProvider('passingValues')]
    public function test_rule_passes(mixed $value, string $rules): void
    {
        $this->assertSame([], $this->errorsFor($value, $rules));
    }

    public static function failingValues(): array
    {
        return [
            'required null' => [null, 'required', 'The field field is required.'],
            'required empty string' => ['', 'required', 'The field field is required.'],
            'required blank string' => ['   ', 'required', 'The field field is required.'],
            'required empty array' => [[], 'required', 'The field field is required.'],
            'string' => [123, 'string', 'The field must be a string.'],
            'email' => ['not-an-email', 'email', 'The field must be a valid email address.'],
            'email non string' => [['a@b.c'], 'email', 'The field must be a valid email address.'],
            'numeric' => ['abc', 'numeric', 'The field must be a number.'],
            'numeric bool' => [true, 'numeric', 'The field must be a number.'],
            'integer float' => [1.5, 'integer', 'The field must be an integer.'],
            'integer string' => ['1.5', 'integer', 'The field must be an integer.'],
            'min string' => ['ab', 'min:3', 'The field must be at least 3 characters.'],
            'max string' => ['abcd', 'max:3', 'The field may not be greater than 3 characters.'],
            'min numeric' => ['9', 'numeric|min:10', 'The field must be at least 10.'],
            'max numeric' => [6, 'integer|max:5', 'The field may not be greater than 5.'],
            'min array' => [['a'], 'min:2', 'The field must have at least 2 items.'],
            'max array' => [['a', 'b'], 'max:1', 'The field may not have more than 1 items.'],
            'between string' => ['a', 'between:2,4', 'The field must be between 2 and 4 characters.'],
            'between numeric' => [9, 'numeric|between:1,5', 'The field must be between 1 and 5.'],
            'between array' => [['a'], 'between:2,3', 'The field must have between 2 and 3 items.'],
            'in' => ['owner', 'in:admin,editor', 'The selected field is invalid.'],
            'in array' => [['admin'], 'in:admin', 'The selected field is invalid.'],
            'alpha_num' => ['abc-123', 'alpha_num', 'The field may only contain letters and numbers.'],
            'url' => ['not a url', 'url', 'The field must be a valid URL.'],
            'url javascript scheme' => ['javascript://alert(1)', 'url', 'The field must be a valid URL.'],
            'url ftp scheme' => ['ftp://example.com', 'url', 'The field must be a valid URL.'],
        ];
    }

    #[DataProvider('failingValues')]
    public function test_rule_fails_with_default_message(mixed $value, string $rules, string $message): void
    {
        $this->assertSame([$message], $this->errorsFor($value, $rules));
    }

    public function test_empty_value_without_required_skips_every_other_rule(): void
    {
        $this->assertSame([], $this->errorsFor('', 'email|min:5'));
        $this->assertSame([], $this->errorsFor(null, 'nullable|integer'));
        $this->assertSame([], (new Validator([], ['missing' => 'string|max:3']))->errors());
    }

    public function test_required_failure_reports_only_the_required_message(): void
    {
        $this->assertSame(['The field field is required.'], $this->errorsFor('', 'required|email|min:5'));
    }

    public function test_every_failing_rule_is_reported_in_order(): void
    {
        $this->assertSame(
            ['The field must be a valid email address.', 'The field may not be greater than 3 characters.'],
            $this->errorsFor('abcdef', 'required|email|max:3'),
        );
    }

    public function test_attribute_underscores_become_spaces(): void
    {
        $errors = (new Validator([], ['first_name' => 'required']))->errors();

        $this->assertSame(['first_name' => ['The first name field is required.']], $errors);
    }

    public function test_confirmed_rule(): void
    {
        $ok = ['field' => 'secret', 'field_confirmation' => 'secret'];
        $this->assertSame([], (new Validator($ok, ['field' => 'confirmed']))->errors());

        $this->assertSame(
            ['The field confirmation does not match.'],
            $this->errorsFor('secret', 'confirmed', ['field_confirmation' => 'other']),
        );
        $this->assertSame(['The field confirmation does not match.'], $this->errorsFor('secret', 'confirmed'));
    }

    public function test_same_rule(): void
    {
        $this->assertSame([], $this->errorsFor('a', 'same:other_field', ['other_field' => 'a']));
        $this->assertSame(['The field and other field must match.'], $this->errorsFor('a', 'same:other_field', ['other_field' => 'b']));
        $this->assertSame(['The field and other field must match.'], $this->errorsFor('a', 'same:other_field'));
    }

    public function test_rules_can_be_given_as_an_array(): void
    {
        $validator = new Validator(['name' => 'Ada'], ['name' => ['required', 'string', 'max:2']]);

        $this->assertSame(['name' => ['The name may not be greater than 2 characters.']], $validator->errors());
    }

    public function test_unique_rule_uses_the_database(): void
    {
        $this->assertSame([], $this->errorsFor('new@example.com', 'unique:users,email'));
        $this->assertSame(['The field has already been taken.'], $this->errorsFor('ada@example.com', 'unique:users,email'));
    }

    public function test_unique_rule_defaults_the_column_to_the_field_name(): void
    {
        $validator = new Validator(['email' => 'ada@example.com'], ['email' => 'unique:users'], [], $this->connection);

        $this->assertSame(['email' => ['The email has already been taken.']], $validator->errors());
    }

    public function test_unique_rule_can_ignore_a_row_by_id(): void
    {
        $this->assertSame([], $this->errorsFor('ada@example.com', 'unique:users,email,1'));
        $this->assertSame([], $this->errorsFor('ada@example.com', 'unique:users,email,1,id'));
        $this->assertSame(['The field has already been taken.'], $this->errorsFor('ada@example.com', 'unique:users,email,2'));
    }

    public function test_exists_rule(): void
    {
        $this->assertSame([], $this->errorsFor('alan@example.com', 'exists:users,email'));
        $this->assertSame(['The selected field is invalid.'], $this->errorsFor('nobody@example.com', 'exists:users,email'));
    }

    public function test_database_rules_reject_non_scalar_values_without_querying(): void
    {
        $this->assertSame(['The field has already been taken.'], $this->errorsFor(['ada@example.com'], 'unique:users,email'));
        $this->assertSame(['The selected field is invalid.'], $this->errorsFor(true, 'exists:users,email'));
    }

    public function test_database_rules_reject_unsafe_table_names(): void
    {
        $this->expectException(\InvalidArgumentException::class);

        $this->errorsFor('x', 'unique:users;drop table users,email');
    }

    public function test_database_rules_fall_back_to_the_container_connection(): void
    {
        $container = new Container();
        $container->instance(Connection::class, $this->connection);
        Container::setInstance($container);

        $validator = Validator::make(['email' => 'ada@example.com'], ['email' => 'unique:users,email']);

        $this->assertSame(['email' => ['The email has already been taken.']], $validator->errors());
    }

    public function test_custom_messages_by_field_and_rule_then_by_rule(): void
    {
        $validator = Validator::make(
            ['name' => '', 'email' => ''],
            ['name' => 'required', 'email' => 'required'],
            ['name.required' => 'Tell us your :attribute!', 'required' => ':attribute is missing'],
        );

        $this->assertSame(
            ['name' => ['Tell us your name!'], 'email' => ['email is missing']],
            $validator->errors(),
        );
    }

    public function test_custom_messages_replace_size_placeholders(): void
    {
        $validator = Validator::make(['code' => 'abcdef'], ['code' => 'between:2,4'], ['between' => ':attribute: :min to :max']);

        $this->assertSame(['code' => ['code: 2 to 4']], $validator->errors());
    }

    public function test_passes_and_fails(): void
    {
        $this->assertTrue(Validator::make(['name' => 'Ada'], ['name' => 'required'])->passes());
        $this->assertFalse(Validator::make(['name' => 'Ada'], ['name' => 'required'])->fails());
        $this->assertFalse(Validator::make([], ['name' => 'required'])->passes());
        $this->assertTrue(Validator::make([], ['name' => 'required'])->fails());
    }

    public function test_validated_returns_only_ruled_fields_that_were_sent(): void
    {
        $validator = Validator::make(
            ['name' => 'Ada', 'is_admin' => true, 'bio' => null],
            ['name' => 'required', 'email' => 'nullable|email', 'bio' => 'nullable|string'],
        );

        $this->assertSame(['name' => 'Ada', 'bio' => null], $validator->validated());
    }

    public function test_validate_returns_validated_data_when_passing(): void
    {
        $data = Validator::make(['name' => 'Ada', 'role' => 'x'], ['name' => 'required|string'])->validate();

        $this->assertSame(['name' => 'Ada'], $data);
    }

    public function test_validate_throws_validation_exception_with_errors(): void
    {
        try {
            Validator::make(['email' => 'nope'], ['name' => 'required', 'email' => 'email'])->validate();
            $this->fail('Expected a ValidationException.');
        } catch (ValidationException $e) {
            $this->assertSame('The given data was invalid.', $e->getMessage());
            $this->assertSame(422, $e->getStatusCode());
            $this->assertSame([
                'name' => ['The name field is required.'],
                'email' => ['The email must be a valid email address.'],
            ], $e->errors());
        }
    }

    public function test_unknown_rule_throws(): void
    {
        $this->expectException(\InvalidArgumentException::class);
        $this->expectExceptionMessage('Unknown validation rule [sometimes].');

        Validator::make(['name' => 'Ada'], ['name' => 'required|sometimes']);
    }

    public function test_rule_missing_parameters_throws(): void
    {
        $this->expectException(\InvalidArgumentException::class);
        $this->expectExceptionMessage('Validation rule [between] requires at least 2 parameter(s).');

        Validator::make(['age' => 3], ['age' => 'between:1']);
    }

    public function test_size_rule_with_non_numeric_parameter_throws(): void
    {
        $this->expectException(\InvalidArgumentException::class);
        $this->expectExceptionMessage('Validation rule [max] requires numeric parameters.');

        Validator::make(['name' => 'Ada'], ['name' => 'max:ten']);
    }

    public function test_non_string_rule_throws(): void
    {
        $this->expectException(\InvalidArgumentException::class);
        $this->expectExceptionMessage('Validation rules must be strings.');

        Validator::make(['name' => 'Ada'], ['name' => ['required', 5]]);
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 6: Run it and watch it fail**

```bash
vendor/bin/phpunit --filter ValidatorTest
```

Expected: FAIL with `Error: Class "Kanin\Validation\Validator" not found`.

- [ ] **Step 7: Write the Validator**

Create `system/Validation/Validator.php`. Behaviour notes for the reader:
- A field that is missing, `null`, a blank string or `[]` is "empty". Empty + `required` → only the required message; empty without `required` → every other rule is skipped. `0`, `'0'` and `false` are not empty.
- Rules are parsed (and unknown rules rejected) in the constructor, so typos fail loudly even when the field is absent.
- `url` only accepts `http`/`https` URLs (`javascript:` and friends are rejected).
- `unique`/`exists` never build SQL by hand: they use `QueryBuilder`, which validates the table/column names and binds the value. Non-scalar values (arrays, booleans) fail without querying. The column defaults to the field name; the optional `ignoreId` is cast to `int` when it is all digits and compared with `!=` on `idColumn` (default `id`).

```php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace Kanin\Validation;

use Kanin\Container;
use Kanin\Database\Connection;

/**
 * Checks an array of input against a set of rules.
 *
 *     $validator = Validator::make($data, [
 *         'name'  => 'required|string|max:100',
 *         'email' => ['required', 'email', 'unique:users,email'],
 *     ]);
 *
 *     if ($validator->fails()) {
 *         $validator->errors(); // ['email' => ['The email has already been taken.']]
 *     }
 *
 * Rules run in the order you write them. A field that is missing or empty
 * (null, '' or []) only fails when it has the "required" rule; every other
 * rule is skipped for empty fields.
 */
class Validator
{
    /** Number of parameters each rule needs at minimum, e.g. "between:1,10" needs 2. */
    private const RULES = [
        'required' => 0, 'nullable' => 0, 'string' => 0, 'email' => 0, 'numeric' => 0,
        'integer' => 0, 'min' => 1, 'max' => 1, 'between' => 2, 'confirmed' => 0,
        'in' => 1, 'same' => 1, 'alpha_num' => 0, 'url' => 0, 'unique' => 1, 'exists' => 1,
    ];

    /** Default messages. Size rules have one message per kind of value. */
    private const MESSAGES = [
        'required' => 'The :attribute field is required.',
        'string' => 'The :attribute must be a string.',
        'email' => 'The :attribute must be a valid email address.',
        'numeric' => 'The :attribute must be a number.',
        'integer' => 'The :attribute must be an integer.',
        'min.numeric' => 'The :attribute must be at least :min.',
        'min.array' => 'The :attribute must have at least :min items.',
        'min.string' => 'The :attribute must be at least :min characters.',
        'max.numeric' => 'The :attribute may not be greater than :max.',
        'max.array' => 'The :attribute may not have more than :max items.',
        'max.string' => 'The :attribute may not be greater than :max characters.',
        'between.numeric' => 'The :attribute must be between :min and :max.',
        'between.array' => 'The :attribute must have between :min and :max items.',
        'between.string' => 'The :attribute must be between :min and :max characters.',
        'confirmed' => 'The :attribute confirmation does not match.',
        'in' => 'The selected :attribute is invalid.',
        'same' => 'The :attribute and :other must match.',
        'alpha_num' => 'The :attribute may only contain letters and numbers.',
        'url' => 'The :attribute must be a valid URL.',
        'unique' => 'The :attribute has already been taken.',
        'exists' => 'The selected :attribute is invalid.',
    ];

    /** @var array<string, array<string, list<string>>> field => [rule => parameters] */
    private array $rules = [];

    /** @var array<string, list<string>>|null Cached result; null until the data is checked. */
    private ?array $errors = null;

    /**
     * @param array<string, mixed> $data The input to check (e.g. $request->all()).
     * @param array<string, string|list<string>> $rules Field => 'rule|rule:param' or ['rule', 'rule:param'].
     * @param array<string, string> $messages Custom messages keyed 'field.rule' or 'rule'.
     * @param Connection|null $connection Database used by unique/exists (defaults to the app's).
     */
    public function __construct(
        private readonly array $data,
        array $rules,
        private readonly array $messages = [],
        private ?Connection $connection = null,
    ) {
        foreach ($rules as $field => $fieldRules) {
            $this->rules[(string) $field] = $this->parseRules($fieldRules);
        }
    }

    /**
     * Shortcut for `new Validator(...)` that reads nicely: Validator::make($data, $rules).
     *
     * @param array<string, mixed> $data
     * @param array<string, string|list<string>> $rules
     * @param array<string, string> $messages
     */
    public static function make(array $data, array $rules, array $messages = []): static
    {
        return new static($data, $rules, $messages);
    }

    /**
     * True when every field passes all of its rules.
     */
    public function passes(): bool
    {
        return $this->errors() === [];
    }

    /**
     * True when at least one field breaks a rule.
     */
    public function fails(): bool
    {
        return ! $this->passes();
    }

    /**
     * Error messages for the failed fields only: ['email' => ['...', '...']].
     *
     * @return array<string, list<string>>
     */
    public function errors(): array
    {
        if ($this->errors === null) {
            $this->errors = [];

            foreach ($this->rules as $field => $rules) {
                $messages = $this->validateField($field, $rules);

                if ($messages !== []) {
                    $this->errors[$field] = $messages;
                }
            }
        }

        return $this->errors;
    }

    /**
     * The input limited to fields that have rules AND were sent.
     * Extra fields the client sent (like "is_admin") are dropped.
     *
     * @return array<string, mixed>
     */
    public function validated(): array
    {
        $validated = [];

        foreach (array_keys($this->rules) as $field) {
            if (array_key_exists($field, $this->data)) {
                $validated[$field] = $this->data[$field];
            }
        }

        return $validated;
    }

    /**
     * Return the validated data, or throw a ValidationException (rendered as 422).
     *
     * @return array<string, mixed>
     */
    public function validate(): array
    {
        if ($this->fails()) {
            throw new ValidationException($this->errors());
        }

        return $this->validated();
    }

    /**
     * Turn 'required|max:100' or ['required', 'max:100'] into ['required' => [], 'max' => ['100']].
     * Unknown rules or missing parameters fail loudly right away.
     */
    private function parseRules(string|array $rules): array
    {
        $parsed = [];

        foreach (is_string($rules) ? explode('|', $rules) : $rules as $rule) {
            if (! is_string($rule)) {
                throw new \InvalidArgumentException('Validation rules must be strings.');
            }

            [$name, $parameters] = array_pad(explode(':', trim($rule), 2), 2, null);

            if ($name === '') {
                continue;
            }

            if (! array_key_exists($name, self::RULES)) {
                throw new \InvalidArgumentException("Unknown validation rule [{$name}].");
            }

            $parameters = $parameters === null ? [] : array_map('trim', explode(',', $parameters));

            if (count($parameters) < self::RULES[$name]) {
                throw new \InvalidArgumentException("Validation rule [{$name}] requires at least " . self::RULES[$name] . ' parameter(s).');
            }

            if (in_array($name, ['min', 'max', 'between'], true) && array_filter($parameters, 'is_numeric') !== $parameters) {
                throw new \InvalidArgumentException("Validation rule [{$name}] requires numeric parameters.");
            }

            $parsed[$name] = $parameters;
        }

        return $parsed;
    }

    /** Run every rule of one field and collect the failure messages. */
    private function validateField(string $field, array $rules): array
    {
        $value = $this->data[$field] ?? null;

        if ($this->isEmpty($value)) {
            return isset($rules['required']) ? [$this->message($field, 'required', [], $value, $rules)] : [];
        }

        $messages = [];

        foreach ($rules as $rule => $parameters) {
            if (! $this->check($field, $value, $rule, $parameters, $rules)) {
                $messages[] = $this->message($field, $rule, $parameters, $value, $rules);
            }
        }

        return $messages;
    }

    /** Does a (non-empty) value pass one rule? */
    private function check(string $field, mixed $value, string $rule, array $parameters, array $rules): bool
    {
        $size = $this->size($value, $rules);

        return match ($rule) {
            'required', 'nullable' => true,
            'string' => is_string($value),
            'email' => is_string($value) && filter_var($value, FILTER_VALIDATE_EMAIL) !== false,
            'numeric' => is_int($value) || is_float($value) || (is_string($value) && is_numeric($value)),
            'integer' => (is_int($value) || is_string($value)) && filter_var($value, FILTER_VALIDATE_INT) !== false,
            'min' => $size !== null && $size >= (float) $parameters[0],
            'max' => $size !== null && $size <= (float) $parameters[0],
            'between' => $size !== null && $size >= (float) $parameters[0] && $size <= (float) $parameters[1],
            'confirmed' => ($this->data[$field . '_confirmation'] ?? null) === $value,
            'in' => is_scalar($value) && in_array((string) $value, $parameters, true),
            'same' => array_key_exists($parameters[0], $this->data) && $this->data[$parameters[0]] === $value,
            'alpha_num' => (is_string($value) || is_int($value)) && preg_match('/^[\pL\pM\pN]+$/u', (string) $value) === 1,
            'url' => is_string($value) && filter_var($value, FILTER_VALIDATE_URL) !== false
                && in_array(strtolower((string) parse_url($value, PHP_URL_SCHEME)), ['http', 'https'], true),
            'unique' => $this->isQueryable($value) && ! $this->rowExists($field, $value, $parameters, true),
            'exists' => $this->isQueryable($value) && $this->rowExists($field, $value, $parameters, false),
        };
    }

    /**
     * Look a value up in the database for the unique/exists rules.
     * Format: unique:table,column[,ignoreId[,idColumn]] and exists:table,column.
     * The column defaults to the field name. QueryBuilder validates the
     * table/column names and binds the value, so this is SQL-injection safe.
     */
    private function rowExists(string $field, string|int|float $value, array $parameters, bool $useIgnore): bool
    {
        $column = ($parameters[1] ?? '') !== '' ? $parameters[1] : $field;
        $query = $this->connection()->table($parameters[0])->where($column, $value);
        $ignoreId = $parameters[2] ?? '';

        if ($useIgnore && $ignoreId !== '') {
            $idColumn = ($parameters[3] ?? '') !== '' ? $parameters[3] : 'id';
            $query->where($idColumn, '!=', ctype_digit($ignoreId) ? (int) $ignoreId : $ignoreId);
        }

        return $query->exists();
    }

    /** The database connection: the one passed in, or the application's. */
    private function connection(): Connection
    {
        return $this->connection ??= Container::getInstance()->make(Connection::class);
    }

    /**
     * The "size" used by min/max/between: the number itself for numeric
     * fields, the item count for arrays, otherwise the character count.
     */
    private function size(mixed $value, array $rules): ?float
    {
        return match ($this->sizeType($value, $rules)) {
            'numeric' => (float) $value,
            'array' => (float) count($value),
            default => is_scalar($value) ? (float) mb_strlen((string) $value) : null,
        };
    }

    /** Which kind of size applies: 'numeric', 'array' or 'string'. */
    private function sizeType(mixed $value, array $rules): string
    {
        $hasNumericRule = isset($rules['numeric']) || isset($rules['integer']);

        return match (true) {
            $hasNumericRule && (is_int($value) || is_float($value) || (is_string($value) && is_numeric($value))) => 'numeric',
            is_array($value) => 'array',
            default => 'string',
        };
    }

    /** Build the message for a failed rule, using a custom message when given. */
    private function message(string $field, string $rule, array $parameters, mixed $value, array $rules): string
    {
        $key = in_array($rule, ['min', 'max', 'between'], true) ? $rule . '.' . $this->sizeType($value, $rules) : $rule;
        $template = $this->messages["{$field}.{$rule}"] ?? $this->messages[$rule] ?? self::MESSAGES[$key];

        return strtr($template, [
            ':attribute' => str_replace('_', ' ', $field),
            ':other' => str_replace('_', ' ', $parameters[0] ?? ''),
            ':min' => $parameters[0] ?? '',
            ':max' => $rule === 'between' ? ($parameters[1] ?? '') : ($parameters[0] ?? ''),
            ':values' => implode(', ', $parameters),
        ]);
    }

    /** Missing, null, blank strings and empty arrays count as "empty". */
    private function isEmpty(mixed $value): bool
    {
        return $value === null || $value === [] || (is_string($value) && trim($value) === '');
    }

    /** Only plain values (not arrays, not booleans) can be looked up in the database. */
    private function isQueryable(mixed $value): bool
    {
        return is_string($value) || is_int($value) || is_float($value);
    }
}
```

```php
    /**
     * Validate this request's input (query + body) against the given rules.
     *
     * Returns only the validated fields. When validation fails a
     * ValidationException is thrown, which KaninPHP turns into a 422 JSON
     * response for you - so your controller code only runs with good data.
     *
     *     $data = $request->validate(['email' => 'required|email']);
     *
     * @param array<string, string|list<string>> $rules
     * @param array<string, string> $messages
     * @return array<string, mixed>
     */
    public function validate(array $rules, array $messages = []): array
    {
        return Validator::make($this->all(), $rules, $messages)->validate();
    }
```

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing Handler test**

Create `tests/Unit/Exceptions/HandlerTest.php`:

```php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace Tests\Unit\Exceptions;

use ErrorException;
use Kanin\Application;
use Kanin\Container;
use Kanin\Exceptions\Handler;
use Kanin\Http\HttpException;
use Kanin\Http\MethodNotAllowedHttpException;
use Kanin\Http\NotFoundHttpException;
use Kanin\Http\Request;
use Kanin\Support\Config;
use Kanin\Validation\ValidationException;
use PHPUnit\Framework\TestCase;
use RuntimeException;

final class HandlerTest extends TestCase
{
    private string $basePath;
    private Application $app;
    private Handler $handler;
    private Request $request;

    protected function setUp(): void
    {
        $this->basePath = sys_get_temp_dir() . '/kanin-handler-' . bin2hex(random_bytes(6));
        mkdir($this->basePath);

        $this->app = new Application($this->basePath);
        $this->setDebug(false);
        $this->handler = new Handler($this->app);
        $this->request = Request::create('/api/test');
    }

    protected function tearDown(): void
    {
        Container::setInstance(null);
        $this->deleteDirectory($this->basePath);
    }

    private function setDebug(bool $debug): void
    {
        $this->app->instance(Config::class, new Config(['app' => ['debug' => $debug]]));
    }

    private function logPath(): string
    {
        return $this->basePath . '/storage/logs/kanin.log';
    }

    private function deleteDirectory(string $path): void
    {
        if (is_file($path)) {
            unlink($path);

            return;
        }

        if (! is_dir($path)) {
            return;
        }

        foreach (scandir($path) as $item) {
            if ($item !== '.' && $item !== '..') {
                $this->deleteDirectory($path . '/' . $item);
            }
        }

        rmdir($path);
    }

    public function test_validation_exception_renders_422_with_errors(): void
    {
        $errors = ['email' => ['The email field is required.']];

        $response = $this->handler->render($this->request, new ValidationException($errors));

        $this->assertSame(422, $response->getStatusCode());
        $this->assertSame(['message' => 'The given data was invalid.', 'errors' => $errors], $response->getData());
        $this->assertStringContainsString('application/json', (string) $response->getHeader('Content-Type'));
    }

    public function test_validation_exception_keeps_a_custom_message(): void
    {
        $response = $this->handler->render($this->request, new ValidationException(['name' => ['Bad.']], 'Check your input.'));

        $this->assertSame('Check your input.', $response->getData()['message']);
    }

    public function test_not_found_renders_404(): void
    {
        $response = $this->handler->render($this->request, new NotFoundHttpException());

        $this->assertSame(404, $response->getStatusCode());
        $this->assertSame(['message' => 'Not Found'], $response->getData());
    }

    public function test_method_not_allowed_renders_405_with_allow_header(): void
    {
        $response = $this->handler->render($this->request, new MethodNotAllowedHttpException(['GET', 'POST']));

        $this->assertSame(405, $response->getStatusCode());
        $this->assertSame(['message' => 'Method Not Allowed'], $response->getData());
        $this->assertSame('GET, POST', $response->getHeader('Allow'));
    }

    public function test_http_exception_keeps_status_message_and_headers(): void
    {
        $exception = new HttpException(401, 'Unauthenticated.', ['WWW-Authenticate' => 'Bearer']);

        $response = $this->handler->render($this->request, $exception);

        $this->assertSame(401, $response->getStatusCode());
        $this->assertSame(['message' => 'Unauthenticated.'], $response->getData());
        $this->assertSame('Bearer', $response->getHeader('WWW-Authenticate'));
    }

    public function test_http_exception_without_message_uses_the_reason_phrase(): void
    {
        $this->assertSame(['message' => 'Forbidden'], $this->handler->render($this->request, new HttpException(403))->getData());
        $this->assertSame(['message' => 'Too Many Requests'], $this->handler->render($this->request, new HttpException(429))->getData());
        $this->assertSame(['message' => 'Service Unavailable'], $this->handler->render($this->request, new HttpException(503))->getData());
    }

    public function test_unknown_status_without_message_uses_a_generic_phrase(): void
    {
        $this->assertSame(['message' => 'Error'], $this->handler->render($this->request, new HttpException(499))->getData());
        $this->assertSame(['message' => 'Server Error'], $this->handler->render($this->request, new HttpException(599))->getData());
    }

    public function test_http_exceptions_never_include_debug_details(): void
    {
        $this->setDebug(true);

        $response = $this->handler->render($this->request, new NotFoundHttpException());

        $this->assertSame(['message' => 'Not Found'], $response->getData());
    }

    public function test_server_error_in_production_hides_every_detail(): void
    {
        $response = $this->handler->render($this->request, new RuntimeException('database password is hunter2'));

        $this->assertSame(500, $response->getStatusCode());
        $this->assertSame(['message' => 'Server Error'], $response->getData());
        $this->assertStringNotContainsString('hunter2', $response->getContent());
        $this->assertStringNotContainsString(__FILE__, str_replace('\\/', '/', $response->getContent()));
    }

    public function test_server_error_in_debug_mode_shows_details(): void
    {
        $this->setDebug(true);
        $exception = new RuntimeException('Something broke');

        $data = $this->handler->render($this->request, $exception)->getData();

        $this->assertSame('Something broke', $data['message']);
        $this->assertSame(RuntimeException::class, $data['exception']);
        $this->assertSame(__FILE__, $data['file']);
        $this->assertSame($exception->getLine(), $data['line']);
        $this->assertIsList($data['trace']);
        $this->assertContainsOnlyString($data['trace']);
        $this->assertStringStartsWith('#0 ', $data['trace'][0]);
    }

    public function test_debug_output_survives_invalid_utf8_in_the_message(): void
    {
        $this->setDebug(true);

        $response = $this->handler->render($this->request, new RuntimeException("bad byte \xB1 here"));

        $this->assertSame(500, $response->getStatusCode());
        $this->assertStringStartsWith('bad byte ', $response->getData()['message']);
    }

    public function test_report_writes_server_errors_to_the_log_creating_the_directory(): void
    {
        $exception = new RuntimeException('boom');

        $this->handler->report($exception);

        $this->assertFileExists($this->logPath());
        $log = (string) file_get_contents($this->logPath());
        $expectedLine = sprintf('RuntimeException: boom in %s:%d', __FILE__, $exception->getLine());
        $this->assertMatchesRegularExpression('/^\[\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2} \d{2}:\d{2}:\d{2}\] /', $log);
        $this->assertStringContainsString($expectedLine, $log);
        $this->assertStringContainsString('#0 ', $log);
    }

    public function test_report_appends_to_an_existing_log(): void
    {
        $this->handler->report(new RuntimeException('first'));
        $this->handler->report(new RuntimeException('second'));

        $log = (string) file_get_contents($this->logPath());
        $this->assertStringContainsString('RuntimeException: first', $log);
        $this->assertStringContainsString('RuntimeException: second', $log);
    }

    public function test_report_logs_http_exceptions_with_status_500_or_more(): void
    {
        $this->handler->report(new HttpException(503, 'Down for maintenance'));

        $this->assertStringContainsString(
            'Kanin\Http\HttpException: Down for maintenance',
            (string) file_get_contents($this->logPath()),
        );
    }

    public function test_report_skips_client_errors_and_validation_errors(): void
    {
        $this->handler->report(new NotFoundHttpException());
        $this->handler->report(new HttpException(401, 'Unauthenticated.'));
        $this->handler->report(new ValidationException(['name' => ['Required.']]));

        $this->assertFileDoesNotExist($this->logPath());
    }

    public function test_report_never_throws_when_the_log_cannot_be_written(): void
    {
        // "storage" is a file, so storage/logs can never be created.
        file_put_contents($this->basePath . '/storage', 'not a directory');

        $this->handler->report(new RuntimeException('boom'));

        $this->assertFileDoesNotExist($this->logPath());
    }

    /**
     * PHPUnit lowers error_reporting() while a test runs, so these tests set
     * E_ALL themselves and always restore both the level and the handler.
     */
    public function test_registered_error_handler_turns_warnings_into_exceptions(): void
    {
        $previousLevel = error_reporting(E_ALL);
        Handler::registerErrorHandler();

        try {
            trigger_error('Careful!', E_USER_WARNING);
            $this->fail('Expected an ErrorException.');
        } catch (ErrorException $e) {
            $this->assertSame('Careful!', $e->getMessage());
            $this->assertSame(E_USER_WARNING, $e->getSeverity());
            $this->assertSame(__FILE__, $e->getFile());
        } finally {
            restore_error_handler();
            error_reporting($previousLevel);
        }
    }

    public function test_registered_error_handler_ignores_silenced_errors(): void
    {
        $previousLevel = error_reporting(E_ALL);
        Handler::registerErrorHandler();

        try {
            $result = @trigger_error('Quiet please', E_USER_NOTICE);
        } finally {
            restore_error_handler();
            error_reporting($previousLevel);
        }

        $this->assertTrue($result);
        $this->assertSame('Quiet please', error_get_last()['message'] ?? null);
    }

    public function test_registered_error_handler_ignores_levels_outside_error_reporting(): void
    {
        $previousLevel = error_reporting(E_ALL & ~E_USER_DEPRECATED);
        Handler::registerErrorHandler();

        try {
            $result = trigger_error('Old API', E_USER_DEPRECATED);
        } finally {
            restore_error_handler();
            error_reporting($previousLevel);
        }

        $this->assertTrue($result);
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Create the first version of Application**

The handler's constructor needs an `Application` (for `basePath()` and `isDebug()`), but the full Application arrives in Task 11. Create this minimal version now; Task 11 overwrites the whole file and must keep these four members behaving identically (its `isDebug()` may use the `config()` helper — it resolves the same `Config` instance from the container, so `HandlerTest` keeps passing).

Create `system/Application.php`:

```php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace Kanin;

use Kanin\Support\Config;

/**
 * The application: the service container plus knowledge of where the project lives.
 *
 * This first version only holds the pieces the exception handler needs
 * (base path and debug flag). Task 11 replaces this file with the full
 * Application (boot, handle, run).
 */
class Application extends Container
{
    public const VERSION = '1.0.0';

    private string $basePath;

    /**
     * Create the application for the project rooted at $basePath.
     *
     * @param string $basePath Absolute path of the project root folder.
     */
    public function __construct(string $basePath)
    {
        $this->basePath = rtrim(str_replace('\\', '/', $basePath), '/');

        static::setInstance($this);
        $this->instance(Application::class, $this);
        $this->instance(Container::class, $this);
    }

    /**
     * Build an absolute path inside the project, e.g. basePath('config').
     *
     * @param string $path A path relative to the project root ('' for the root itself).
     */
    public function basePath(string $path = ''): string
    {
        $path = ltrim(str_replace('\\', '/', $path), '/');

        return $path === '' ? $this->basePath : $this->basePath . '/' . $path;
    }

    /**
     * Whether detailed error output is enabled (config "app.debug").
     */
    public function isDebug(): bool
    {
        return (bool) $this->make(Config::class)->get('app.debug', false);
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Implement the Handler**

Create `system/Exceptions/Handler.php`:

```php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace Kanin\Exceptions;

use ErrorException;
use Kanin\Application;
use Kanin\Http\HttpException;
use Kanin\Http\JsonResponse;
use Kanin\Http\Request;
use Kanin\Validation\ValidationException;
use Throwable;

/**
 * Turns every error into a JSON response and writes server errors to a log file.
 *
 * - ValidationException → 422 {"message": ..., "errors": {...}}
 * - HttpException       → its own status, e.g. 404 {"message": "Not Found"}
 * - anything else       → 500 {"message": "Server Error"}
 *
 * With APP_DEBUG=true a 500 also shows the real message, file, line and trace.
 * Never enable debug in production: it reveals your code to strangers.
 */
class Handler
{
    /** Standard reason phrases, used when an HttpException has no message. */
    private const REASON_PHRASES = [
        400 => 'Bad Request',
        401 => 'Unauthorized',
        403 => 'Forbidden',
        404 => 'Not Found',
        405 => 'Method Not Allowed',
        406 => 'Not Acceptable',
        408 => 'Request Timeout',
        409 => 'Conflict',
        410 => 'Gone',
        413 => 'Content Too Large',
        415 => 'Unsupported Media Type',
        422 => 'Unprocessable Content',
        429 => 'Too Many Requests',
        500 => 'Internal Server Error',
        501 => 'Not Implemented',
        502 => 'Bad Gateway',
        503 => 'Service Unavailable',
        504 => 'Gateway Timeout',
    ];

    /**
     * Create the handler.
     *
     * @param Application $app Used for the log path and the debug flag.
     */
    public function __construct(private readonly Application $app)
    {
    }

    /**
     * Write a server error to storage/logs/kanin.log.
     *
     * Client errors (4xx HttpException, ValidationException) are expected and
     * are not logged. This method never throws: a broken log file must not
     * hide the original error from the user.
     */
    public function report(Throwable $e): void
    {
        if (! $this->shouldReport($e)) {
            return;
        }

        try {
            $this->writeLog($this->formatLogEntry($e));
        } catch (Throwable) {
            // Logging failed (disk full, no permission...). Nothing more we can do.
        }
    }

    /**
     * Convert any exception into a JSON response.
     *
     * @param Request   $request The request that failed (available to subclasses).
     * @param Throwable $e       The error to render.
     */
    public function render(Request $request, Throwable $e): JsonResponse
    {
        return match (true) {
            $e instanceof ValidationException => new JsonResponse(
                ['message' => $e->getMessage(), 'errors' => $e->errors()],
                $e->getStatusCode(),
            ),
            $e instanceof HttpException => $this->renderHttpException($e),
            default => $this->renderServerError($e),
        };
    }

    /**
     * Make PHP warnings and notices throw an ErrorException instead of
     * printing text into your JSON. Errors silenced with @ are left alone.
     */
    public static function registerErrorHandler(): void
    {
        set_error_handler(static function (int $severity, string $message, string $file = '', int $line = 0): bool {
            if ((error_reporting() & $severity) === 0) {
                return false;
            }

            throw new ErrorException($message, 0, $severity, $file, $line);
        });
    }

    private function shouldReport(Throwable $e): bool
    {
        if ($e instanceof ValidationException) {
            return false;
        }

        return ! ($e instanceof HttpException && $e->getStatusCode() < 500);
    }

    private function renderHttpException(HttpException $e): JsonResponse
    {
        $status = $e->getStatusCode();
        $message = $e->getMessage() !== '' ? $e->getMessage() : $this->reasonPhrase($status);

        return new JsonResponse(['message' => $this->clean($message)], $status, $e->getHeaders());
    }

    private function renderServerError(Throwable $e): JsonResponse
    {
        if (! $this->app->isDebug()) {
            return new JsonResponse(['message' => 'Server Error'], 500);
        }

        return new JsonResponse([
            'message' => $this->clean($e->getMessage()),
            'exception' => $e::class,
            'file' => $this->clean($e->getFile()),
            'line' => $e->getLine(),
            'trace' => array_map($this->clean(...), explode("\n", $e->getTraceAsString())),
        ], 500);
    }

    private function reasonPhrase(int $status): string
    {
        return self::REASON_PHRASES[$status] ?? ($status >= 500 ? 'Server Error' : 'Error');
    }

    /**
     * Replace invalid UTF-8 bytes so json_encode() can never fail on a message.
     */
    private function clean(string $value): string
    {
        return mb_scrub($value, 'UTF-8');
    }

    private function formatLogEntry(Throwable $e): string
    {
        return sprintf(
            "[%s] %s: %s in %s:%d\n%s\n\n",
            date('Y-m-d H:i:s'),
            $e::class,
            $e->getMessage(),
            $e->getFile(),
            $e->getLine(),
            $e->getTraceAsString(),
        );
    }

    private function writeLog(string $entry): void
    {
        $path = $this->app->basePath('storage/logs/kanin.log');
        $directory = dirname($path);

        if (! is_dir($directory)) {
            @mkdir($directory, 0775, true);
        }

        @file_put_contents($path, $entry, FILE_APPEND | LOCK_EX);
    }
}
```

```php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace Tests\Unit\Support;

use Kanin\Container;
use Kanin\Database\Connection;
use Kanin\Database\QueryBuilder;
use Kanin\Http\HttpException;
use Kanin\Http\JsonResponse;
use Kanin\Http\NotFoundHttpException;
use Kanin\Http\Request;
use Kanin\Routing\Router;
use Kanin\Support\Config;
use PHPUnit\Framework\TestCase;

final class HelpersTest extends TestCase
{
    private Container $container;

    protected function setUp(): void
    {
        $this->container = new Container();
        Container::setInstance($this->container);
        $this->container->instance(Config::class, new Config([
            'app' => ['name' => 'Helper App', 'url' => 'http://example.test/'],
        ]));
    }

    protected function tearDown(): void
    {
        Container::setInstance(null);
        unset($_ENV['KANIN_HELPER_FLAG']);
    }

    public function test_app_returns_the_container_or_a_service(): void
    {
        $this->assertSame($this->container, app());
        $this->assertSame($this->container->make(Config::class), app(Config::class));
    }

    public function test_app_passes_parameters_to_make(): void
    {
        $fresh = app(\ArrayObject::class, ['array' => ['a' => 1]]);
        $this->assertSame(['a' => 1], $fresh->getArrayCopy());
    }

    public function test_config_reads_dot_notation_with_default(): void
    {
        $this->assertSame('Helper App', config('app.name'));
        $this->assertSame('fallback', config('app.missing', 'fallback'));
        $this->assertSame($this->container->make(Config::class), config());
    }

    public function test_env_reads_and_casts_environment_values(): void
    {
        $_ENV['KANIN_HELPER_FLAG'] = 'true';

        $this->assertTrue(env('KANIN_HELPER_FLAG'));
        $this->assertSame('default', env('KANIN_HELPER_MISSING', 'default'));
    }

    public function test_response_builds_a_json_response(): void
    {
        $response = response(['id' => 1], 201, ['X-Test' => 'yes']);

        $this->assertInstanceOf(JsonResponse::class, $response);
        $this->assertSame(201, $response->getStatusCode());
        $this->assertSame('{"id":1}', $response->getContent());
        $this->assertSame('yes', $response->getHeader('X-Test'));
    }

    public function test_response_with_no_arguments_is_json_null_200(): void
    {
        $response = response();

        $this->assertSame(200, $response->getStatusCode());
        $this->assertSame('null', $response->getContent());
    }

    public function test_route_builds_a_named_route_url(): void
    {
        $router = new Router($this->container);
        $this->container->instance(Router::class, $router);
        $router->get('/users/{id}', fn () => null)->name('users.show');

        $this->assertSame('/users/5', route('users.show', ['id' => 5]));
    }

    public function test_route_with_unknown_name_throws(): void
    {
        $this->container->instance(Router::class, new Router($this->container));

        $this->expectException(\InvalidArgumentException::class);

        route('nope');
    }

    public function test_url_joins_the_app_url_and_a_path(): void
    {
        $this->assertSame('http://example.test', url());
        $this->assertSame('http://example.test/api/users', url('/api/users'));
        $this->assertSame('http://example.test/api', url('api'));
    }

    public function test_request_returns_the_current_request_or_an_input_value(): void
    {
        $request = Request::create('/users?page=2');
        $this->container->instance(Request::class, $request);

        $this->assertSame($request, request());
        $this->assertSame('2', request('page'));
        $this->assertSame(15, request('per_page', 15));
    }

    public function test_db_returns_the_connection_or_a_query_builder(): void
    {
        $connection = new Connection(['driver' => 'sqlite', 'database' => ':memory:']);
        $this->container->instance(Connection::class, $connection);

        $this->assertSame($connection, db());
        $this->assertInstanceOf(QueryBuilder::class, db('users'));
    }

    public function test_abort_404_throws_not_found(): void
    {
        try {
            abort(404);
            $this->fail('Expected NotFoundHttpException.');
        } catch (NotFoundHttpException $e) {
            $this->assertSame(404, $e->getStatusCode());
            $this->assertSame('Not Found', $e->getMessage());
        }
    }

    public function test_abort_404_keeps_a_custom_message(): void
    {
        $this->expectException(NotFoundHttpException::class);
        $this->expectExceptionMessage('User not found.');

        abort(404, 'User not found.');
    }

    public function test_abort_other_codes_throw_http_exception_with_headers(): void
    {
        try {
            abort(403, 'Forbidden.', ['X-Reason' => 'policy']);
            $this->fail('Expected HttpException.');
        } catch (HttpException $e) {
            $this->assertNotInstanceOf(NotFoundHttpException::class, $e);
            $this->assertSame(403, $e->getStatusCode());
            $this->assertSame('Forbidden.', $e->getMessage());
            $this->assertSame(['X-Reason' => 'policy'], $e->getHeaders());
        }
    }

    public function test_dd_dumps_every_value_and_exits_with_code_1(): void
    {
        $autoload = dirname(__DIR__, 3) . '/vendor/autoload.php';
        $code = 'require ' . var_export($autoload, true) . "; dd('first', 42);";
        $output = [];
        $exitCode = null;

        exec(escapeshellarg(PHP_BINARY) . ' -r ' . escapeshellarg($code) . ' 2>&1', $output, $exitCode);

        $text = implode("\n", $output);
        $this->assertSame(1, $exitCode);
        $this->assertStringContainsString('string(5) "first"', $text);
        $this->assertStringContainsString('int(42)', $text);
    }

    public function test_every_helper_is_defined(): void
    {
        foreach (['app', 'config', 'env', 'response', 'route', 'url', 'request', 'db', 'abort', 'dd'] as $function) {
            $this->assertTrue(function_exists($function), "Helper [{$function}] is missing.");
        }
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the helpers**

Replace the whole of `system/Support/helpers.php` with (it is already listed in composer.json `autoload.files` by Task 1, so no `dump-autoload` is needed):

```php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

/*
 * Global helper functions, loaded automatically by Composer.
 *
 * They are small shortcuts to services in the container, so that
 * `config('app.name')` reads better than
 * `Container::getInstance()->make(Config::class)->get('app.name')`.
 *
 * Each one is wrapped in `function_exists` so an app can define its own
 * version first if it really needs to.
 */

use Kanin\Container;
use Kanin\Database\Connection;
use Kanin\Database\QueryBuilder;
use Kanin\Http\HttpException;
use Kanin\Http\JsonResponse;
use Kanin\Http\NotFoundHttpException;
use Kanin\Http\Request;
use Kanin\Routing\Router;
use Kanin\Support\Config;
use Kanin\Support\Env;

if (! function_exists('app')) {
    /**
     * Get the container, or build a service from it: app(Router::class).
     */
    function app(?string $abstract = null, array $parameters = []): mixed
    {
        $container = Container::getInstance();

        return $abstract === null ? $container : $container->make($abstract, $parameters);
    }
}

if (! function_exists('config')) {
    /**
     * Read a config value with dot notation: config('app.name', 'Default').
     * With no key, returns the Config object itself.
     */
    function config(?string $key = null, mixed $default = null): mixed
    {
        $config = app(Config::class);

        return $key === null ? $config : $config->get($key, $default);
    }
}

if (! function_exists('env')) {
    /**
     * Read an environment variable (from .env or the server): env('APP_DEBUG', false).
     * Use it inside config/*.php files, and config() everywhere else.
     */
    function env(string $key, mixed $default = null): mixed
    {
        return Env::get($key, $default);
    }
}

if (! function_exists('response')) {
    /**
     * Build a JSON response: return response($user, 201);
     */
    function response(mixed $data = null, int $status = 200, array $headers = []): JsonResponse
    {
        return new JsonResponse($data, $status, $headers);
    }
}

if (! function_exists('route')) {
    /**
     * Build the URL path of a named route: route('users.show', ['id' => 1]) → '/api/users/1'.
     */
    function route(string $name, array $parameters = []): string
    {
        return app(Router::class)->url($name, $parameters);
    }
}

if (! function_exists('url')) {
    /**
     * Build a full URL from APP_URL: url('api/users') → 'http://localhost:8000/api/users'.
     */
    function url(string $path = ''): string
    {
        $base = rtrim((string) config('app.url', ''), '/');

        return $path === '' ? $base : $base . '/' . ltrim($path, '/');
    }
}

if (! function_exists('request')) {
    /**
     * Get the current Request, or one input value from it: request('page', 1).
     */
    function request(?string $key = null, mixed $default = null): mixed
    {
        $request = app(Request::class);

        return $key === null ? $request : $request->input($key, $default);
    }
}

if (! function_exists('db')) {
    /**
     * Get the database Connection, or start a query on a table: db('users')->where('id', 1)->first().
     */
    function db(?string $table = null): Connection|QueryBuilder
    {
        $connection = app(Connection::class);

        return $table === null ? $connection : $connection->table($table);
    }
}

if (! function_exists('abort')) {
    /**
     * Stop the request with an HTTP error: abort(403, 'Forbidden.').
     * The exception handler turns it into a JSON error response.
     */
    function abort(int $code, string $message = '', array $headers = []): never
    {
        if ($code === 404 && $headers === []) {
            throw new NotFoundHttpException($message === '' ? 'Not Found' : $message);
        }

        throw new HttpException($code, $message, $headers);
    }
}

if (! function_exists('dd')) {
    /**
     * "Dump and die": print each value with var_dump, then stop the script.
     * Handy while debugging; never leave it in committed code.
     */
    function dd(mixed ...$vars): never
    {
        foreach ($vars as $var) {
            var_dump($var);
        }

        exit(1);
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Write the failing config files test**

Create `tests/Unit/Support/ConfigFilesTest.php`. It loads each real `config/*.php` with controlled environment variables and restores the environment afterwards (phpunit.xml forces some of these variables, so the test must put them back):

```php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace Tests\Unit\Support;

use Kanin\Middleware\AuthenticateToken;
use Kanin\Middleware\HandleCors;
use PHPUnit\Framework\TestCase;

final class ConfigFilesTest extends TestCase
{
    /**
     * Require config/<name>.php with the given environment variables.
     * A null value means "not set at all". The real environment is restored afterwards.
     */
    private function loadConfig(string $name, array $env = []): array
    {
        $backup = [];

        foreach ($env as $key => $value) {
            $backup[$key] = [$_ENV[$key] ?? null, $_SERVER[$key] ?? null, getenv($key)];
            unset($_ENV[$key], $_SERVER[$key]);
            putenv($key);

            if ($value !== null) {
                $_ENV[$key] = $value;
            }
        }

        try {
            return require dirname(__DIR__, 3) . "/config/{$name}.php";
        } finally {
            foreach ($backup as $key => [$envValue, $serverValue, $processValue]) {
                unset($_ENV[$key], $_SERVER[$key]);

                if ($envValue !== null) {
                    $_ENV[$key] = $envValue;
                }

                if ($serverValue !== null) {
                    $_SERVER[$key] = $serverValue;
                }

                putenv($processValue === false ? $key : "{$key}={$processValue}");
            }
        }
    }

    public function test_app_config_defaults(): void
    {
        $config = $this->loadConfig('app', [
            'APP_NAME' => null, 'APP_ENV' => null, 'APP_DEBUG' => null, 'APP_URL' => null, 'APP_TIMEZONE' => null,
        ]);

        $this->assertSame([
            'name' => 'KaninPHP',
            'env' => 'production',
            'debug' => false,
            'url' => 'http://localhost:8000',
            'timezone' => 'UTC',
            'middleware' => [HandleCors::class],
            'middleware_aliases' => ['auth.token' => AuthenticateToken::class],
        ], $config);
    }

    public function test_app_config_reads_the_environment(): void
    {
        $config = $this->loadConfig('app', [
            'APP_NAME' => 'My API', 'APP_ENV' => 'local', 'APP_DEBUG' => 'true',
            'APP_URL' => 'https://api.example.com', 'APP_TIMEZONE' => 'Asia/Manila',
        ]);

        $this->assertSame('My API', $config['name']);
        $this->assertSame('local', $config['env']);
        $this->assertTrue($config['debug']);
        $this->assertSame('https://api.example.com', $config['url']);
        $this->assertSame('Asia/Manila', $config['timezone']);
    }

    public function test_app_debug_false_string_is_false(): void
    {
        $this->assertFalse($this->loadConfig('app', ['APP_DEBUG' => 'false'])['debug']);
    }

    public function test_database_config_defaults_to_the_sqlite_file(): void
    {
        $config = $this->loadConfig('database', ['DB_CONNECTION' => null, 'DB_DATABASE' => null]);

        $this->assertSame('sqlite', $config['default']);
        $this->assertSame([
            'driver' => 'sqlite',
            'database' => dirname(__DIR__, 3) . '/database/database.sqlite',
        ], $config['connections']['sqlite']);
    }

    public function test_database_config_reads_the_environment(): void
    {
        $config = $this->loadConfig('database', ['DB_CONNECTION' => 'sqlite', 'DB_DATABASE' => ':memory:']);

        $this->assertSame(':memory:', $config['connections']['sqlite']['database']);
    }

    public function test_database_config_server_defaults(): void
    {
        $config = $this->loadConfig('database', [
            'DB_HOST' => null, 'DB_PORT' => null, 'DB_DATABASE' => null, 'DB_USERNAME' => null, 'DB_PASSWORD' => null,
        ]);

        $this->assertSame([
            'driver' => 'mysql', 'host' => '127.0.0.1', 'port' => 3306, 'database' => 'kanin',
            'username' => 'root', 'password' => '', 'charset' => 'utf8mb4',
        ], $config['connections']['mysql']);
        $this->assertSame(5432, $config['connections']['pgsql']['port']);
        $this->assertSame('postgres', $config['connections']['pgsql']['username']);
        $this->assertSame('pgsql', $config['connections']['pgsql']['driver']);
    }

    public function test_cors_config_defaults(): void
    {
        $this->assertSame([
            'allowed_origins' => ['*'],
            'allowed_methods' => ['GET', 'POST', 'PUT', 'PATCH', 'DELETE', 'OPTIONS'],
            'allowed_headers' => ['Content-Type', 'Authorization', 'Accept', 'X-Requested-With'],
            'exposed_headers' => [],
            'max_age' => 600,
            'supports_credentials' => false,
        ], $this->loadConfig('cors', ['CORS_ALLOWED_ORIGINS' => null]));
    }

    public function test_cors_origins_are_comma_separated_and_trimmed(): void
    {
        $config = $this->loadConfig('cors', ['CORS_ALLOWED_ORIGINS' => ' https://a.test , ,https://b.test ']);

        $this->assertSame(['https://a.test', 'https://b.test'], $config['allowed_origins']);
    }

    public function test_auth_tokens_are_comma_separated_trimmed_and_non_empty(): void
    {
        $this->assertSame(['abc', 'def'], $this->loadConfig('auth', ['API_TOKENS' => ' abc , ,def, '])['tokens']);
    }

    public function test_auth_tokens_default_to_an_empty_list_which_denies_everything(): void
    {
        $this->assertSame([], $this->loadConfig('auth', ['API_TOKENS' => null])['tokens']);
        $this->assertSame([], $this->loadConfig('auth', ['API_TOKENS' => ''])['tokens']);
    }
}
```

Create `config/app.php`:

```php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

use Kanin\Middleware\AuthenticateToken;
use Kanin\Middleware\HandleCors;

return [
    // The application name, returned by GET /api.
    'name' => env('APP_NAME', 'KaninPHP'),

    // "local", "testing" or "production".
    'env' => env('APP_ENV', 'production'),

    // Show exception details in error responses. NEVER true in production.
    'debug' => (bool) env('APP_DEBUG', false),

    // Public base URL, used by the url() helper.
    'url' => env('APP_URL', 'http://localhost:8000'),

    // Default timezone for date() and friends.
    'timezone' => env('APP_TIMEZONE', 'UTC'),

    // Global middleware: runs on every request, first listed runs outermost.
    'middleware' => [
        HandleCors::class,
    ],

    // Short names for route middleware: ->middleware('auth.token').
    'middleware_aliases' => [
        'auth.token' => AuthenticateToken::class,
    ],
];
```

Create `config/database.php` (the SQLite path uses `?:` so an empty `DB_DATABASE=` line in `.env` still falls back to the default file; the `pgsql` charset is `utf8` because PostgreSQL has no `utf8mb4`):

```php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

return [
    // Which connection below to use: "sqlite", "mysql" or "pgsql".
    'default' => env('DB_CONNECTION', 'sqlite'),

    'connections' => [
        'sqlite' => [
            'driver' => 'sqlite',
            // A file path, or ":memory:" for a throw-away database.
            'database' => env('DB_DATABASE') ?: dirname(__DIR__) . '/database/database.sqlite',
        ],

        'mysql' => [
            'driver' => 'mysql',
            'host' => env('DB_HOST', '127.0.0.1'),
            'port' => (int) env('DB_PORT', 3306),
            'database' => env('DB_DATABASE', 'kanin'),
            'username' => env('DB_USERNAME', 'root'),
            'password' => env('DB_PASSWORD', ''),
            'charset' => 'utf8mb4',
        ],

        'pgsql' => [
            'driver' => 'pgsql',
            'host' => env('DB_HOST', '127.0.0.1'),
            'port' => (int) env('DB_PORT', 5432),
            'database' => env('DB_DATABASE', 'kanin'),
            'username' => env('DB_USERNAME', 'postgres'),
            'password' => env('DB_PASSWORD', ''),
            'charset' => 'utf8',
        ],
    ],
];
```

```php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

return [
    // Origins allowed to call the API from a browser. "*" means any origin.
    // Set CORS_ALLOWED_ORIGINS=https://app.example.com,https://admin.example.com to restrict.
    'allowed_origins' => array_values(array_filter(array_map('trim', explode(',', (string) env('CORS_ALLOWED_ORIGINS', '*'))))),

    'allowed_methods' => ['GET', 'POST', 'PUT', 'PATCH', 'DELETE', 'OPTIONS'],

    'allowed_headers' => ['Content-Type', 'Authorization', 'Accept', 'X-Requested-With'],

    // Response headers the browser's JavaScript is allowed to read.
    'exposed_headers' => [],

    // How long (seconds) a browser may cache a preflight response.
    'max_age' => 600,

    // Allow cookies/credentials. Keep false for token-based APIs.
    'supports_credentials' => false,
];
```

- [ ] **Step 9: Write the test middleware fixtures**

Create `tests/Unit/Core/Fixtures/AddHeaderMiddleware.php`:

```php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace Tests\Unit\Core\Fixtures;

use Kanin\Http\Middleware;
use Kanin\Http\Request;
use Kanin\Http\Response;

/**
 * Test middleware: adds "X-Fixture: seen" to whatever response comes back.
 */
final class AddHeaderMiddleware implements Middleware
{
    public function handle(Request $request, \Closure $next): Response
    {
        return $next($request)->header('X-Fixture', 'seen');
    }
}
```

```php
<?php

use Kanin\Http\Middleware;

final class ThrowingMiddleware implements Middleware
{
    public function handle(Request $request, \Closure $next): Response

    }
}
```
