- [ ] **Step 10: Write the failing Application test**

Create `tests/Unit/Core/ApplicationTest.php`. Each test builds a throw-away project folder in the system temp dir with its own `config/` and `routes/api.php`. `tearDown()` pops the error handler that `boot()` installs (PHPUnit 12 with `failOnRisky` would otherwise flag the test), restores the timezone, and resets the global container.

```php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace Tests\Unit\Core;

use Kanin\Application;
use Kanin\Container;
use Kanin\Database\Connection;
use Kanin\Exceptions\Handler;
use Kanin\Http\Request;
use Kanin\Http\Response;
use Kanin\Routing\Router;
use Kanin\Support\Config;
use PHPUnit\Framework\TestCase;
use Tests\Unit\Core\Fixtures\AddHeaderMiddleware;
use Tests\Unit\Core\Fixtures\ThrowingMiddleware;

final class ApplicationTest extends TestCase
{
    private string $root;

    private mixed $originalErrorHandler;

    private string $originalTimezone;

    protected function setUp(): void
    {
        $this->root = str_replace('\\', '/', sys_get_temp_dir()) . '/kanin-app-' . bin2hex(random_bytes(6));
        mkdir($this->root . '/config', 0777, true);

        $this->writeConfig('app', ['name' => 'Test App', 'debug' => false, 'timezone' => 'UTC', 'middleware' => [], 'middleware_aliases' => []]);
        $this->writeConfig('database', ['default' => 'sqlite', 'connections' => ['sqlite' => ['driver' => 'sqlite', 'database' => ':memory:']]]);

        $this->originalErrorHandler = $this->currentErrorHandler();
        $this->originalTimezone = date_default_timezone_get();
    }

    protected function tearDown(): void
    {
        // boot() installs Handler's error handler; remove it so PHPUnit does not flag the test as risky.
        for ($i = 0; $i < 10 && $this->currentErrorHandler() !== $this->originalErrorHandler; $i++) {
            restore_error_handler();
        }

        date_default_timezone_set($this->originalTimezone);
        unset($_ENV['KANIN_APP_TEST_NAME'], $_SERVER['KANIN_APP_TEST_NAME']);
        putenv('KANIN_APP_TEST_NAME');
        Container::setInstance(null);
        $this->deleteDirectory($this->root);
    }

    public function test_version_is_1_0_0(): void
    {
        $this->assertSame('1.0.0', Application::VERSION);
    }

    public function test_base_path_is_normalized(): void
    {
        $app = new Application('C:\\Projects\\Kanin\\');

        $this->assertSame('C:/Projects/Kanin', $app->basePath());
        $this->assertSame('C:/Projects/Kanin/config', $app->basePath('config'));
        $this->assertSame('C:/Projects/Kanin/routes/api.php', $app->basePath('/routes/api.php'));
    }

    public function test_constructor_registers_the_app_as_the_global_container(): void
    {
        $app = new Application($this->root);

        $this->assertInstanceOf(Container::class, $app);
        $this->assertSame($app, Container::getInstance());
        $this->assertSame($app, $app->make(Application::class));
        $this->assertSame($app, $app->make(Container::class));
    }

    public function test_boot_loads_the_env_file_before_the_config_files(): void
    {
        file_put_contents($this->root . '/.env', "KANIN_APP_TEST_NAME=FromDotEnv\n");
        $this->writeFile('config/app.php', "<?php\n\nreturn ['name' => env('KANIN_APP_TEST_NAME', 'fallback')];\n");

        $app = $this->bootedApp();

        $this->assertSame('FromDotEnv', $app->make(Config::class)->get('app.name'));
    }

    public function test_boot_sets_the_configured_timezone(): void
    {
        $this->writeConfig('app', ['timezone' => 'Asia/Manila']);

        $this->bootedApp();

        $this->assertSame('Asia/Manila', date_default_timezone_get());
    }

    public function test_boot_defaults_the_timezone_to_utc(): void
    {
        $this->writeConfig('app', []);
        date_default_timezone_set('Asia/Tokyo');

        $this->bootedApp();

        $this->assertSame('UTC', date_default_timezone_get());
    }

    public function test_boot_registers_the_core_services_as_singletons(): void
    {
        $app = $this->bootedApp();

        $this->assertInstanceOf(Router::class, $app->make(Router::class));
        $this->assertSame($app->make(Router::class), $app->make(Router::class));
        $this->assertInstanceOf(Handler::class, $app->make(Handler::class));
        $this->assertSame($app->make(Handler::class), $app->make(Handler::class));
        $this->assertSame($app->make(Connection::class), $app->make(Connection::class));
        $this->assertSame('sqlite', $app->make(Connection::class)->getDriverName());
    }

    public function test_unconfigured_default_connection_throws_when_used(): void
    {
        $this->writeConfig('database', ['default' => 'oracle', 'connections' => []]);
        $app = $this->bootedApp();

        $this->expectException(\RuntimeException::class);
        $this->expectExceptionMessage('Database connection [oracle] is not configured.');

        $app->make(Connection::class);
    }

    public function test_boot_installs_the_exception_handlers_error_handler(): void
    {
        // PHPUnit lowers error_reporting() while a test runs, so we only check that
        // boot() pushed a new handler; HandlerTest covers what that handler does.
        $this->bootedApp();

        $this->assertNotSame($this->originalErrorHandler, $this->currentErrorHandler());
    }

    public function test_boot_only_runs_once(): void
    {
        $this->writeRoutes("\$router->get('/ping', fn () => ['pong' => true]);");
        $app = $this->bootedApp();
        $handlerAfterFirstBoot = $this->currentErrorHandler();

        $app->boot();

        $this->assertCount(1, $app->make(Router::class)->routes());
        $this->assertSame($handlerAfterFirstBoot, $this->currentErrorHandler());
    }

    public function test_routes_file_is_loaded_under_the_api_prefix(): void
    {
        $this->writeRoutes("\$router->get('/ping', fn () => ['pong' => true]);");
        $app = $this->bootedApp();

        $this->assertSame('/api/ping', array_values($app->make(Router::class)->routes())[0]->uri());

        $response = $app->handle(Request::create('/api/ping'));

        $this->assertSame(200, $response->getStatusCode());
        $this->assertSame(['pong' => true], json_decode($response->getContent(), true));
    }

    public function test_missing_routes_file_gives_json_404(): void
    {
        $app = new Application($this->root);

        $response = $app->handle(Request::create('/api'));

        $this->assertSame(404, $response->getStatusCode());
        $this->assertSame('application/json', $response->getHeader('Content-Type'));
        $this->assertSame(['message' => 'Not Found'], json_decode($response->getContent(), true));
    }

    public function test_middleware_aliases_from_config_are_registered(): void
    {
        $this->writeConfig('app', ['middleware_aliases' => ['fixture.header' => AddHeaderMiddleware::class]]);
        $this->writeRoutes("\$router->get('/ping', fn () => ['pong' => true])->middleware('fixture.header');");
        $app = new Application($this->root);

        $response = $app->handle(Request::create('/api/ping'));

        $this->assertSame(200, $response->getStatusCode());
        $this->assertSame('seen', $response->getHeader('X-Fixture'));
    }

    public function test_global_middleware_wraps_every_response_including_errors(): void
    {
        $this->writeConfig('app', ['middleware' => [AddHeaderMiddleware::class]]);
        $this->writeRoutes("\$router->get('/ping', fn () => ['pong' => true]);");
        $app = new Application($this->root);

        $ok = $app->handle(Request::create('/api/ping'));
        $missing = $app->handle(Request::create('/api/nope'));

        $this->assertSame('seen', $ok->getHeader('X-Fixture'));
        $this->assertSame(404, $missing->getStatusCode());
        $this->assertSame('seen', $missing->getHeader('X-Fixture'));
    }

    public function test_exception_in_production_is_logged_and_rendered_without_details(): void
    {
        $this->writeRoutes("\$router->get('/boom', fn () => throw new \\RuntimeException('secret detail'));");
        $app = new Application($this->root);

        $response = $app->handle(Request::create('/api/boom'));

        $this->assertFalse($app->isDebug());
        $this->assertSame(500, $response->getStatusCode());
        $this->assertSame(['message' => 'Server Error'], json_decode($response->getContent(), true));
        $this->assertStringContainsString('RuntimeException: secret detail', (string) file_get_contents($this->root . '/storage/logs/kanin.log'));
    }

    public function test_exception_in_debug_mode_shows_the_real_message(): void
    {
        $this->writeConfig('app', ['debug' => true]);
        $this->writeRoutes("\$router->get('/boom', fn () => throw new \\RuntimeException('secret detail'));");
        $app = new Application($this->root);

        $response = $app->handle(Request::create('/api/boom'));
        $body = json_decode($response->getContent(), true);

        $this->assertTrue($app->isDebug());
        $this->assertSame(500, $response->getStatusCode());
        $this->assertSame('secret detail', $body['message']);
        $this->assertSame(\RuntimeException::class, $body['exception']);
    }

    public function test_exception_thrown_by_global_middleware_is_rendered(): void
    {
        $this->writeConfig('app', ['middleware' => [ThrowingMiddleware::class]]);
        $app = new Application($this->root);

        $response = $app->handle(Request::create('/api'));

        $this->assertSame(418, $response->getStatusCode());
        $this->assertSame(['message' => 'I am a teapot.'], json_decode($response->getContent(), true));
    }

    public function test_failure_while_booting_is_rendered_as_a_generic_server_error(): void
    {
        $this->writeFile('config/broken.php', "<?php\n\nthrow new \\RuntimeException('config exploded');\n");
        $app = new Application($this->root);

        $response = $app->handle(Request::create('/api'));

        $this->assertSame(500, $response->getStatusCode());
        $this->assertSame(['message' => 'Server Error'], json_decode($response->getContent(), true));
    }

    public function test_handle_binds_the_current_request_in_the_container(): void
    {
        $app = new Application($this->root);
        $request = Request::create('/api/anything');

        $app->handle($request);

        $this->assertSame($request, $app->make(Request::class));
    }

    public function test_is_debug_is_false_before_boot_and_when_not_configured(): void
    {
        $this->writeConfig('app', []);
        $app = new Application($this->root);

        $this->assertFalse($app->isDebug());

        $app->boot();

        $this->assertFalse($app->isDebug());
    }

    public function test_run_captures_handles_and_sends_the_response(): void
    {
        $this->writeRoutes("\$router->get('/ping', fn () => ['pong' => true]);");
        $app = new Application($this->root);
        $server = $_SERVER;
        $_SERVER['REQUEST_METHOD'] = 'GET';
        $_SERVER['REQUEST_URI'] = '/api/ping';

        try {
            ob_start();
            $app->run();
            $output = (string) ob_get_clean();
        } finally {
            $_SERVER = $server;
        }

        $this->assertSame('{"pong":true}', $output);
    }

    private function bootedApp(): Application
    {
        $app = new Application($this->root);
        $app->boot();

        return $app;
    }

    private function writeConfig(string $name, array $values): void
    {
        $this->writeFile("config/{$name}.php", "<?php\n\nreturn " . var_export($values, true) . ";\n");
    }

    private function writeRoutes(string $code): void
    {
        $this->writeFile('routes/api.php', "<?php\n\n{$code}\n");
    }

    private function writeFile(string $relative, string $contents): void
    {
        $path = $this->root . '/' . $relative;

        if (! is_dir(dirname($path))) {
            mkdir(dirname($path), 0777, true);
        }

        file_put_contents($path, $contents);
    }

    private function currentErrorHandler(): mixed
    {
        $current = set_error_handler(null);
        restore_error_handler();

        return $current;
    }

    private function deleteDirectory(string $directory): void
    {
        if (! is_dir($directory)) {
            return;
        }

        foreach (scandir($directory) ?: [] as $item) {
            if ($item === '.' || $item === '..') {
                continue;
            }

            $path = $directory . '/' . $item;
            is_dir($path) ? $this->deleteDirectory($path) : unlink($path);
        }

        rmdir($directory);
    }
}

- [ ] **Step 12: Write the Application**

Replace the whole of `system/Application.php` (Task 10's minimal version) with the file below. Notes for the reader:
- `boot()` sets its "booted" flag first, so it runs at most once per Application, even if it fails half way (the failure is rendered as a 500 by `handle()`).
- `routes/api.php` is required inside a `static` closure: the file sees `$router` (and nothing else); use the helpers (`config()`, `app()`) for anything more.
- Errors from the router are rendered **inside** the global middleware pipeline, so global middleware such as CORS still decorates 404/405/422/500 responses. Errors thrown by `boot()` or by global middleware itself are rendered by the outer `catch`.
- If rendering the error fails too (for example the config folder could not be loaded), the response is a bare `{"message":"Server Error"}` 500 — never a PHP error page.

```php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace Kanin;

use Kanin\Database\Connection;
use Kanin\Exceptions\Handler;
use Kanin\Http\JsonResponse;
use Kanin\Http\Request;
use Kanin\Http\Response;
use Kanin\Routing\Pipeline;
use Kanin\Routing\Router;
use Kanin\Support\Config;
use Kanin\Support\Env;

/**
 * The heart of a KaninPHP app.
 *
 * It is the service container (so you can ask it for any service) and it
 * knows how to turn an HTTP Request into a Response:
 *
 *     $app = new Application(dirname(__DIR__));
 *     $app->run();   // capture the request, handle it, send the response
 */
class Application extends Container
{
    /** The framework version, shown by GET /api and the CLI. */
    public const VERSION = '1.0.0';

    /** Project root with forward slashes and no trailing slash. */
    private string $basePath;

    /** Has boot() already run? It must only run once. */
    private bool $booted = false;

    /**
     * Create the application for the project folder at $basePath.
     * The new app becomes the global container used by app() and config().
     */
    public function __construct(string $basePath)
    {
        $this->basePath = rtrim(str_replace('\\', '/', $basePath), '/');

        static::setInstance($this);
        $this->instance(Application::class, $this);
        $this->instance(Container::class, $this);
    }

    /**
     * Build an absolute path inside the project: basePath('config') → '<root>/config'.
     */
    public function basePath(string $path = ''): string
    {
        $path = ltrim(str_replace('\\', '/', $path), '/');

        return $path === '' ? $this->basePath : $this->basePath . '/' . $path;
    }

    /**
     * Load .env and config, register the core services and the API routes.
     * It runs at most once: calling it again does nothing.
     */
    public function boot(): void
    {
        if ($this->booted) {
            return;
        }

        $this->booted = true;

        Env::load($this->basePath('.env'));

        $config = Config::fromDirectory($this->basePath('config'));
        $this->instance(Config::class, $config);

        date_default_timezone_set((string) $config->get('app.timezone', 'UTC'));
        Handler::registerErrorHandler();

        $this->registerServices($config);
        $this->loadRoutes($config);
    }

    /**
     * Turn a Request into a Response. Global middleware (like CORS) wraps
     * the router, and every exception becomes a JSON error response.
     */
    public function handle(Request $request): Response
    {
        try {
            $this->boot();
            $this->instance(Request::class, $request);

            return (new Pipeline($this))
                ->send($request)
                ->through((array) $this->make(Config::class)->get('app.middleware', []))
                ->then(fn (Request $request): Response => $this->dispatchToRouter($request));
        } catch (\Throwable $e) {
            // Reached only when boot() or a global middleware itself throws.
            return $this->renderException($request, $e);
        }
    }

    /**
     * Handle the current HTTP request and send the response to the browser.
     * This is what public/index.php calls.
     */
    public function run(): void
    {
        $this->handle(Request::capture())->send();
    }

    /**
     * Is debug mode on (APP_DEBUG=true)? Never enable it in production:
     * error responses would show file paths and stack traces.
     */
    public function isDebug(): bool
    {
        return $this->has(Config::class) && (bool) $this->make(Config::class)->get('app.debug', false);
    }

    /**
     * Register the shared services every request needs.
     * Each one is created lazily, the first time somebody asks for it.
     */
    private function registerServices(Config $config): void
    {
        $this->singleton(Router::class, fn (): Router => new Router($this));

        $this->singleton(Connection::class, function () use ($config): Connection {
            $name = (string) $config->get('database.default', 'sqlite');
            $settings = $config->get("database.connections.{$name}");

            if (! is_array($settings)) {
                throw new \RuntimeException("Database connection [{$name}] is not configured.");
            }

            return new Connection($settings);
        });

        $this->singleton(Handler::class, fn (): Handler => new Handler($this));
    }

    /**
     * Register middleware aliases and load routes/api.php under the /api prefix.
     * Inside routes/api.php the variable $router is the Router.
     */
    private function loadRoutes(Config $config): void
    {
        $router = $this->make(Router::class);

        foreach ((array) $config->get('app.middleware_aliases', []) as $alias => $class) {
            $router->aliasMiddleware((string) $alias, (string) $class);
        }

        $routesFile = $this->basePath('routes/api.php');

        if (is_file($routesFile)) {
            $router->group(['prefix' => 'api'], static function (Router $router) use ($routesFile): void {
                require $routesFile;
            });
        }
    }

    /**
     * The end of the global middleware pipeline: let the router find and run the route.
     * Errors are rendered here so global middleware can still decorate them.
     */
    private function dispatchToRouter(Request $request): Response
    {
        try {
            return $this->make(Router::class)->dispatch($request);
        } catch (\Throwable $e) {
            return $this->renderException($request, $e);
        }
    }

    /**
     * Log the exception (when it matters) and convert it into a JSON response.
     * If even that fails, fall back to a plain 500 that reveals nothing.
     */
    private function renderException(Request $request, \Throwable $e): Response
    {
        try {
            $handler = $this->make(Handler::class);
            $handler->report($e);

            return $handler->render($request, $e);
        } catch (\Throwable) {
            return new JsonResponse(['message' => 'Server Error'], 500);
        }
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 14: Write the front controller**

Replace the whole of `public/index.php` (the old v0.0.1 file left in place by Task 1) with the file below. Under `php kanin serve` (PHP's built-in server, `PHP_SAPI === 'cli-server'`) it returns `false` for a real file inside `public/` so the server sends that file itself; `../` escapes, hidden dotfiles (`.htaccess`) and `index.php` itself always go through the application.

```php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

/*
 * KaninPHP front controller: every HTTP request enters the app here.
 *
 * With Apache, public/.htaccess sends every non-file request to this file.
 * With `php kanin serve` (PHP's built-in server) this file is the router
 * script, so it must hand real files (images, robots.txt...) back to the
 * server: returning false tells the built-in server "serve it yourself".
 */

use Kanin\Application;

if (PHP_SAPI === 'cli-server') {
    $publicDir = (string) realpath(__DIR__);
    $requested = realpath($publicDir . rawurldecode((string) parse_url($_SERVER['REQUEST_URI'] ?? '/', PHP_URL_PATH)));

    // Only plain files that really live inside public/: never "../" escapes,
    // never hidden dotfiles such as .htaccess, never this script itself.
    if (
        $requested !== false
        && is_file($requested)
        && ! str_starts_with(basename($requested), '.')
        && str_starts_with($requested, $publicDir . DIRECTORY_SEPARATOR)
        && $requested !== realpath(__FILE__)
    ) {
        return false;
    }
}

require dirname(__DIR__) . '/vendor/autoload.php';

(new Application(dirname(__DIR__)))->run();
```

sleep 2

curl -s -i http://127.0.0.1:8099/.htaccess
curl -s -i --path-as-is http://127.0.0.1:8099/../composer.json

The CORS middleware from Task 6 adds `Access-Control-Allow-Origin` only when an `Origin` header is sent, so it does not appear here.

- Global helper `response(mixed $data = null, int $status = 200, array $headers = []): JsonResponse` (Task 11) — used by `controller.api.stub`

Produces:
- `Kanin\Console\Kanin::__construct(Application $app, $output = null)` (`$output` is a stream resource, default `STDOUT`) and `run(array $argv): int` (`$argv[0]` is the script name, `$argv[1]` the command). Commands: `help` (default, also `--help`/`-h`), `serve [--host=127.0.0.1] [--port=8000]`, `routes`, `make:controller Name [--api]`, `make:model Name`, `make:middleware Name`, `db:seed`. Exit code 0 on success, 1 on any refusal or error; exceptions thrown inside a command are caught and printed as `Error: <message>` (exit 1).
- `protected function execute(string $command): int` — the only place that calls `passthru()`; tests subclass `Kanin` and override it to record the `serve` command instead of starting a server. `Kanin` is therefore not `final`.
- `serve` runs `escapeshellarg(PHP_BINARY) -S 'host:port' -t '<basePath>/public' '<basePath>/public/index.php'` (every piece escaped; absolute paths from `basePath()` so it works from any working directory) and returns the server's exit code.
- `db:seed` (Task 13/14 rely on it): refuses with `Seed file not found: database/seed.sql` when the file is missing; for the default connection with `driver` `sqlite` and a file path (not `:memory:`), creates the folder and empty file when missing; then runs the file through `Connection::unprepared()`; prints `Database seeded from database/seed.sql`.
- Generated files: `app/Controllers/<Name>.php`, `app/Models/<Name>.php`, `app/Middleware/<Name>.php`; stubs are read from `system/Console/stubs/` via `__DIR__` (so they work for any project root).
- The `kanin` script: `php kanin <command>` from the repo root (Task 14 smoke test uses `php kanin db:seed` and `php kanin serve`).

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing console test**

Create `tests/Unit/Console/KaninTest.php`. Each test gets a brand-new temporary project folder, so the generators never touch the real `app/` folder. Generated classes get unique names and are loaded with `require`, which proves every stub is valid, working PHP. The `serve` tests use a subclass that records the shell command instead of starting a server.

```php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace Tests\Unit\Console;

use Kanin\Application;
use Kanin\Console\Kanin;
use Kanin\Container;
use Kanin\Database\Connection;
use Kanin\Http\JsonResponse;
use Kanin\Http\Request;
use Kanin\Http\Response;
use Kanin\Routing\Router;
use Kanin\Support\Config;
use PHPUnit\Framework\Attributes\DataProvider;
use PHPUnit\Framework\TestCase;

final class KaninTest extends TestCase
{
    private string $basePath;

    private ?Application $app = null;

    /** @var resource */
    private $stream;

    protected function setUp(): void
    {
        parent::setUp();

        // Every test gets its own empty project folder, so generators never touch the real app/.
        $this->basePath = str_replace('\\', '/', sys_get_temp_dir()) . '/kanin-console-' . bin2hex(random_bytes(6));
        mkdir($this->basePath, 0777, true);

        $this->app = new Application($this->basePath);
        $this->stream = fopen('php://memory', 'w+');
    }

    protected function tearDown(): void
    {
        fclose($this->stream);
        Container::setInstance(null);
        $this->app = null;
        gc_collect_cycles(); // release SQLite file handles before deleting the folder (Windows)
        $this->removeDirectory($this->basePath);

        parent::tearDown();
    }

    public function test_help_is_the_default_command(): void
    {
        $this->assertSame(0, $this->kanin());

        $output = $this->printed();
        $this->assertStringContainsString('KaninPHP ' . Application::VERSION, $output);
        $this->assertStringContainsString('serve', $output);
        $this->assertStringContainsString('routes', $output);
        $this->assertStringContainsString('make:controller Name [--api]', $output);
        $this->assertStringContainsString('make:model Name', $output);
        $this->assertStringContainsString('make:middleware Name', $output);
        $this->assertStringContainsString('db:seed', $output);
    }

    #[DataProvider('helpAliases')]
    public function test_help_aliases_print_the_command_list(string $alias): void
    {
        $this->assertSame(0, $this->kanin($alias));
        $this->assertStringContainsString('Commands:', $this->printed());
    }

    public static function helpAliases(): array
    {
        return ['help' => ['help'], '--help' => ['--help'], '-h' => ['-h']];
    }

    public function test_unknown_command_fails(): void
    {
        $this->assertSame(1, $this->kanin('migrate'));
        $this->assertStringContainsString('Unknown command [migrate].', $this->printed());
    }

    public function test_make_controller_creates_the_class_from_the_stub(): void
    {
        $name = $this->uniqueName('Controller');

        $this->assertSame(0, $this->kanin('make:controller', $name));

        $relative = "app/Controllers/{$name}.php";
        $this->assertStringContainsString("Created {$relative}", $this->printed());
        $this->assertFileExists($this->basePath . '/' . $relative);

        $code = (string) file_get_contents($this->basePath . '/' . $relative);
        $this->assertStringStartsWith("<?php\n\ndeclare(strict_types=1);", $code);
        $this->assertStringContainsString('namespace App\Controllers;', $code);
        $this->assertStringContainsString("class {$name}", $code);
        $this->assertStringNotContainsString('{{ class }}', $code);

        require $this->basePath . '/' . $relative;
        $class = "App\\Controllers\\{$name}";
        $this->assertSame(['message' => "{$name} works!"], (new $class())->index(Request::create('/')));
    }

    public function test_make_controller_with_api_flag_creates_all_resource_actions(): void
    {
        $name = $this->uniqueName('Controller');

        $this->assertSame(0, $this->kanin('make:controller', $name, '--api'));

        $relative = "app/Controllers/{$name}.php";
        require $this->basePath . '/' . $relative;
        $class = "App\\Controllers\\{$name}";
        $controller = new $class();

        $this->assertSame(['data' => []], $controller->index(Request::create('/things')));
        $this->assertSame(['id' => 7], $controller->show(7));
        $this->assertSame(['id' => 7], $controller->update(Request::create('/things/7', 'PUT'), 7));

        $created = $controller->store(Request::create('/things', 'POST'));
        $this->assertInstanceOf(JsonResponse::class, $created);
        $this->assertSame(201, $created->getStatusCode());

        $deleted = $controller->destroy(7);
        $this->assertInstanceOf(JsonResponse::class, $deleted);
        $this->assertSame(204, $deleted->getStatusCode());
    }

    public function test_make_model_creates_a_model_class(): void
    {
        $name = $this->uniqueName('Model');

        $this->assertSame(0, $this->kanin('make:model', $name));
        $this->assertStringContainsString("Created app/Models/{$name}.php", $this->printed());

        require $this->basePath . "/app/Models/{$name}.php";
        $class = "App\\Models\\{$name}";
        $this->assertInstanceOf(\Kanin\Database\Model::class, new $class());
    }

    public function test_make_middleware_creates_a_pass_through_middleware(): void
    {
        $name = $this->uniqueName('Middleware');

        $this->assertSame(0, $this->kanin('make:middleware', $name));
        $this->assertStringContainsString("Created app/Middleware/{$name}.php", $this->printed());

        require $this->basePath . "/app/Middleware/{$name}.php";
        $class = "App\\Middleware\\{$name}";
        $middleware = new $class();
        $this->assertInstanceOf(\Kanin\Http\Middleware::class, $middleware);

        $response = $middleware->handle(Request::create('/'), fn (Request $request): Response => new Response('passed', 200));
        $this->assertSame('passed', $response->getContent());
    }

    public function test_generators_create_the_target_directory_when_missing(): void
    {
        $this->assertDirectoryDoesNotExist($this->basePath . '/app');

        $this->assertSame(0, $this->kanin('make:model', 'Invoice'));

        $this->assertDirectoryExists($this->basePath . '/app/Models');
        $this->assertFileExists($this->basePath . '/app/Models/Invoice.php');
    }

    public function test_generators_refuse_to_overwrite_an_existing_file(): void
    {
        mkdir($this->basePath . '/app/Controllers', 0777, true);
        file_put_contents($this->basePath . '/app/Controllers/PostController.php', 'original');

        $this->assertSame(1, $this->kanin('make:controller', 'PostController'));

        $this->assertStringContainsString('app/Controllers/PostController.php already exists.', $this->printed());
        $this->assertSame('original', file_get_contents($this->basePath . '/app/Controllers/PostController.php'));
    }

    #[DataProvider('invalidNames')]
    public function test_generators_reject_invalid_class_names(string $name): void
    {
        $this->assertSame(1, $this->kanin('make:controller', $name));

        $this->assertStringContainsString("Invalid name [{$name}].", $this->printed());
        $this->assertDirectoryDoesNotExist($this->basePath . '/app');
    }

    public static function invalidNames(): array
    {
        return [
            'lowercase first letter' => ['post'],
            'starts with a digit' => ['1Post'],
            'path traversal' => ['../Evil'],
            'sub folder' => ['Admin/Post'],
            'backslash' => ['Admin\\Post'],
            'dash' => ['Bad-Name'],
            'file extension' => ['Post.php'],
            'space' => ['Post Controller'],
        ];
    }

    #[DataProvider('generatorCommands')]
    public function test_generators_require_a_name(string $command): void
    {
        $this->assertSame(1, $this->kanin($command));
        $this->assertStringContainsString("Missing class name. Example: php kanin {$command} Name", $this->printed());
    }

    public static function generatorCommands(): array
    {
        return [
            'make:controller' => ['make:controller'],
            'make:model' => ['make:model'],
            'make:middleware' => ['make:middleware'],
        ];
    }

    public function test_routes_prints_a_table_of_every_route(): void
    {
        $router = $this->app->instance(Router::class, new Router($this->app));
        $router->get('/users', ['App\Controllers\UserController', 'index'])->name('users.index');
        $router->post('/users', fn () => null);
        $router->delete('/users/{id}', 'App\Controllers\UserController@destroy');

        $this->assertSame(0, $this->kanin('routes'));

        $lines = explode(PHP_EOL, trim($this->printed()));
        $this->assertMatchesRegularExpression('/^METHOD\s+\|\s+URI\s+\|\s+NAME\s+\|\s+ACTION$/', $lines[0]);
        $this->assertMatchesRegularExpression('/^-+-\+-+-\+-+-\+-+$/', $lines[1]);
        $this->assertMatchesRegularExpression('/^GET\s+\|\s+\/users\s+\|\s+users\.index\s+\|\s+App\\\\Controllers\\\\UserController@index$/', $lines[2]);
        $this->assertMatchesRegularExpression('/^POST\s+\|\s+\/users\s+\|\s+\|\s+Closure$/', $lines[3]);
        $this->assertMatchesRegularExpression('/^DELETE\s+\|\s+\/users\/\{id\}\s+\|\s+\|\s+App\\\\Controllers\\\\UserController@destroy$/', $lines[4]);
        $this->assertCount(5, $lines);
    }

    public function test_routes_joins_multiple_methods_with_a_pipe(): void
    {
        $router = $this->app->instance(Router::class, new Router($this->app));
        $router->match(['PUT', 'PATCH'], '/users/{id}', fn () => null);

        $this->assertSame(0, $this->kanin('routes'));
        $this->assertStringContainsString('PUT|PATCH', $this->printed());
    }

    public function test_routes_says_so_when_there_are_no_routes(): void
    {
        $this->app->instance(Router::class, new Router($this->app));

        $this->assertSame(0, $this->kanin('routes'));
        $this->assertStringContainsString('No routes registered.', $this->printed());
    }

    public function test_serve_starts_the_built_in_server_with_escaped_arguments(): void
    {
        $console = $this->recordingConsole();

        $this->assertSame(0, $console->run(['kanin', 'serve']));

        $expected = implode(' ', array_map('escapeshellarg', [
            PHP_BINARY,
            '-S',
            '127.0.0.1:8000',
            '-t',
            $this->basePath . '/public',
            $this->basePath . '/public/index.php',
        ]));
        $this->assertSame([$expected], $console->commands);
        $this->assertStringContainsString('http://127.0.0.1:8000', $this->printed());
    }

    public function test_serve_accepts_a_custom_host_and_port(): void
    {
        $console = $this->recordingConsole();

        $this->assertSame(0, $console->run(['kanin', 'serve', '--host=0.0.0.0', '--port=8080']));

        $this->assertCount(1, $console->commands);
        $this->assertStringContainsString(escapeshellarg('0.0.0.0:8080'), $console->commands[0]);
    }

    public function test_serve_returns_the_server_exit_code(): void
    {
        $console = $this->recordingConsole(exitCode: 255);

        $this->assertSame(255, $console->run(['kanin', 'serve']));
    }

    #[DataProvider('invalidHosts')]
    public function test_serve_rejects_an_invalid_host(string $option): void
    {
        $console = $this->recordingConsole();

        $this->assertSame(1, $console->run(['kanin', 'serve', $option]));

        $this->assertSame([], $console->commands);
        $this->assertStringContainsString('Invalid host.', $this->printed());
    }

    public static function invalidHosts(): array
    {
        return [
            'shell injection' => ['--host=127.0.0.1;whoami'],
            'space' => ['--host=local host'],
            'empty' => ['--host='],
            'flag without value' => ['--host'],
        ];
    }

    #[DataProvider('invalidPorts')]
    public function test_serve_rejects_an_invalid_port(string $option): void
    {
        $console = $this->recordingConsole();

        $this->assertSame(1, $console->run(['kanin', 'serve', $option]));

        $this->assertSame([], $console->commands);
        $this->assertStringContainsString('Invalid port.', $this->printed());
    }

    public static function invalidPorts(): array
    {
        return [
            'zero' => ['--port=0'],
            'too big' => ['--port=65536'],
            'not a number' => ['--port=http'],
            'negative' => ['--port=-1'],
            'shell injection' => ['--port=8000&calc'],
            'flag without value' => ['--port'],
        ];
    }

    public function test_db_seed_creates_the_sqlite_file_and_runs_the_seed(): void
    {
        $database = $this->basePath . '/storage/db/app.sqlite';
        $this->useSqliteDatabase($database);
        mkdir($this->basePath . '/database');
        file_put_contents(
            $this->basePath . '/database/seed.sql',
            "CREATE TABLE notes (id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY, body TEXT NOT NULL);\nINSERT INTO notes (body) VALUES ('one'), ('two');"
        );

        $this->assertFileDoesNotExist($database);
        $this->assertSame(0, $this->kanin('db:seed'));

        $this->assertFileExists($database);
        $this->assertStringContainsString("Created SQLite database {$database}", $this->printed());
        $this->assertStringContainsString('Database seeded from database/seed.sql', $this->printed());

        $count = $this->app->make(Connection::class)->selectOne('select count(*) as total from notes');
        $this->assertSame(2, (int) $count['total']);
    }

    public function test_db_seed_reuses_an_existing_sqlite_file(): void
    {
        $database = $this->basePath . '/database/app.sqlite';
        $this->useSqliteDatabase($database);
        mkdir($this->basePath . '/database');
        touch($database);
        file_put_contents($this->basePath . '/database/seed.sql', 'CREATE TABLE notes (id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY);');

        $this->assertSame(0, $this->kanin('db:seed'));

        $this->assertStringNotContainsString('Created SQLite database', $this->printed());
        $this->assertStringContainsString('Database seeded', $this->printed());
    }

    public function test_db_seed_fails_when_the_seed_file_is_missing(): void
    {
        $database = $this->basePath . '/database/app.sqlite';
        $this->useSqliteDatabase($database);

        $this->assertSame(1, $this->kanin('db:seed'));

        $this->assertStringContainsString('Seed file not found: database/seed.sql', $this->printed());
        $this->assertFileDoesNotExist($database);
    }

    public function test_command_errors_are_printed_instead_of_thrown(): void
    {
        $this->useSqliteDatabase($this->basePath . '/database/app.sqlite');
        mkdir($this->basePath . '/database');
        file_put_contents($this->basePath . '/database/seed.sql', 'THIS IS NOT SQL;');

        $this->assertSame(1, $this->kanin('db:seed'));
        $this->assertStringContainsString('Error: ', $this->printed());
    }

    /**
     * Run the console with the given arguments (the script name is added for you).
     */
    private function kanin(string ...$arguments): int
    {
        return (new Kanin($this->app, $this->stream))->run(['kanin', ...$arguments]);
    }

    /**
     * Everything the console has printed so far.
     */
    private function printed(): string
    {
        rewind($this->stream);

        return (string) stream_get_contents($this->stream);
    }

    /**
     * A console that records shell commands instead of running them.
     */
    private function recordingConsole(int $exitCode = 0): Kanin
    {
        return new class ($this->app, $this->stream, $exitCode) extends Kanin {
            /** @var list<string> */
            public array $commands = [];

            public function __construct(Application $app, $output, private readonly int $exitCode)
            {
                parent::__construct($app, $output);
            }

            protected function execute(string $command): int
            {
                $this->commands[] = $command;

                return $this->exitCode;
            }
        };
    }

    /**
     * Point the app at a SQLite file inside the temporary project.
     */
    private function useSqliteDatabase(string $path): void
    {
        $settings = ['driver' => 'sqlite', 'database' => $path];

        $this->app->instance(Config::class, new Config([
            'database' => ['default' => 'sqlite', 'connections' => ['sqlite' => $settings]],
        ]));
        $this->app->instance(Connection::class, new Connection($settings));
    }

    /**
     * A class name no other test has used (generated classes are loaded with require).
     */
    private function uniqueName(string $suffix): string
    {
        return 'Generated' . bin2hex(random_bytes(4)) . $suffix;
    }

    private function removeDirectory(string $directory): void
    {
        if (! is_dir($directory)) {
            return;
        }

        foreach (scandir($directory) ?: [] as $item) {
            if ($item === '.' || $item === '..') {
                continue;
            }

            $path = $directory . '/' . $item;
            is_dir($path) ? $this->removeDirectory($path) : unlink($path);
        }

        rmdir($directory);
    }
}

<?php

/**

 *
 * Register all five routes in routes/api.php with:

 */
class {{ class }}
{
    /**
     * GET /things: list the resources.
     */
    public function index(Request $request): array
    {
        return ['data' => []];
    }

    /**
     * POST /things: validate the input and create a resource.
     */
    public function store(Request $request): JsonResponse
    {
        $data = $request->validate([
            // 'name' => 'required|string|max:100',
        ]);

        return response($data, 201);
    }

    /**
     * GET /things/{id}: show one resource.
     */
    public function show(int $id): array
    {
        return ['id' => $id];
    }

    /**
     * PUT or PATCH /things/{id}: validate the input and update a resource.
     */
    public function update(Request $request, int $id): array
    {
        $data = $request->validate([
            // 'name' => 'required|string|max:100',
        ]);

        return ['id' => $id] + $data;
    }

    /**
     * DELETE /things/{id}: delete a resource and answer "204 No Content".
     */
    public function destroy(int $id): JsonResponse
    {
        return response(null, 204);
    }

- [ ] **Step 4: Implement the console**

Create `system/Console/Kanin.php`:

```php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace Kanin\Console;

use Closure;
use Kanin\Application;
use Kanin\Database\Connection;
use Kanin\Routing\Router;
use Kanin\Support\Config;
use RuntimeException;
use Throwable;

/**
 * The "kanin" command line tool.
 *
 * Usage: php kanin <command> [arguments] [--options]
 * Every command returns an exit code: 0 means success, 1 means failure.
 */
class Kanin
{
    /**
     * Generator commands: command => [stub name, target folder].
     */
    private const array GENERATORS = [
        'make:controller' => ['controller', 'app/Controllers'],
        'make:model' => ['model', 'app/Models'],
        'make:middleware' => ['middleware', 'app/Middleware'],
    ];

    /**
     * Where text is written (a stream resource such as STDOUT).
     *
     * @var resource
     */
    private $output;

    /**
     * Create the console.
     *
     * @param Application $app    The application (already booted by the kanin script).
     * @param resource|null $output Stream to write to; defaults to STDOUT.
     */
    public function __construct(private readonly Application $app, $output = null)
    {
        $this->output = $output ?? STDOUT;
    }

    /**
     * Run the command found in $argv and return the exit code.
     *
     * $argv is PHP's own argument list: $argv[0] is the script name
     * ("kanin"), $argv[1] the command, the rest are its arguments.
     *
     * @param list<string> $argv
     */
    public function run(array $argv): int
    {
        $command = $argv[1] ?? 'help';
        [$arguments, $options] = $this->parseArguments(array_slice($argv, 2));

        try {
            return match ($command) {
                'help', '--help', '-h' => $this->help(),
                'serve' => $this->serve($options),
                'routes' => $this->routes(),
                'make:controller', 'make:model', 'make:middleware' => $this->generate($command, $arguments[0] ?? null, $options),
                'db:seed' => $this->seed(),
                default => $this->fail("Unknown command [{$command}]. Run \"php kanin help\" to see every command."),
            };
        } catch (Throwable $e) {
            return $this->fail('Error: ' . $e->getMessage());
        }
    }

    /**
     * Run a shell command, streaming its output, and return its exit code.
     *
     * Kept in its own method so tests can replace it.
     */
    protected function execute(string $command): int
    {
        passthru($command, $exitCode);

        return $exitCode;
    }

    /**
     * Split arguments into plain values and --options.
     *
     * "--port=8080" becomes ['port' => '8080'], "--api" becomes ['api' => true].
     *
     * @param list<string> $raw
     * @return array{0: list<string>, 1: array<string, string|true>}
     */
    private function parseArguments(array $raw): array
    {
        $arguments = [];
        $options = [];

        foreach ($raw as $value) {
            if (! str_starts_with($value, '--')) {
                $arguments[] = $value;
                continue;
            }

            $parts = explode('=', substr($value, 2), 2);
            $options[$parts[0]] = $parts[1] ?? true;
        }

        return [$arguments, $options];
    }

    /**
     * Print the list of commands.
     */
    private function help(): int
    {
        $this->line('KaninPHP ' . Application::VERSION);
        $this->line('');
        $this->line('Usage:');
        $this->line('  php kanin <command> [arguments] [--options]');
        $this->line('');
        $this->line('Commands:');
        $this->line('  help                              Show this list');
        $this->line('  serve [--host=127.0.0.1] [--port=8000]');
        $this->line('                                    Start the development server');
        $this->line('  routes                            List every registered route');
        $this->line('  make:controller Name [--api]      Create app/Controllers/Name.php');
        $this->line('  make:model Name                   Create app/Models/Name.php');
        $this->line('  make:middleware Name              Create app/Middleware/Name.php');
        $this->line('  db:seed                           Run database/seed.sql');

        return 0;
    }

    /**
     * Start PHP's built-in web server with public/ as the document root.
     *
     * @param array<string, string|true> $options
     */
    private function serve(array $options): int
    {
        $host = $options['host'] ?? '127.0.0.1';
        $port = $options['port'] ?? '8000';

        if (! is_string($host) || preg_match('/^[A-Za-z0-9.\-]+$/', $host) !== 1) {
            return $this->fail('Invalid host. Use letters, digits, dots and dashes only, e.g. --host=127.0.0.1');
        }

        if (! is_string($port) || preg_match('/^[0-9]{1,5}$/', $port) !== 1 || (int) $port < 1 || (int) $port > 65535) {
            return $this->fail('Invalid port. Use a number from 1 to 65535, e.g. --port=8000');
        }

        // Every piece is escaped so nothing typed on the command line can run extra shell commands.
        $command = implode(' ', array_map('escapeshellarg', [
            PHP_BINARY,
            '-S',
            $host . ':' . $port,
            '-t',
            $this->app->basePath('public'),
            $this->app->basePath('public/index.php'),
        ]));

        $this->line("KaninPHP development server: http://{$host}:{$port}");
        $this->line('Press Ctrl+C to stop.');

        return $this->execute($command);
    }

    /**
     * Print every route as a table: METHOD | URI | NAME | ACTION.
     */
    private function routes(): int
    {
        $routes = $this->app->make(Router::class)->routes();

        if ($routes === []) {
            $this->line('No routes registered.');

            return 0;
        }

        $rows = [['METHOD', 'URI', 'NAME', 'ACTION']];

        foreach ($routes as $route) {
            $rows[] = [
                implode('|', $route->methods()),
                $route->uri(),
                $route->getName() ?? '',
                $this->describeAction($route->getAction()),
            ];
        }

        $this->table($rows);

        return 0;
    }

    /**
     * Turn a route action into readable text, e.g. "App\Controllers\UserController@index".
     */
    private function describeAction(Closure|array|string $action): string
    {
        if ($action instanceof Closure) {
            return 'Closure';
        }

        if (is_array($action)) {
            $class = is_object($action[0]) ? $action[0]::class : (string) $action[0];

            return $class . '@' . (string) ($action[1] ?? '');
        }

        return $action;
    }

    /**
     * Print rows as aligned columns; the first row is the header.
     *
     * @param list<list<string>> $rows
     */
    private function table(array $rows): void
    {
        $widths = [];

        foreach ($rows as $row) {
            foreach ($row as $i => $cell) {
                $widths[$i] = max($widths[$i] ?? 0, strlen($cell));
            }
        }

        foreach ($rows as $index => $row) {
            $cells = [];

            foreach ($row as $i => $cell) {
                $cells[] = str_pad($cell, $widths[$i]);
            }

            $this->line(rtrim(implode(' | ', $cells)));

            if ($index === 0) {
                $this->line(implode('-+-', array_map(fn (int $width): string => str_repeat('-', $width), $widths)));
            }
        }
    }

    /**
     * Create a controller, model or middleware class from its stub.
     *
     * @param array<string, string|true> $options
     */
    private function generate(string $command, ?string $name, array $options): int
    {
        if ($name === null) {
            return $this->fail("Missing class name. Example: php kanin {$command} Name");
        }

        if (preg_match('/^[A-Z][A-Za-z0-9]*$/', $name) !== 1) {
            return $this->fail("Invalid name [{$name}]. Use letters and digits only, starting with an uppercase letter (e.g. PostController).");
        }

        [$stub, $folder] = self::GENERATORS[$command];

        if ($command === 'make:controller' && isset($options['api'])) {
            $stub = 'controller.api';
        }

        $relativePath = "{$folder}/{$name}.php";
        $path = $this->app->basePath($relativePath);

        if (file_exists($path)) {
            return $this->fail("{$relativePath} already exists. Nothing was changed.");
        }

        $this->ensureDirectory(dirname($path));
        file_put_contents($path, str_replace('{{ class }}', $name, $this->stub($stub)));
        $this->line("Created {$relativePath}");

        return 0;
    }

    /**
     * Read a template from system/Console/stubs.
     */
    private function stub(string $name): string
    {
        $contents = file_get_contents(__DIR__ . "/stubs/{$name}.stub");

        if ($contents === false) {
            throw new RuntimeException("Stub [{$name}] could not be read.");
        }

        return $contents;
    }

    /**
     * Create the database (for SQLite) and run database/seed.sql.
     */
    private function seed(): int
    {
        $seedFile = $this->app->basePath('database/seed.sql');

        if (! is_file($seedFile)) {
            return $this->fail('Seed file not found: database/seed.sql');
        }

        $config = $this->app->make(Config::class);
        $settings = (array) $config->get('database.connections.' . (string) $config->get('database.default'), []);

        if (($settings['driver'] ?? null) === 'sqlite') {
            $this->createSqliteFile((string) ($settings['database'] ?? ''));
        }

        $this->app->make(Connection::class)->unprepared((string) file_get_contents($seedFile));
        $this->line('Database seeded from database/seed.sql');

        return 0;
    }

    /**
     * Create an empty SQLite database file (and its folder) when it is missing.
     */
    private function createSqliteFile(string $path): void
    {
        if ($path === '' || $path === ':memory:' || file_exists($path)) {
            return;
        }

        $this->ensureDirectory(dirname($path));
        touch($path);
        $this->line("Created SQLite database {$path}");
    }

    /**
     * Create a directory (and its parents) if it does not exist yet.
     */
    private function ensureDirectory(string $directory): void
    {
        if (! is_dir($directory) && ! mkdir($directory, 0777, true) && ! is_dir($directory)) {
            throw new RuntimeException("Directory [{$directory}] could not be created.");
        }
    }

    /**
     * Write one line of text.
     */
    private function line(string $text): void
    {
        fwrite($this->output, $text . PHP_EOL);
    }

    /**
     * Write an error message and return the failure exit code (1).
     */
    private function fail(string $message): int
    {
        $this->line($message);

        return 1;
    }
}
```

Security notes for the reviewer: class names are checked against `/^[A-Z][A-Za-z0-9]*$/` before any path is built (no `../`, slashes or dots can reach the filesystem); generators never overwrite; `serve` validates host and port and passes every argument through `escapeshellarg()`.

Consumes:

- `Kanin\Http\Request::create(string $uri, string $method = 'GET', array $parameters = [], array $headers = [], ?string $content = null, array $server = []): static`, `Request::query(?string $key = null, mixed $default = null): mixed`, `Request::validate(array $rules, array $messages = []): array` (Tasks 4/9)

- `Kanin\Database\Connection::__construct(array $config)`, `unprepared(string $sql): bool` (Task 7); `QueryBuilder::orderBy(string $column, string $direction = 'asc'): static`, `paginate(int $perPage = 15, int $page = 1): array` (Task 7)
- `Kanin\Database\Model` — `static setConnection(?Connection $c): void`, `static query(): QueryBuilder`, `static findOrFail(int|string $id): static`, `static create(array $attributes): static`, `fill(array $attributes): static`, `save(): bool`, `delete(): bool`, `protected array $fillable` (Task 8)
- Validation rules `required`, `string`, `email`, `max`, `unique:table,column[,ignoreId]` and their default messages; 422 body `{"message": "The given data was invalid.", "errors": {...}}` (Tasks 9/10)

-- Demo data for KaninPHP. Run it with: php kanin db:seed
-- Warning: this drops and re-creates the users table.

DROP TABLE IF EXISTS users;

CREATE TABLE users (
    id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
    name VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    email VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL UNIQUE,
    created_at TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);

INSERT INTO users (name, email) VALUES ('Ada Lovelace', 'ada@example.com');
INSERT INTO users (name, email) VALUES ('Alan Turing', 'alan@example.com');
INSERT INTO users (name, email) VALUES ('Grace Hopper', 'grace@example.com');

- [ ] **Step 2: Create the feature-test base class**

Create `tests/TestCase.php`:

```php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace Tests;

use Kanin\Application;
use Kanin\Container;
use Kanin\Database\Connection;
use Kanin\Database\Model;
use Kanin\Http\Request;
use Kanin\Http\Response;
use PHPUnit\Framework\TestCase as BaseTestCase;

/**
 * Base class for feature tests: boots the real application against a fresh
 * in-memory SQLite database (seeded from database/seed.sql) for every test,
 * and offers helpers to send JSON requests through Application::handle().
 */
abstract class TestCase extends BaseTestCase
{
    protected Application $app;

    /**
     * Headers added to every request (filled by withToken()).
     *
     * @var array<string, string>
     */
    private array $defaultHeaders = [];

    protected function setUp(): void
    {
        parent::setUp();

        $this->app = new Application(dirname(__DIR__));
        $this->app->boot();

        // A brand-new database per test, so tests never affect each other.
        $connection = new Connection(['driver' => 'sqlite', 'database' => ':memory:']);
        $this->app->instance(Connection::class, $connection);
        $connection->unprepared((string) file_get_contents($this->app->basePath('database/seed.sql')));

        Model::setConnection(null);
    }

    protected function tearDown(): void
    {
        // boot() installed the framework's PHP error handler; remove it so
        // PHPUnit does not flag the test as risky.
        restore_error_handler();

        Container::setInstance(null);
        Model::setConnection(null);

        parent::tearDown();
    }

    /**
     * Send a JSON request through the whole application and return the response.
     *
     * GET requests put $data in the query string; other methods send it as a JSON body.
     */
    protected function json(string $method, string $uri, array $data = [], array $headers = []): Response
    {
        $method = strtoupper($method);
        $headers = array_merge(
            ['Content-Type' => 'application/json', 'Accept' => 'application/json'],
            $this->defaultHeaders,
            $headers,
        );

        if ($method === 'GET') {
            return $this->app->handle(Request::create($uri, $method, $data, $headers));
        }

        $content = json_encode($data, JSON_THROW_ON_ERROR);

        return $this->app->handle(Request::create($uri, $method, [], $headers, $content));
    }

    /**
     * Send a GET request.
     */
    protected function getJson(string $uri, array $headers = []): Response
    {
        return $this->json('GET', $uri, [], $headers);
    }

    /**
     * Send a POST request with a JSON body.
     */
    protected function postJson(string $uri, array $data = [], array $headers = []): Response
    {
        return $this->json('POST', $uri, $data, $headers);
    }

    /**
     * Send a PUT request with a JSON body.
     */
    protected function putJson(string $uri, array $data = [], array $headers = []): Response
    {
        return $this->json('PUT', $uri, $data, $headers);
    }

    /**
     * Send a PATCH request with a JSON body.
     */
    protected function patchJson(string $uri, array $data = [], array $headers = []): Response
    {
        return $this->json('PATCH', $uri, $data, $headers);
    }

    /**
     * Send a DELETE request.
     */
    protected function deleteJson(string $uri, array $data = [], array $headers = []): Response
    {
        return $this->json('DELETE', $uri, $data, $headers);
    }

    /**
     * Send "Authorization: Bearer <token>" with every following request.
     *
     * 'test-token' is the token phpunit.xml puts in API_TOKENS.
     */
    protected function withToken(string $token = 'test-token'): static
    {
        $this->defaultHeaders['Authorization'] = 'Bearer ' . $token;

        return $this;
    }

    /**
     * Decode a response's JSON body into arrays (null for an empty body).
     */
    protected function decode(Response $response): mixed
    {
        $content = $response->getContent();

        return $content === '' ? null : json_decode($content, true, 512, JSON_THROW_ON_ERROR);
    }
}
```

Create `tests/Feature/WelcomeTest.php`:

```php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace Tests\Feature;

use Kanin\Application;
use Tests\TestCase;

final class WelcomeTest extends TestCase
{
    public function test_api_root_returns_the_app_name_and_version(): void
    {
        $response = $this->getJson('/api');

        $this->assertSame(200, $response->getStatusCode());
        $this->assertSame('application/json', $response->getHeader('Content-Type'));
        $this->assertSame(
            ['name' => config('app.name'), 'version' => Application::VERSION],
            $this->decode($response)
        );
        $this->assertSame('1.0.0', $this->decode($response)['version']);
    }

    public function test_api_root_accepts_a_trailing_slash(): void
    {
        $response = $this->getJson('/api/');

        $this->assertSame(200, $response->getStatusCode());
        $this->assertSame(Application::VERSION, $this->decode($response)['version']);
    }

    public function test_head_request_is_answered_by_the_get_route(): void
    {
        $response = $this->json('HEAD', '/api');

        $this->assertSame(200, $response->getStatusCode());
    }
}
```

Create `tests/Feature/CorsTest.php`:

```php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace Tests\Feature;

use Kanin\Http\Request;
use Tests\TestCase;

final class CorsTest extends TestCase
{
    public function test_preflight_request_is_answered_with_204_and_cors_headers(): void
    {
        $request = Request::create('/api/users', 'OPTIONS', [], [
            'Origin' => 'https://frontend.example',
            'Access-Control-Request-Method' => 'POST',
            'Access-Control-Request-Headers' => 'Content-Type, Authorization',
        ]);

        $response = $this->app->handle($request);

        $this->assertSame(204, $response->getStatusCode());
        $this->assertSame('', $response->getContent());
        $this->assertSame('*', $response->getHeader('Access-Control-Allow-Origin'));
        $this->assertSame('GET, POST, PUT, PATCH, DELETE, OPTIONS', $response->getHeader('Access-Control-Allow-Methods'));
        $this->assertSame('Content-Type, Authorization, Accept, X-Requested-With', $response->getHeader('Access-Control-Allow-Headers'));
        $this->assertSame('600', $response->getHeader('Access-Control-Max-Age'));
        $this->assertNull($response->getHeader('Access-Control-Allow-Credentials'));
    }

    public function test_preflight_for_a_protected_route_does_not_need_a_token(): void
    {
        $request = Request::create('/api/users/1', 'OPTIONS', [], [
            'Origin' => 'https://frontend.example',
            'Access-Control-Request-Method' => 'DELETE',
        ]);

        $response = $this->app->handle($request);

        $this->assertSame(204, $response->getStatusCode());
        $this->assertSame('*', $response->getHeader('Access-Control-Allow-Origin'));
    }

    public function test_normal_request_with_origin_gets_the_allow_origin_header(): void
    {
        $response = $this->getJson('/api', ['Origin' => 'https://frontend.example']);

        $this->assertSame(200, $response->getStatusCode());
        $this->assertSame('*', $response->getHeader('Access-Control-Allow-Origin'));
    }

    public function test_error_responses_also_carry_cors_headers(): void
    {
        $response = $this->postJson('/api/users', ['name' => 'Eve'], ['Origin' => 'https://frontend.example']);

        $this->assertSame(401, $response->getStatusCode());
        $this->assertSame('*', $response->getHeader('Access-Control-Allow-Origin'));
    }

    public function test_request_without_origin_gets_no_cors_headers(): void
    {
        $response = $this->getJson('/api');

        $this->assertSame(200, $response->getStatusCode());
        $this->assertNull($response->getHeader('Access-Control-Allow-Origin'));
    }
}

- [ ] **Step 5: Create the API routes**

Create `routes/api.php`:

```php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

/*
 * API routes. Everything in this file is loaded under the /api prefix,
 * so get('/') answers GET /api and apiResource('users', ...) answers /api/users.
 *
 * The $router variable is provided by the application while it boots.
 */

use App\Controllers\UserController;
use Kanin\Application;
use Kanin\Routing\Router;

/** @var Router $router */

$router->get('/', fn () => ['name' => config('app.name'), 'version' => Application::VERSION])->name('home');

// Anyone may read users: GET /api/users and GET /api/users/{id}.
$router->apiResource('users', UserController::class, ['only' => ['index', 'show']]);

// Changing users needs a valid "Authorization: Bearer <token>" header (see API_TOKENS in .env).
$router->group(['middleware' => 'auth.token'], fn (Router $router) => $router->apiResource('users', UserController::class, ['only' => ['store', 'update', 'destroy']]));
```

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace Tests\Feature;

    public function test_index_lists_the_seeded_users_with_pagination_meta(): void

```php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace Tests\Feature;

use Kanin\Http\Request;
use Tests\TestCase;

final class HttpErrorsTest extends TestCase
{
    public function test_unknown_route_returns_404_json(): void
    {
        $response = $this->getJson('/api/does-not-exist');

        $this->assertSame(404, $response->getStatusCode());
        $this->assertSame('application/json', $response->getHeader('Content-Type'));
        $this->assertSame(['message' => 'Not Found'], $this->decode($response));
    }

    public function test_route_outside_the_api_prefix_returns_404(): void
    {
        $response = $this->getJson('/users');

        $this->assertSame(404, $response->getStatusCode());
        $this->assertSame(['message' => 'Not Found'], $this->decode($response));
    }

    public function test_wrong_method_returns_405_with_allow_header(): void
    {
        $response = $this->deleteJson('/api');

        $this->assertSame(405, $response->getStatusCode());
        $this->assertSame(['message' => 'Method Not Allowed'], $this->decode($response));
        $this->assertStringContainsString('GET', (string) $response->getHeader('Allow'));
    }

    public function test_wrong_method_on_a_resource_lists_the_allowed_methods(): void
    {
        $response = $this->putJson('/api/users', ['name' => 'Nobody']);

        $this->assertSame(405, $response->getStatusCode());
        $allow = (string) $response->getHeader('Allow');
        $this->assertStringContainsString('GET', $allow);
        $this->assertStringContainsString('POST', $allow);
        $this->assertStringNotContainsString('PUT', $allow);
    }

    public function test_malformed_json_body_returns_400(): void
    {
        $request = Request::create('/api/users', 'POST', [], [
            'Content-Type' => 'application/json',
            'Accept' => 'application/json',
            'Authorization' => 'Bearer test-token',
        ], '{"name": "Ada", "email":');

        $response = $this->app->handle($request);

        $this->assertSame(400, $response->getStatusCode());
        $this->assertSame(['message' => 'Malformed JSON body.'], $this->decode($response));
        $this->assertSame(3, $this->decode($this->getJson('/api/users'))['meta']['total']);
    }

    public function test_json_body_that_is_not_an_object_returns_400(): void
    {
        $request = Request::create('/api/users', 'POST', [], [
            'Content-Type' => 'application/json',
            'Authorization' => 'Bearer test-token',
        ], '"just a string"');

        $response = $this->app->handle($request);

        $this->assertSame(400, $response->getStatusCode());
        $this->assertSame(['message' => 'Malformed JSON body.'], $this->decode($response));
    }
}

<?php

namespace App\Models;

use Kanin\Database\Model;

/**
 * A row in the "users" table (the table name comes from the class name).
 */
class User extends Model
{

- [ ] **Step 11: Create the UserController**

Create `app/Controllers/UserController.php`:

```php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace App\Controllers;

use App\Models\User;
use Kanin\Http\JsonResponse;
use Kanin\Http\Request;

/**
 * The /api/users resource: list, show, create, update and delete users.
 *
 * Arrays and models returned here are turned into JSON responses automatically.
 */
class UserController
{
    /**
     * GET /api/users?page=1&per_page=15: one page of users plus pagination info.
     */
    public function index(Request $request): array
    {
        return User::query()
            ->orderBy('id')
            ->paginate((int) $request->query('per_page', 15), (int) $request->query('page', 1));
    }

    /**
     * GET /api/users/{id}: one user, or 404 when it does not exist.
     */
    public function show(int $id): User
    {
        return User::findOrFail($id);
    }

    /**
     * POST /api/users: validate the input, create the user, answer 201 Created.
     */
    public function store(Request $request): JsonResponse
    {
        $data = $request->validate($this->rules());

        return response(User::create($data), 201);
    }

    /**
     * PUT or PATCH /api/users/{id}: validate the input and update the user.
     */
    public function update(Request $request, int $id): User
    {
        $user = User::findOrFail($id);
        $data = $request->validate($this->rules($id));

        $user->fill($data)->save();

        return $user;
    }

    /**
     * DELETE /api/users/{id}: delete the user, answer 204 No Content.
     */
    public function destroy(int $id): JsonResponse
    {
        User::findOrFail($id)->delete();

        return response(null, 204);
    }

    /**
     * The validation rules for creating or updating a user.
     *
     * When updating, $ignoreId tells the unique rule to skip the user's own
     * row, so saving an unchanged email address is not "already taken".
     */
    private function rules(?int $ignoreId = null): array
    {
        $unique = 'unique:users,email' . ($ignoreId === null ? '' : ',' . $ignoreId);

        return [
            'name' => 'required|string|max:100',
            'email' => 'required|email|max:255|' . $unique,
        ];
    }
}
```

Security notes for the reviewer: only validated keys reach `User::create()`/`fill()` (and `$fillable` limits them again), so `id` or `created_at` in the request body are ignored (`test_store_ignores_fields_that_are_not_validated`); write routes sit behind `auth.token`; the `{id}` route parameter is constrained to digits by `apiResource()` and arrives as `int` through `Container::call()`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing release-docs test**

Create `tests/Unit/Release/ReleaseDocsTest.php`. It reads the documents from the project root and checks them against the real framework: the README's `use Kanin\...;` lines must name existing classes, every helper it documents must exist, and every `php` fenced block is compiled with `php -l` (snippets that do not start with `<?php` get it prepended).

```php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace Tests\Unit\Release;

use Kanin\Application;
use PHPUnit\Framework\TestCase;

/**
 * Guards the public release documents: README, CHANGELOG and LICENSE must exist,
 * agree with each other and only show code that matches the real framework.
 */
final class ReleaseDocsTest extends TestCase
{
    /** Every section the public README must contain (spec section 9). */
    private const README_SECTIONS = [
        '## Requirements',
        '## Installation',
        '## Quick Start',
        '## Directory Structure',
        '## Configuration',
        '## Routing',
        '## Controllers',
        '## Requests and Validation',
        '## Responses',
        '## Middleware',
        '### CORS',
        '### Token authentication',
        '### Writing your own middleware',
        '## Database',
        '### Connection',
        '### Query builder',
        '### Pagination',
        '## Models',
        '## Error Handling',
        '## Helpers',
        '## CLI Reference',
        '## Testing',
        '## Deployment',
        '### Apache',
        '### Nginx',
        '## Security Notes',
        '## Contributing',
        '## License',
    ];

    private const CLI_COMMANDS = [
        'php kanin help',
        'php kanin serve',
        'php kanin routes',
        'php kanin make:controller',
        'php kanin make:model',
        'php kanin make:middleware',
        'php kanin db:seed',
    ];

    private const VALIDATION_RULES = [
        'required', 'nullable', 'string', 'email', 'numeric', 'integer', 'min:n', 'max:n',
        'between:a,b', 'confirmed', 'in:a,b,c', 'same:other', 'alpha_num', 'url',
        'unique:table,column', 'exists:table,column',
    ];

    private const HELPERS = ['app', 'config', 'env', 'response', 'route', 'url', 'request', 'db', 'abort', 'dd'];

    /**
     * Read a file from the project root, failing clearly when it is missing.
     */
    private function read(string $file): string
    {
        $path = dirname(__DIR__, 3) . '/' . $file;
        $this->assertFileExists($path, "{$file} is missing from the project root.");

        return (string) file_get_contents($path);
    }

    /**
     * Collect the bodies of every ```php fenced block in the README.
     *
     * @return list<string>
     */
    private function readmePhpBlocks(): array
    {
        preg_match_all('/^```php\R(.*?)^```$/ms', $this->read('README.md'), $matches);

        return $matches[1];
    }

    public function test_license_is_mit_with_the_project_copyright(): void
    {
        $license = $this->read('LICENSE');

        $this->assertStringStartsWith('MIT License', $license);
        $this->assertStringContainsString('Copyright (c) 2020-2026 KaninPHP contributors', $license);
        $this->assertStringContainsString('THE SOFTWARE IS PROVIDED "AS IS"', $license);

        $composer = json_decode($this->read('composer.json'), true, 512, JSON_THROW_ON_ERROR);
        $this->assertSame('MIT', $composer['license']);
    }

    public function test_changelog_has_a_dated_entry_for_the_current_version(): void
    {
        $changelog = $this->read('CHANGELOG.md');

        $this->assertSame('1.0.0', Application::VERSION);
        $this->assertStringContainsString('## [' . Application::VERSION . '] - 2026-10-13', $changelog);
        foreach (['### Added', '### Changed', '### Removed', '### Security'] as $heading) {
            $this->assertStringContainsString($heading, $changelog);
        }
    }

    public function test_readme_has_every_documented_section_in_order(): void
    {
        $readme = $this->read('README.md');
        $previous = -1;

        foreach (self::README_SECTIONS as $heading) {
            $found = preg_match('/^' . preg_quote($heading, '/') . '$/m', $readme, $match, PREG_OFFSET_CAPTURE);
            $this->assertSame(1, $found, "README is missing the heading \"{$heading}\".");
            $this->assertGreaterThan($previous, $match[0][1], "README heading \"{$heading}\" is out of order.");
            $previous = $match[0][1];
        }
    }

    public function test_readme_states_the_current_version_and_welcome_payload(): void
    {
        $readme = $this->read('README.md');

        $this->assertStringStartsWith('# KaninPHP', $readme);
        $this->assertStringContainsString('Current version: **' . Application::VERSION . '**', $readme);
        $this->assertStringContainsString('{"name":"KaninPHP","version":"' . Application::VERSION . '"}', $readme);
    }

    public function test_readme_documents_every_cli_command(): void
    {
        $readme = $this->read('README.md');

        foreach (self::CLI_COMMANDS as $command) {
            $this->assertStringContainsString("`{$command}", $readme, "README does not document {$command}.");
        }
        $this->assertStringContainsString('--api', $readme);
        $this->assertStringContainsString('--host=127.0.0.1', $readme);
        $this->assertStringContainsString('--port=8000', $readme);
    }

    public function test_readme_documents_every_validation_rule(): void
    {
        $readme = $this->read('README.md');

        foreach (self::VALIDATION_RULES as $rule) {
            $this->assertStringContainsString("| `{$rule}` |", $readme, "README does not document the {$rule} rule.");
        }
    }

    public function test_readme_documents_every_helper_and_each_helper_exists(): void
    {
        $readme = $this->read('README.md');

        foreach (self::HELPERS as $helper) {
            $this->assertTrue(function_exists($helper), "Helper {$helper}() does not exist.");
            $this->assertStringContainsString("| `{$helper}(", $readme, "README does not document {$helper}().");
        }
    }

    public function test_readme_documents_every_env_example_key(): void
    {
        $readme = $this->read('README.md');
        preg_match_all('/^([A-Z][A-Z0-9_]*)=/m', $this->read('.env.example'), $matches);

        $this->assertNotEmpty($matches[1]);
        foreach ($matches[1] as $key) {
            $this->assertStringContainsString("`{$key}`", $readme, "README does not document {$key}.");
        }
    }

    public function test_readme_only_imports_framework_classes_that_exist(): void
    {
        preg_match_all('/^use (Kanin\\\\[A-Za-z\\\\]+);$/m', $this->read('README.md'), $matches);

        $this->assertNotEmpty($matches[1]);
        foreach (array_unique($matches[1]) as $class) {
            $this->assertTrue(
                class_exists($class) || interface_exists($class) || enum_exists($class),
                "README imports {$class}, which does not exist.",
            );
        }
    }

    public function test_readme_php_examples_have_valid_syntax(): void
    {
        $blocks = $this->readmePhpBlocks();
        $this->assertGreaterThanOrEqual(15, count($blocks), 'README should show plenty of PHP examples.');

        foreach ($blocks as $index => $code) {
            $source = str_starts_with($code, '<?php') ? $code : "<?php\n\n" . $code;
            $file = tempnam(sys_get_temp_dir(), 'kanin-readme-');
            file_put_contents($file, $source);

            $output = [];
            exec(escapeshellarg(PHP_BINARY) . ' -l ' . escapeshellarg($file) . ' 2>&1', $output, $exitCode);
            unlink($file);

            $this->assertSame(0, $exitCode, "README PHP block #{$index} does not compile:\n"
                . implode("\n", $output) . "\n\n" . $code);
        }
    }

    public function test_readme_has_no_leftovers_from_the_old_skeleton(): void
    {
        $readme = $this->read('README.md');

        foreach (['v0.0.1', 'Rice', 'core.php', 'TODO', 'TBD', 'MVC Framework'] as $leftover) {
            $this->assertStringNotContainsString($leftover, $readme);
        }
    }
}
```
